## 1090075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec109-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガル帝国の進攻で守備隊が出払った王都で<line-break>かねて王妃エリスと対立していた上流貴族たちが<line-break>エリエナイ公レムオンを中心に決起。<line-break>王妃エリスを殺害、その甥ゼネテス総司令を<line-break>処刑しようとした。<line-break>クーデターは意外な形で終結した。<line-break>闇の力に魅入られた王女アトレイアが乱入し、<line-break>貴族達を殺害して逃亡したのである。<line-break>現在のところ、王女アトレイアの行方は<line-break>分かっていない。<end>"
EN: "With the garrison absent from the royal capital because of the Dyneskal Empire's advance, high nobles long opposed to Queen Eris rose in revolt under Lemghon, Duke of Elienai. They killed Queen Eris and attempted to execute her nephew, Commander-in-Chief Xenetes. The coup ended unexpectedly when Princess Atleia, enthralled by the power of darkness, stormed in, killed the nobles, and fled. Princess Atleia's whereabouts remain unknown.<end>"
KO: "딩갈 제국의 침공으로 수비대가 빠져나간 왕도에서 오래전부터 왕비 에리스와 대립하던 상급 귀족들이 엘리에나이 공 레무온을 중심으로 봉기했다. 왕비 에리스를 살해하고 조카인 총사령 제네테스까지 처형하려 했다. 쿠데타는 뜻밖의 형태로 끝났다. 어둠의 힘에 홀린 왕녀 아트레이아가 난입해 귀족들을 죽이고 도주한 것이다. 현재 아트레이아 왕녀의 행방은 알 수 없다.<end>"

## 1090076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec109-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラとゼネテスの決戦後<end>"
EN: "After the Decisive Battle Between Karula and Xenetes<end>"
KO: "카를라와 제네테스 결전 후<end>"

## 1090077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec109-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラはロストールで反乱が起こることを<line-break>見越していた。クーデターで混乱する王都に軍を<line-break>進めようとしたカルラだったが、ベルゼーヴァの<line-break>命により、攻撃は中止された。ベルゼーヴァは<line-break>ネメア死すの報を受け、カルラの勢力の強大化を<line-break>危ぶんだのである。カルラもネメア死後、情勢が<line-break>流動化することを想定し、ロセンに撤兵した。<line-break>ロストールでは王女アトレイアが、<line-break>政治の立て直しを図っている。また、王女はエリ<line-break>エナイ邸で民衆と王都の復興活動も行っている。<end>"
EN: "Karula had anticipated an uprising in Rostorl. She intended to advance her army into the royal capital amid the chaos of the coup, but the attack was canceled on Beelzeva's orders. Upon receiving the report that Nemea was dead, Beelzeva feared Karula's power would grow too great. Karula, too, anticipated that the political situation would become volatile after Nemea's death and withdrew to Rocen. In Rostorl, Princess Atleia is working to restore the government. From the Elienai estate, the princess is also working alongside the populace to rebuild the royal capital.<end>"
KO: "카를라는 로스톨에서 반란이 일어날 것을 내다보고 있었다. 쿠데타로 혼란한 왕도에 군을 진격시키려 했지만 벨제바의 명령으로 공격은 중지됐다. 벨제바는 네메아가 죽었다는 소식을 듣고 카를라 세력이 지나치게 커지는 것을 우려한 것이다. 카를라도 네메아 사후 정세가 유동적으로 변할 것을 예상해 로센으로 철수했다. 로스톨에서는 아트레이아 왕녀가 정치를 재건하고 있으며 엘리에나이 저택에서 민중과 함께 왕도 복구에도 나서고 있다.<end>"

## 1100001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、おとなしくついてこい。<line-break>老いぼれに手荒なマネはしたくないが、<line-break>従わないのならば、容赦しないぞ。<end>"
EN: "Come quietly. I would rather not be rough with an old man, but if you refuse to obey, I will show no mercy.<end>"
KO: "자, 얌전히 따라와라. 늙은이에게 거칠게 굴고 싶진 않지만 따르지 않으면 용서하지 않겠다.<end>"

## 1100002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "容赦せんか…。　　　　　<line-break>ふっふっ、恐ろしいの。<line-break>　<line-break>エンシャントでは、<line-break>老人をいたわらんらしい。<end>"
EN: "No mercy, you say? Heh heh. How frightening. It seems Ancient has little regard for the elderly.<end>"
KO: "봐주지 않는군…. 후후, 무섭구나. 엔샨트에서는 노인을 공경하지 않는 모양이야.<end>"

## 1100003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "노인을 돕는다<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml

## 1100005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴様！　本官に逆らうか！<line-break>殺してもかまわん！　排除しろ！<end>"
EN: "You! You dare defy an officer? Lethal force is authorized! Eliminate the offender!<end>"
KO: "네놈! 본관에게 거역하겠다는 거냐! 죽여도 상관없다! 제거해라!<end>"

## 1100006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "礼を言わせてもらうぞ。しかし…<line-break>ふふ、余計なことをしたものじゃ。<end>"
EN: "You have my thanks. Still... heh, you did something quite unnecessary.<end>"
KO: "감사는 하지. 하지만… 후후, 괜한 짓을 했구나.<end>"

## 1100007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気がついたか？<end>"
EN: "Awake, are you?<end>"
KO: "정신이 들었나?<end>"

## 1100008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "礼を言わせてもらうぞ。しかし…<line-break>ふふ、余計なことをしたものじゃ。<end>"
EN: "You have my thanks. Still... heh, you did something quite unnecessary.<end>"
KO: "감사는 하지. 하지만… 후후, 괜한 짓을 했구나.<end>"

## 1100009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふっふっ、まあ、安心せい。<line-break>今はただの老いぼれじゃ。<line-break>わしは何もしとらんよ。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. Don't worry. These days, I'm just an old man. I'm not up to anything.<end>"
KO: "후후, 뭐, 안심하게. 지금은 그저 늙은이일 뿐이야. 난 아무것도 하지 않았네.<end>"

## 1100010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前は、わしがお尋ね者の　　　　　<line-break>妖術宰相ゾフォルと知らなんだか。<line-break>　<line-break>かつて、魔王バロルのもとで<line-break>その手腕をふるい、<line-break>世界を闇に落とそうとした魔道士と。<end>"
EN: "Did you not know that I am Zofor, the wanted Sorcerous Chancellor? I once served under Balor, Demon King, putting my talents to use in an attempt to plunge the world into darkness.<end>"
KO: "너는 내가 수배 중인 요술 재상 조포르라는 걸 몰랐느냐. 한때 마왕 발로르 아래에서 수완을 떨치며 세계를 어둠에 빠뜨리려 했던 마도사라는 것을.<end>"

## 1100011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ついてくるがいい。<end>"
EN: "Come with me.<end>"
KO: "따라오도록 해.<end>"

## 1100012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ようこそ。ふっふっ…。<line-break>わしが妖術宰相と知っても　　　　　<line-break>ここに来るか。<line-break>お前をここに駆り立てたものは<line-break>好奇心か？　探求心か？<line-break>そのいずれもが、世界を変える力…。<end>"
EN: "Welcome. Heh heh... So you came even knowing that I am the Sorcerous Chancellor. Was it curiosity that drove you here? A thirst for knowledge? Either one has the power to change the world...<end>"
KO: "어서 오너라. 후후후…. 내가 요술 재상인 걸 알고도 여기 왔나. 너를 여기로 이끈 건 호기심인가? 탐구심인가? 어느 쪽이든 세계를 바꾸는 힘이지….<end>"

## 1100013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それを持つ者が<line-break>無限のソウルの持ち主なれば<line-break>なおのこと運命を感じるわ…。　　<line-break>わしとお前とは対立するじゃろう。<line-break>それが運命じゃ。じゃが、今は<line-break>殺し合いをするには及ぶまい。<end>"
EN: "And when one possessed of such a quality also bears an Infinite Soul, I feel the hand of fate all the more keenly... You and I will find ourselves opposed. That is our destiny. But for now, there is no need for us to kill one another.<end>"
KO: "그것을 가진 자가 무한의 소울의 소유자라면 더욱 운명을 느끼는군…. 나와 너는 대립하게 될 것이다. 그것이 운명이지. 하지만 지금 당장 서로 죽일 필요는 없겠지.<end>"

## 1100014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "探求心がお前を駆り立てるなら、<line-break>また、ここに来るがよい。<end>"
EN: "If your thirst for knowledge drives you, come back here.<end>"
KO: "탐구심이 너를 재촉한다면 다시 이곳에 오너라.<end>"

## 1100015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じじいの話し相手になる気があれば、<line-break>また、ここを訪れてくるがよい。<end>"
EN: "If you feel like keeping an old man company, come visit me here again.<end>"
KO: "이 늙은이의 말상대가 되어줄 생각이 있다면, 또 이곳을 찾아오거라.<end>"

## 1100016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントの<line-break>魔道の塔を知っておるか？　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>かつて、アルレシアの七王は<line-break>強大な魔力を持ち、<line-break>その力でこの世に神聖王国を建てた。<end>"
EN: "Do you know of the Arcane Tower in Ancient? Long ago, the Seven Kings of Alresia possessed tremendous magical power. With it, they founded the Holy Kingdom in this world.<end>"
KO: "엔샨트의마도의 탑을 알고 있느냐? 옛날 알레시아의 칠왕은강대한 마력을 지니고, 그 힘으로 이 세상에 신성왕국을 세웠다.<end>"

## 1100017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界が彼らによって<line-break>統一されることを竜王は嫌った。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>彼らは七王のうちふたりに、先人類の<line-break>魔道文明の遺産を与え、背かせた。<line-break>彼らが魔道王国の祖となった。<end>"
EN: "The Dragon Kings had no wish to see the world united under them. They bestowed the legacy of the earlier human race's arcane civilization upon two of the Seven Kings and induced them to rebel. Those two became the founders of the Magic Kingdom.<end>"
KO: "용왕은 세계가 그들에 의해 통일되는 것을 싫어했다. 그들은 일곱 왕 중 둘에게 선인류의 마도문명 유산을 주어 배반하게 했다. 그들이 마도왕국의 시조가 되었다.<end>"

## 1100018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、そのうちのひとりは<line-break>先人類の遺産を見て、その偉大なる力と<line-break>不遜なる思いにとらわれた。<line-break>人はもっと、<line-break>すばらしい存在たり得るのでは<line-break>ないのか？<end>"
EN: "But when one of them beheld that legacy, he was captivated by its magnificent power―and by a presumptuous thought. Could humanity not become something greater?<end>"
KO: "하지만 그중 한 사람은 선인류의 유산을 보고 그 위대한 힘과 오만한 생각에 사로잡혔다. 인간은 더 훌륭한 존재가 될 수 있지 않은가?<end>"

## 1100019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人は神の力を手に入れることも　　　　<line-break>可能ではないのか？<line-break>　<line-break>その男、シャロームは愚かにも<line-break>その不遜な考えを実行に移そうとした。<end>"
EN: "Could humanity not even attain the power of the gods? That man, Shalom, was fool enough to try to put his presumptuous idea into practice.<end>"
KO: "인간도 신의 힘을 손에 넣을 수 있지 않은가? 그 남자 샤로움은 어리석게도 그 불손한 생각을 실행에 옮기려 했다.<end>"

## 1100020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは、強大な力の出現を嫌う<line-break>竜王にとって反逆に等しかった。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>竜王は神聖王国の五王を使い、<line-break>不遜の企みの中心地エンシャントの<line-break>魔道の塔を攻めさせた。<end>"
EN: "To the Dragon Kings, who abhorred the emergence of any great power, that was tantamount to rebellion. They sent the Holy Kingdom's five kings to attack the Arcane Tower in Ancient, the heart of that presumptuous scheme.<end>"
KO: "그건 강대한 힘의 출현을 꺼리는 용왕에게 반역이나 다름없었다. 용왕은 신성왕국의 다섯 왕을 움직여 불손한 계획의 중심지 엔샨트의 마도의 탑을 공격하게 했다.<end>"

## 1100021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "激闘が続き、人はさらに数を減らした。<line-break>ついにシャロームが倒れたとき、<line-break>五王もナザールを残すのみだった。　　<line-break>竜王は死ぬことのないシャロームを<line-break>魔道の塔に封じ込めた。そして、<line-break>その塔をナザールに守るように命じた。<end>"
EN: "The fierce struggle dragged on, and humanity's numbers dwindled further. By the time Shalom finally fell, Nazar alone remained of the five kings. The Dragon Kings imprisoned the deathless Shalom within the Arcane Tower, then commanded Nazar to guard it.<end>"
KO: "격전이 이어지며 사람은 더욱 줄어들었다. 마침내 샬롬이 쓰러졌을 때 오왕도 나자르만 남았다. 용왕은 죽지 않는 샬롬을 마도의 탑에 봉인했고, 그 탑을 나자르에게 지키게 했다.<end>"

## 1100022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これが神聖王国成立の次第じゃ。<line-break>わしはこの伝説を追い求め、<line-break>ついに魔道の塔の位置を突き止めた。　<line-break>シャロームは、生きておった。<line-break>その力は無力化されてはおったが、<line-break>その知識はわしの興味に十分に耐えた。<end>"
EN: "And that is how the Holy Kingdom came to be. I pursued the legend and at last discovered the location of the Arcane Tower. Shalom was still alive. His power had been rendered useless, but his knowledge proved more than sufficient to hold my interest.<end>"
KO: "이것이 신성왕국이 세워진 내력이네. 나는 이 전설을 좇아 마침내 마도의 탑 위치를 찾아냈지. 샬롬은 살아 있었다. 힘은 무력화되어 있었지만, 그 지식은 내 흥미를 충분히 만족시켰네.<end>"

## 1100023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴は、わしに男と女を要求した。　　　<line-break>わしは要求に応えた。<line-break>わしは代わりに知識を得た。<line-break>ベルゼーヴァも<line-break>魔道の塔に通っておる様じゃ。<line-break>行ってみるがよかろう。<end>"
EN: "He demanded a man and a woman from me. I obliged, and received knowledge in return. It seems Beelzeva has also been visiting the Arcane Tower. You may find it worthwhile to go there yourself.<end>"
KO: "놈은 내게 남자와 여자를 요구했다. 나는 그 요구에 응했고, 대신 지식을 얻었다. 벨제바도 마도의 탑에 다니는 모양이니 가 보면 좋을 게다.<end>"

## 1100024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ベルゼーヴァとシャロームの関係？　　<line-break>いうなれば、父と子じゃよ。<line-break>　<line-break>ベルゼーヴァの生家は貴族でな。<line-break>バロル様による特権階級弾圧政策で<line-break>当時、貧しく、ひどい有様じゃった。<end>"
EN: "Beelzeva and Shalom's relationship? You might call them father and son. Beelzeva was born into a noble family, you see. But Lord Balor's campaign against the privileged classes had left them destitute and in a wretched state.<end>"
KO: "벨제바와 샬롬의 관계? 말하자면 아버지와 아들이지. 벨제바의 생가는 귀족이었어. 발로르 님의 특권계급 탄압 정책 때문에 당시엔 가난하고 비참한 형편이었지.<end>"

## 1100025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこにわしがつけ込んだのよ。　　　　<line-break>魔法の儀式に立ち会えば<line-break>地位と金をやろうとな。<line-break>ベルゼーヴァの父母ウリアとバシテバは<line-break>シャロームに身体を貸した。<line-break>マノンの封印の弱まる金環食の日にな。<end>"
EN: "And I took advantage of their plight. I offered them wealth and status if they would attend a magical ritual. Beelzeva's parents, Uria and Bashiteba, lent their bodies to Shalom on the day of an annular eclipse, when Manon's seal was weakened.<end>"
KO: "그 틈을 내가 파고든 게지. 마법 의식에 참여하면 지위와 돈을 주겠다고 말이야. 벨제바의 부모 우리아와 바시테바는 샬롬에게 몸을 빌려주었다. 마논의 봉인이 약해지는 금환식 날에 말이지.<end>"

## 1100026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シャロームは彼らの身体を使い<line-break>ベルゼーヴァをもうけた。<line-break>奴には肉体の両親の他に父がいるのよ。<line-break>かわいそうにウリアとバシテバは<line-break>ベルゼーヴァをもうけてじきに死んだ。<line-break>術に身体が耐えられなんだのじゃな。<end>"
EN: "Shalom used their bodies to beget Beelzeva. So besides his parents in the flesh, Beelzeva has another father. Poor Uria and Bashiteba died not long after bringing him into being. Their bodies could not withstand the ritual.<end>"
KO: "샬롬은 그들의 몸을 이용해 벨제바를 만들었다. 녀석에게는 육체의 부모 외에도 아버지가 있는 셈이지. 가엾게도 우리아와 바시테바는 벨제바를 낳고 얼마 지나지 않아 죽었다. 술법을 몸이 견디지 못한 게지.<end>"

## 1100027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウリアとバシテバの愚は笑えぬ。　　　<line-break>それほどに、当時、特権階級への<line-break>弾圧は厳しかったのだから…。<line-break>マノンの封印の弱まる金環食の日には<line-break>シャロームもそのくらいの術は使える。<line-break>うかつに、奴に近づかぬことじゃな。<end>"
EN: "Uria and Bashiteba's folly is no laughing matter. The persecution of the privileged classes was that severe in those days. On the day of an annular eclipse, when Manon's seal weakens, Shalom is capable of magic on that scale. You'd best be careful not to go near him.<end>"
KO: "우리아와 바시테바의 어리석음을 비웃을 수만은 없지. 당시 특권계급에 대한 탄압이 그만큼 심했으니까…. 마논의 봉인이 약해지는 금환식 날이면 샬롬도 그 정도 술법은 쓸 수 있어. 함부로 가까이 가지 말게.<end>"

## 1100028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふぉっふぉっふぉ。<line-break>マノンの封印について聞きたいのか？　<line-break>　<line-break>昔、アリアロという<line-break>無限のソウルの持ち主が、<line-break>その力でシャロームの封印を破った。<end>"
EN: "Ho ho ho. You wish to hear about Manon's seal? Long ago, Ariaro, bearer of an Infinite Soul, used its power to break the seal binding Shalom.<end>"
KO: "허허허. 마논의 봉인에 대해 묻고 싶은가? 옛날 아리아로라는 무한의 소울 소유자가 그 힘으로 샤롬의 봉인을 깨뜨렸다네.<end>"

## 1100029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お主にも覚えがあろう？<line-break>偉大な力を持ちながら、<line-break>そのことを知らず、利用される…。　　<line-break>アリアロもそう。恋しい男の理想のため<line-break>結果的にはその男を闇に落とす扉を<line-break>開いてしもうたのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "That must sound familiar to you, does it not? To possess great power without realizing it, only to be used by others... Ariaro was much the same, pursuing the ideals of a dearly loved man, only to open the door that led him into darkness.<end>"
KO: "너도 짐작 가는 게 있겠지? 위대한 힘을 지녔지만 그걸 모르고 이용당한다…. 아리아로도 그랬다. 사랑하는 남자의 이상을 위해 결국 그 남자를 어둠에 빠뜨리는 문을 열어 버렸지.<end>"

## 1100030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その封印をもとに戻すため、<line-break>マノンは自らのソウルを腕輪に封じ<line-break>シャロームの力を再び封じたという。　<line-break>じゃが、ソウルの干渉によって<line-break>金環食の日には封印の力が弱くなり、<line-break>シャロームは少しだけ術を使えるのよ。<end>"
EN: "To restore the seal, Manon is said to have placed her own Soul within a bracelet, sealing Shalom's power away once more. But interference between Souls weakens the seal on the day of an annular eclipse, allowing Shalom to work a little magic.<end>"
KO: "그 봉인을 되돌리기 위해 마논은 자신의 소울을 팔찌에 봉인해 샬롬의 힘을 다시 봉인했다고 하지. 하지만 소울의 간섭 때문에 금환식 날에는 봉인의 힘이 약해져 샬롬이 조금은 술법을 쓸 수 있어.<end>"

## 1100031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その日にはマノンの腕輪を安置した　　<line-break>部屋の扉すらも、アガスティアと<line-break>ブラックバイパーの持ち主には開く。<line-break>ただ、シャロームも、これまで何人も<line-break>腕輪を破壊するべく人を送っておるが<line-break>すべて失敗しておるということじゃ。<end>"
EN: "On that day, even the door to the chamber where Manon's Bracelet rests will open for those who bear Agastia and the Black Viper. Shalom has sent many people to destroy the bracelet, but I am told they have all failed.<end>"
KO: "그날에는 마논의 팔찌를 안치한 방의 문조차 아가스티아와 블랙바이퍼의 소유자에게 열린다네. 다만 샬롬도 지금까지 팔찌를 파괴하려고 여러 사람을 보냈지만 모두 실패했다고 하더군.<end>"

## 1100032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "妖術宰相の隠れ家にようこそ…。<line-break>何も出せんが<line-break>まあ、ゆっくりしていくといい。<end>"
EN: "Welcome to the Sorcerous Chancellor's hideaway. I've nothing to offer you, but make yourself at home.<end>"
KO: "요술 재상의 은신처에 온 걸 환영하지…. 내놓을 건 없지만, 뭐 느긋하게 쉬다 가게.<end>"

## 1100033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…人は誰しも<line-break>胸の内に怪物を飼っておる。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>人が怪物を憎むのはな、そいつを通して<line-break>自分が胸の内に押し込んでいた<line-break>醜いものの存在を感じるからじゃ。<end>"
EN: "…Everyone harbors a monster within. People hate monsters because, in them, they catch a glimpse of the ugliness they have buried inside themselves.<end>"
KO: "…사람은 누구나 가슴속에 괴물을 키우고 있지. 사람이 괴물을 미워하는 건, 그 괴물을 통해 자기 안에 눌러 두었던 추한 것을 느끼기 때문이야.<end>"

## 1100034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "外の怪物を殺そうとも、<line-break>内の怪物を殺そうとも、<line-break>解放にも解決にもならぬのにな。<end>"
EN: "Yet killing the monster without or the monster within brings neither freedom nor resolution.<end>"
KO: "밖의 괴물을 죽이든 안의 괴물을 죽이든 해방도 해결도 되지 않는데 말이지.<end>"

## 1100035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "年をとると、人は変われぬようになる。<line-break>年をとるほど、変わるために<line-break>捨てねばならぬ物が増えてゆくのに、<line-break>年をとるほどに、そういう物を<line-break>捨てるのに必要な勇気とか<line-break>無分別という物が枯渇してゆくのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "As people grow older, they become unable to change. The older they get, the more they must cast aside in order to change, while the courage and recklessness needed to do so dwindle away.<end>"
KO: "나이를 먹으면 사람은 변하기 어려워진다. 나이를 먹을수록 변하기 위해 버려야 할 것이 늘어나는데, 나이를 먹을수록 그런 걸 버리는 데 필요한 용기나 무모함이 메말라 가는 법이지.<end>"

## 1100036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人はきれいに生きようとする。<line-break>悲しいほどにきれいにな。<line-break>　<line-break>そうすると人を許せなくなる。<line-break>滑稽なほどに。<line-break>無責任なほどにな。<end>"
EN: "People try to lead spotless lives. So spotless it's sad. In doing so, they become unable to forgive others. Ridiculously so. Irresponsibly so.<end>"
KO: "사람은 깨끗하게 살려고 하지. 슬플 정도로 깨끗하게. 그러다 보면 다른 사람을 용서하지 못하게 돼. 우스울 만큼, 무책임할 만큼.<end>"

## 1100037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしには予言の力がある。　　　　　　<line-break>人の不幸が言葉となって<line-break>頭に浮かぶのじゃ。<line-break>じゃが、運命は変えられぬ。<line-break>わしは不幸をくいとめようとしたが<line-break>ムダなことじゃった。<end>"
EN: "I have the gift of prophecy. Other people's misfortunes take shape as words in my mind. But fate cannot be changed. I tried to prevent those misfortunes, but it was futile.<end>"
KO: "나에게는 예언의 힘이 있다. 사람의 불행이 말이 되어 머릿속에 떠오르지. 하지만 운명은 바꿀 수 없어. 불행을 막으려 했지만 소용없는 일이었네.<end>"

## 1100038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最後には、わし自身が<line-break>不幸を呼ぶ者としてうとまれ　　　　　<line-break>村を追い出された。<line-break>まだ１０才じゃった。<line-break>あのときの母親の形相は今でも忘れぬ。<line-break>あのときの気持ちもな。<end>"
EN: "In the end, I was shunned as someone who brought misfortune and driven from the village. I was only ten. I have never forgotten the look on my mother's face that day, or how I felt.<end>"
KO: "마지막에는 나 자신이 불행을 부르는 자라며 미움받고 마을에서 쫓겨났다. 아직 열 살이었지. 그때 어머니의 표정은 지금도 잊지 못한다. 그때의 마음도 말이야.<end>"

## 1100039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自分は奴らの言うような　　　　　　　<line-break>不幸を呼ぶ者ではないんだと<line-break>そう強烈に思うた。<line-break>その思いは老境にあってまだ<line-break>忘れぬのに、わしは今でも<line-break>こうして世界を恐怖させておる。<end>"
EN: "I was fiercely certain that I was not the bringer of misfortune they claimed I was. Even now, in my old age, I have not forgotten that conviction. And yet here I am, still holding the world in terror.<end>"
KO: "나는 녀석들이 말하듯 불행을 부르는 자가 아니라고 강하게 생각했지. 그 마음은 늙은 지금도 잊지 않았는데, 나는 아직도 이렇게 세상을 두렵게 만들고 있구나.<end>"

## 1100040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "運命だったのじゃよ…。<line-break>誰も運命からは逃れられぬ。<end>"
EN: "It was fate... No one can escape fate.<end>"
KO: "운명이었던 게야…. 누구도 운명에서 벗어날 수 없지.<end>"

## 1100041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふぉふぉ、アカデミーの爆破は<line-break>そんなことが原因じゃったのか。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>パーティーにそんな娘がいるとはな。<line-break>いい気味よ。<line-break>アカデミーなどロクな所ではない。<end>"
EN: "Ho ho! So that was what prompted the bombing of the Academy. To think you have a girl like that in your party. Serves the place right. The Academy was never worth a damn.<end>"
KO: "허허, 아카데미 폭파는 그런 게 원인이었나. 파티에 그런 아가씨가 있다니. 고소하군. 아카데미 같은 곳은 변변치 않아.<end>"

## 1100042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしは若いころ、<line-break>忘れられた剣士の伝承について　　　　<line-break>研究しておった。<line-break>邪竜エルアザルを倒し、世界を救うたは<line-break>賢王アルキュオネ、ドワーフ王ドレンガ<line-break>冒険エルフの祖ラミュだけではない…。<end>"
EN: "In my youth, I studied the legends of the Forgotten Swordsman. Wise King Alcyone, Dwarf King Drenga, and Ramuz, forebear of the adventuring Elves, were not the only ones who defeated the Evil Dragon Elazar and saved the world...<end>"
KO: "나는 젊었을 때 잊힌 검사의 전승을 연구했네. 사룡 엘아자르를 쓰러뜨리고 세계를 구한 건 현왕 알큐오네, 드워프 왕 드렌가, 모험 엘프의 시조 라뮤만이 아니야….<end>"

## 1100043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もうひとり、<line-break>忘れられた人間の剣士がいたのじゃ。　<line-break>　<line-break>この者が闇の神器、虚無の剣を抜き、<line-break>その力で邪竜を倒したのじゃが、<line-break>剣の呪いで世界から消えたのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "There was one other: a forgotten human swordsman. This swordsman drew the Sword of the Void, a Dark Artifact, and used its power to defeat the Evil Dragon. But the sword's curse erased its wielder from the world.<end>"
KO: "또 한 명, 잊힌 인간 검사도 있었지. 그자가 어둠의 신기, 허무의 검을 뽑아 그 힘으로 사룡을 쓰러뜨렸지만 검의 저주로 세상에서 사라졌단다.<end>"

## 1100044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これが、わしが世界をめぐって　　　　<line-break>伝承を集め、<line-break>くだした結論じゃった。<line-break>じゃが、その論文に対してアカデミー、<line-break>特にエルフやドワーフの学者どもが<line-break>実につまらぬ理由から非難した。<end>"
EN: "That was the conclusion I reached after traveling the world and gathering its legends. But the Academy, especially its Elf and Dwarf scholars, condemned my paper for the most trivial of reasons.<end>"
KO: "이것이 내가 세계를 돌며 전승을 모아 내린 결론이었다. 하지만 그 논문을 두고 아카데미, 특히 엘프와 드워프 학자들이 정말 하찮은 이유로 비난했지.<end>"

## 1100045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人間偏重の歴史を作るための<line-break>人間至上主義の論文だとぬかす。　　<line-break>　<line-break>奴らは人間とエルフとドワーフが<line-break>バランスのいい比率で邪竜を退治した<line-break>ことにしないと気がすまぬらしい。<end>"
EN: "They called it a human-supremacist paper, written to create a history favoring humankind. Apparently, they will accept no account of the Evil Dragon's defeat unless humans, Elves, and Dwarves all contributed in suitably balanced proportions.<end>"
KO: "인간 편중의 역사를 만들기 위한 인간지상주의 논문이라더군. 놈들은 인간과 엘프와 드워프가 균형 잡힌 비율로 사룡을 퇴치했다는 이야기가 아니면 마음에 안 드는 모양이야.<end>"

## 1100046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしはアカデミーをあとにした。<line-break>真実より政治学が幅をきかす場所に　　<line-break>いたくなかったのでな。<line-break>それから、わしはその伝承にあった　　<line-break>虚無の剣を求めて闇の力にはまり、<line-break>今では妖術宰相などと呼ばれとる。<end>"
EN: "So I left the Academy. I had no wish to remain where politics held more sway than truth. After that, I sought the Sword of the Void described in those legends and became ensnared by the power of darkness. Now they call me the Sorcerous Chancellor.<end>"
KO: "나는 아카데미를 떠났네. 진실보다 정치학이 더 힘을 쓰는 곳에 있고 싶지 않았거든. 그 뒤 전승에 나온 허무의 검을 찾아 어둠의 힘에 빠졌고, 지금은 요술 재상 따위로 불리고 있지.<end>"

## 1100047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひょっとしたら、ここにいたのは　　　<line-break>無害で伝承を求めているだけの<line-break>うだつの上がらぬ学者だったんじゃ。<line-break>じゃがな、…運命じゃよ。<line-break>人は変われぬのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps all you would once have found here was a harmless, unsuccessful scholar seeking nothing more than old legends. But... such is fate. People cannot change.<end>"
KO: "어쩌면 여기 있던 건 무해하게 전승만 찾던 보잘것없는 학자였을지도 몰라. 하지만… 운명이란 거지. 사람은 변할 수 없는 거야.<end>"

## 1100048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなた、誰？<end>"
EN: "Who are you?<end>"
KO: "당신, 누구야?<end>"

## 1100049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…近寄らないで。<end>"
EN: "Don't come any closer.<end>"
KO: "…가까이 오지 마.<end>"

## 1100050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴ様。東方兵力配置の件で<line-break>ベルゼーヴァ様がお話があるそうです。<line-break>至急、政庁にお越しください！<end>"
EN: "Zagiv, ma'am! Beelzeva would like to speak with you about the eastern troop deployments. Please come to the government offices immediately!<end>"
KO: "자기브 님. 동방 병력 배치 건으로 벨제바 님께서 할 말씀이 있으시답니다. 즉시 정청으로 와 주십시오!<end>"

## 1100051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一般人の前で機密事項に関することを<line-break>しゃべるなんて、どういうつもり？<line-break>本来なら厳重処分ものよ。<line-break>以後、気をつけて。<end>"
EN: "What were you thinking, discussing classified matters in front of a civilian? That would normally warrant severe disciplinary action. See that it doesn't happen again.<end>"
KO: "일반인 앞에서 기밀 사항을 말하다니 무슨 생각이야? 원래라면 엄중 처분감이야. 앞으로 조심해.<end>"

## 1100052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "は、はい。<end>"
EN: "Y-yes.<end>"
KO: "네, 네.<end>"

## 1100053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すぐ行くわ。<line-break>ベルゼーヴァ閣下にはそう伝えて。<end>"
EN: "I'll go at once. Tell His Excellency Beelzeva that.<end>"
KO: "곧 갈게. 벨제바 각하께 그렇게 전해 줘.<end>"

## 1100054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ。<end>"
EN: "Yes, ma'am.<end>"
KO: "핫.<end>"

## 1100056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなた、いったい何なの？ <line-break>私がここで何をしてようが<line-break>私の自由でしょう？<line-break>私につきまとわないで。<end>"
EN: "What is your problem? Whatever I do here is my own business, isn't it? Stop following me around.<end>"
KO: "당신, 대체 뭐야? 내가 여기서 뭘 하든 내 자유잖아? 나를 따라다니지 마.<end>"

## 1100057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら。また、あなたなの。<line-break>不思議とよく会うわね。<end>"
EN: "Oh. It's you again. We do seem to run into each other a lot.<end>"
KO: "어머. 또 당신이네. 이상하게 자주 만나네.<end>"

## 1100058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は忙しいの。失礼するわ。<end>"
EN: "I'm busy. Excuse me.<end>"
KO: "난 바빠. 실례할게.<end>"

## 1100059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "少し、待ってて。<end>"
EN: "Wait a moment.<end>"
KO: "잠깐 기다려.<end>"

## 1100060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "고마워, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1100061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴ様！<end>"
EN: "Zagiv, ma'am!<end>"
KO: "자기브 님!<end>"

## 1100062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今、行くわ。<end>"
EN: "I'm coming.<end>"
KO: "지금 갈게.<end>"

## 1100063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい、失礼するわ。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry, but I have to go.<end>"
KO: "미안해, 실례할게.<end>"

## 1100064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんにちは。墓まいりかね？　　　　<line-break>わしはドルドラム。<line-break>ソリアスに仕える神官じゃ。<line-break>ドワーフでソリアスの神官というのも<line-break>珍しかろ？　これも、何かの縁じゃ。<line-break>少し、一緒に散歩でもせんかね。<end>"
EN: "Hello there. Visiting a grave, are you? I'm Doldrum, a priest in the service of Solias. A Dwarf priest of Solias is a rare sight, eh? Perhaps our meeting was meant to be. Care to walk with me for a while?<end>"
KO: "안녕하신가. 성묘하러 왔나? 나는 돌드람, 솔리아스를 섬기는 신관일세. 드워프가 솔리아스의 신관이라니 드물지? 이것도 인연이니 잠시 같이 산책하지 않겠나.<end>"

## 1100065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "墓地を散歩するのも　　　　　<line-break>面白いものでな。<line-break>　<line-break>墓地は人生の終点であり、<line-break>その人間について記された<line-break>大いなる書物でもある。<end>"
EN: "There is something rather fascinating about walking through a cemetery. A cemetery marks the end of a life, but it is also a great book in which that life is recorded.<end>"
KO: "묘지를 산책하는 것도 꽤 재미있는 일이지. 묘지는 인생의 종착점이자 그 인간에 대해 기록된 거대한 책이기도 하다.<end>"

## 1100066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "歴史の物言わぬ証人でもあり、<line-break>遺された者にとっては<line-break>記憶の眠る場所でもある。<end>"
EN: "It stands as a silent witness to history, and for those left behind, it is a place where memories lie sleeping.<end>"
KO: "역사의 말없는 증인이기도 하고, 남겨진 이들에게는 기억이 잠든 장소이기도 하지.<end>"

## 1100067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう、この墓が気になるのか？　　　<line-break>この墓は皇帝の一族、<line-break>ディンガル家の代々の墓じゃ。<line-break>邪眼帝バロルは、ザギヴという娘が<line-break>ディンガルを支配するという<line-break>妖術宰相ゾフォルの予言に惑わされ、<end>"
EN: "Oh? Has this grave caught your eye? This is the ancestral tomb of House Dyneskal, the imperial family. The Evil-Eyed Emperor Balor was led astray by the Sorcerous Chancellor Zofor's prophecy that a girl named Zagiv would rule Dyneskal.<end>"
KO: "호오, 이 무덤이 신경 쓰이나? 이 무덤은 황제 일족, 딘갈 가문의 대대로 이어진 묘다. 사안제 발로르는 자기브라는 소녀가 딘갈을 지배한다는 요술 재상 조포르의 예언에 현혹되어….<end>"

## 1100068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴという娘とその一族を捕らえ、<line-break>破壊神の円卓騎士マゴスに<line-break>襲わせたのじゃ。<line-break>ネメア様がマゴスを倒し、<line-break>ザギヴという娘だけは助けられたが、<line-break>あとの方々は…間に合わなんだ…。<end>"
EN: "Balor had Zagiv and her family captured, then set Magos, a Knight of the Round Table serving the God of Destruction, upon them. Lord Nemea defeated Magos and managed to save Zagiv, but for the others... help came too late.<end>"
KO: "자기브라는 아가씨와 그 일족을 붙잡아 파괴신의 원탁기사 마고스에게 습격하게 했지. 네메아 님이 마고스를 쓰러뜨려 자기브라는 아가씨만은 구했지만 나머지는… 늦고 말았어….<end>"

## 1100069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、ザギヴという娘は、その後、<line-break>魔道アカデミーを主席で卒業。<line-break>そして、官僚になったそうじゃ。<line-break>かなり有能で<line-break>将来有望らしいぞ。<end>"
EN: "Well, Zagiv later graduated at the top of her class from the Academy of Magic. I hear she then became an official. Apparently, she is highly capable and has a promising future ahead of her.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 자기브라는 아가씨는 그 뒤 마도 아카데미를 수석 졸업하고 관료가 되었다더군. 상당히 유능해서 장래가 촉망된다는군.<end>"

## 1100070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、かわいそうな方々じゃよ。<line-break>皇帝の一族に生まれながら…、<line-break>　<line-break>バロル帝の特権階級弾圧政策ゆえに<line-break>つらい生活を強いられ、<line-break>最後は魔物の餌食じゃからなぁ。<end>"
EN: "Still, what a pitiful fate for her family. Born into the imperial line, yet forced to endure hardship under Emperor Balor's policy of persecuting the privileged classes... only to end up as prey for monsters.<end>"
KO: "하지만 참 가엾은 분들이지. 황제 일족으로 태어났으면서도 발로르 황제의 특권계급 탄압 정책 때문에 힘든 삶을 살았고, 마지막엔 마물의 먹이가 되었으니 말이야.<end>"

## 1100071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴという娘もつらかろう。　　<line-break>自分が予言の中にあるのと<line-break>同じ名前であったがゆえに、<line-break>一族が殺され、<line-break>自分だけは生き延びて<line-break>しまったのじゃからな。<end>"
EN: "It must be hard for Zagiv as well. Simply because she bore the same name as the girl in the prophecy, her family was killed, and she alone survived.<end>"
KO: "자기브라는 아이도 괴롭겠지. 자신이 예언 속 인물과 같은 이름이었다는 이유로 일족이 죽고 자기만 살아남았으니 말이다.<end>"

## 1100072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さらばじゃ。お前様とは、また<line-break>どこかで会えそうな気がするわい。<line-break>よければ、名前を聞かせてくれんかな？<end>"
EN: "Farewell, then. I have a feeling you and I will meet again somewhere. Before I go, would you tell me your name?<end>"
KO: "잘 있어라. 너와는 또 어디선가 만날 것 같은 기분이 드는군. 괜찮다면 이름을 알려 주겠나?<end>"

## 1100073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、か。<line-break>また会おうぞ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, is it? Until we meet again, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가. 또 만나자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1100074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと、そこのあなた！！<end>"
EN: "Wait, you there!<end>"
KO: "잠깐, 거기 당신!!<end>"

## 1100075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よかった…追いついたわ。<line-break>ひとつ聞きたいのだけど<line-break>あなた、名前は？<end>"
EN: "I'm glad I caught up with you. There's something I'd like to ask. What's your name?<end>"
KO: "다행이다… 따라잡았어. 하나 묻고 싶은데, 당신 이름은?<end>"

## 1100076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたの名前よ。<line-break>確認しておきたいの。<end>"
EN: "Your name. I want to make sure.<end>"
KO: "네 이름 말이야. 확인해 두고 싶어.<end>"

## 1100077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>確か…。　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>…いえ、なんでもないわ。<line-break>ありがとう。<line-break>それでは失礼するわ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... If I remember correctly... No, it's nothing. Thank you. I'll be going, then.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 분명…. …아니, 아무것도 아니야. 고마워. 그럼 실례할게.<end>"

## 1100078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなた、<value:$2B>の<line-break><value:$28>ね。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "You're <value:$28>, the <value:$2B>, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "당신, <value:$2B>의 <value:$28>군요.<end>"

## 1100079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたのこと、調べさせてもらったわ。<line-break>今売り出し中の冒険者ね。<line-break>　<line-break>そして、古代の闇の怪物も<line-break>何度か退治している…。<end>"
EN: "I took the liberty of looking into you. You're an up-and-coming adventurer. And you've defeated ancient monsters of darkness several times...<end>"
KO: "당신에 대해 조사해 봤어. 요즘 이름을 날리는 모험자더군. 그리고 고대의 어둠의 괴물도 몇 번이나 쓰러뜨렸고….<end>"

## 1100080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ありがとう。<end>"
EN: "...Thank you.<end>"
KO: "…고마워.<end>"

## 1100081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしてって？<end>"
EN: "Why, you ask?<end>"
KO: "왜냐고?<end>"

## 1100082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私にとって闇の怪物は、<line-break>仇…だからよ。<end>"
EN: "The monsters of darkness are my sworn enemies... That's why.<end>"
KO: "…나에게 어둠의 괴물은 원수…니까.<end>"

## 1100083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、<value:$28>、どうしたの？<line-break>私をこの墓の中に入れてくれるの？<end>"
EN: "Heh, <value:$28>, what is it? Are you going to put me in this grave?<end>"
KO: "후후, <value:$28>, 왜 그래? 나를 이 무덤 안에 넣어 줄 거야?<end>"

## 1100084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冗談よ。<line-break>私もたまには冗談くらい言うわ。<end>"
EN: "I'm joking. I do make jokes once in a while.<end>"
KO: "농담이야. 나도 가끔은 농담 정도는 해.<end>"

## 1100085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたなら、私が一番憎んでいる怪物を<line-break>退治してくれるかもしれないわね。<end>"
EN: "You might just be able to slay the monster I hate most.<end>"
KO: "당신이라면 내가 가장 증오하는 괴물을 퇴치해 줄지도 모르겠네.<end>"

## 1100086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その怪物？<end>"
EN: "The monster I mean?<end>"
KO: "그 괴물?<end>"

## 1100087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私、を…。<end>"
EN: "...me...<end>"
KO: "…나를….<end>"

## 1100088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "は…ああ…あ、あぁあ。<end>"
EN: "Hah... ah... a-aaaah.<end>"
KO: "하… 아아… 아, 아아아.<end>"

## 1100089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "い、意識を…保たな…きゃ…。<line-break>う、うう…！<end>"
EN: "I-I have to... stay conscious... Nngh...!<end>"
KO: "으, 의식을… 유지해야… 해…. 으, 으으…!<end>"

## 1100090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、収まった…？<line-break>まだ…まだ私だ。<line-break>私でいられている…。<end>"
EN: "H-has it stopped...? I'm still... still me. I can still be myself...<end>"
KO: "오, 가라앉았나…? 아직… 아직 나는 나다. 나로 있을 수 있어….<end>"

## 1100091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いったい、いつまで…。<end>"
EN: "How much longer...?<end>"
KO: "대체 언제까지….<end>"

## 1100092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたは…<line-break><value:$28>…。<line-break>見ていたの？<end>"
EN: "You... <value:$28>... Were you watching?<end>"
KO: "당신은… <value:$28>…. 보고 있었어?<end>"

## 1100093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく逃げなかったわね。<line-break>恐ろしくないの？<end>"
EN: "I'm surprised you didn't run. Aren't you afraid?<end>"
KO: "잘도 도망치지 않았네. 무섭지 않아?<end>"

## 1100094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "恐ろしくない<end>"
EN: "I'm not afraid.<end>"
KO: "두렵지 않다<end>"

## 1100095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "放っておけなかった<end>"
EN: "I couldn't just leave you.<end>"
KO: "내버려둘 수 없었다<end>"

## 1100096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "変な人ね。<line-break>初めてだわ…。<line-break>そんな人、今までいなかった…。<end>"
EN: "You're strange. That's a first... I've never met anyone like you before...<end>"
KO: "이상한 사람이네. 처음이야…. 그런 사람, 지금까지 없었어….<end>"

## 1100097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待て、そこの冒険者。<end>"
EN: "Hold there, adventurer.<end>"
KO: "기다려, 거기 모험자.<end>"

## 1100098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無限のソウルの可能性を秘めている…。<line-break>だが、いまだインフィニティアは<line-break>手に入れていないようだな…。<line-break>…行っていいぞ。<end>"
EN: "The potential of the Infinite Soul lies within you... But it seems you have yet to obtain Infinitia. You may go.<end>"
KO: "무한의 소울의 가능성을 품고 있다…. 하지만 아직 인피니티아는 손에 넣지 못한 모양이군…. …가도 좋다.<end>"

## 1100099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、待て、名を聞こう。<line-break>私はベルゼーヴァ・ベルライン。<line-break>帝国宰相だ。君は？<end>"
EN: "No, wait. Tell me your name. I am Beelzeva Berline, Chancellor of the Empire. And you are?<end>"
KO: "아니, 잠깐. 이름을 듣지. 나는 벨제바 벨라인. 제국 재상이다. 자네는?<end>"

## 1100100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>？<line-break>さえない名だな。　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>だが、君が人類の革新をなすことも<line-break>ありうる。その名は記憶しておこう。<line-break>さらばだ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>? Not much of a name. Still, you may yet bring about Humanity's Evolution. I shall remember your name. Farewell.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>? 별 볼 일 없는 이름이군. 하지만 네가 인류의 혁신을 이룰 수도 있겠지. 그 이름은 기억해 두마. 잘 있어라.<end>"

## 1100101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エスト・リューガ博士に　　　　　　　<line-break>ついて知っているか？<line-break>　<line-break>新進気鋭の学者だ。主に考古学的な<line-break>見地からだが、現在までの歴史観を<line-break>根底からくつがえす男だ。<end>"
EN: "Do you know of Dr. Est Ryuga? He is a brilliant, up-and-coming scholar. His work is chiefly archaeological, but it overturns our entire understanding of history.<end>"
KO: "에스트 류가 박사에 대해 알고 있나? 떠오르는 신진 학자다. 주로 고고학적 관점에서 연구하지만, 지금까지의 역사관을 뿌리부터 뒤엎을 사내지.<end>"

## 1100102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼は先人類の存在を主張している。<line-break>それは、現在の人類よりもはるかに強く<line-break>優れた魔道技術を操る超人類だ。<line-break>ネメア様が求めておられる闇の神器も<line-break>彼らの所産だという。エストは<line-break>その構造にまで手を着けている。<end>"
EN: "He contends that a human race existed before our own―a race of superhumans, far stronger than modern humanity and masters of vastly superior magical technology. He claims that even the Dark Artifacts Lord Nemea seeks were their creation. Est has gone so far as to investigate how they were constructed.<end>"
KO: "그는 선인류의 존재를 주장하고 있네. 현재의 인류보다 훨씬 강하고 뛰어난 마도 기술을 다루는 초인류지. 네메아 님이 찾는 어둠의 신기도 그들의 산물이라고 하네. 에스트는 그 구조까지 연구하고 있어.<end>"

## 1100103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界中の学者で<line-break>自力でここまでたどり着いた者は　<line-break>これまで皆無だ。<line-break>古くさいロストールなどに生まれて<line-break>いなければ、恐るべき存在だった。<line-break>人類の革新のための貴重な人材だ。<end>"
EN: "No scholar anywhere in the world has ever come this far independently. Had he not been born in a hidebound place like Rostorl, he would have become a formidable force. He is an invaluable asset to Humanity's Evolution.<end>"
KO: "세계의 학자 중 자기 힘만으로 여기까지 온 자는 지금껏 한 명도 없다. 낡아빠진 로스톨 같은 곳에서 태어나지만 않았어도 무서운 존재였겠지. 인류 혁신을 위한 귀중한 인재다.<end>"

## 1100104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただ、強大な力を嫌う竜王に<line-break>抹殺されねばよいがな…。<line-break>ロストールの攻略は早めるべきだろう。<end>"
EN: "I only hope the Dragon King, with that hatred of great power, does not have him eliminated... We should hasten the conquest of Rostorl.<end>"
KO: "다만 강대한 힘을 싫어하는 용왕에게 말살당하지 않으면 좋겠군…. 로스톨 공략은 서두르는 편이 좋겠어.<end>"

## 1100105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぜだ、我が子よ？<line-break>なぜ我が夢に荷担しようとせぬ？<end>"
EN: "Why, my son? Why will you not join me in realizing my dream?<end>"
KO: "왜냐, 내 아이야? 어째서 나의 꿈에 힘을 보태지 않는 것이냐?<end>"

## 1100106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "答は簡単だ。シャロームよ。<line-break>貴様の夢にはなんら実現性がない。　　<line-break>　<line-break>人の革新は貴様の道の果てにはない。<line-break>貴様には、竜王に吊されたその肉塊が<line-break>朽ち果てていく道しか残されていない。<end>"
EN: "The answer is simple, Shalom. Your dream has no hope of becoming reality. Humanity's Evolution does not lie at the end of your path. All that remains for you is to hang there, a lump of flesh strung up by the Dragon King, and rot away.<end>"
KO: "답은 간단하다, 샬롬. 네 꿈에는 아무런 실현 가능성이 없다. 인간의 혁신은 네 길 끝에 있지 않아. 네게 남은 건 용왕에게 매달린 그 살덩이가 썩어 없어지는 길뿐이다.<end>"

## 1100107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "汝に強力な資質を授けた親に対して　　<line-break>その口の聞き方とはな。<line-break>　<line-break>我が恩を思え、ベルゼーヴァ。<line-break>そして我が手先となれ。<line-break>竜王を封じ、我を解放せよ。<end>"
EN: "Is that any way to address the parent who endowed you with such power? Remember what you owe me, Beelzeva, and become my instrument. Seal away the Dragon King and set me free.<end>"
KO: "너에게 강대한 자질을 준 부모에게 그 말버릇이 무엇이냐. 내 은혜를 생각하라, 벨제바. 그리고 내 수족이 되어라. 용왕을 봉인하고 나를 해방하라.<end>"

## 1100108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすれば人類の革新を阻むものはない。<line-break>人類は真の霊長として<line-break>世界に君臨するのだ。<end>"
EN: "Then nothing shall stand in the way of Humanity's Evolution. Humanity shall reign over the world as the true pinnacle of life.<end>"
KO: "그러면 인류의 혁신을 막을 것은 없다. 인류는 진정한 영장으로서 세계에 군림할 것이다.<end>"

## 1100109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "真の霊長？　ものは言いようだな。<line-break>おごれる王シャロームよ。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>それは、貴様が魔法で強化した奴ら…<line-break>神聖王国時代の魔道兵のような<line-break>でき損ないをさして言っているのか？<end>"
EN: "The pinnacle of life? You do know how to dress things up, Shalom, you arrogant king. Do you mean those you enhanced with magic―the failures like the sorcerous soldiers of the Holy Kingdom era?<end>"
KO: "진정한 영장류? 말은 하기 나름이군. 오만한 왕 샬롬이여. 그건 네가 마법으로 강화한 녀석들… 신성왕국 시대의 마도병 같은 실패작을 말하는 건가?<end>"

## 1100111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "笑止。あれこそ、<line-break>魔人のでき損ないではないか。<end>"
EN: "Absurd. He is nothing but a failed demon.<end>"
KO: "가소롭군. 저것이야말로 마인의 실패작 아닌가.<end>"

## 1100112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、もうひとり。　　　<line-break>…<value:$28>。<line-break>　<line-break>無限のソウルを持つ者だ…。<line-break>あるいは、あの者にも、<line-break>革新を望めるかもしれぬ。<end>"
EN: "And there is another... <value:$28>. One who bears the Infinite Soul... Perhaps that one, too, may offer hope for Humanity's Evolution.<end>"
KO: "그리고 또 한 명…. <value:$28>. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자다…. 어쩌면 그자에게도 혁신을 기대할 수 있을지 모른다.<end>"

## 1100113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "息子よ。我が道の他に<line-break>革新の道なし。ともに歩むのだ。<end>"
EN: "My son, there is no path to Humanity's Evolution but mine. Walk it with me.<end>"
KO: "아들이여. 나의 길 외에 혁신의 길은 없다. 함께 걷자.<end>"

## 1100114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "歩く？　フフ、父上、あなたには<line-break>そこで吊られているのがお似合いだ。<end>"
EN: "Walk? Heh. Father, you are far better suited to hanging there.<end>"
KO: "걷는다고? 후후, 아버님, 당신은 거기 매달려 있는 게 잘 어울려요.<end>"

## 1100115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가.<end>"

## 1100116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつか、君に<line-break>先人類の話をしたことがある。<line-break>覚えているか？<end>"
EN: "I told you about the precursor race once. Do you remember?<end>"
KO: "언젠가 너에게 선인류 이야기를 한 적이 있지. 기억하나?<end>"

## 1100117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人間は先人類のように強大で<line-break>もっとすばらしい生き物となる　<line-break>こともできたはずだ。<line-break>今まで、強大な力を嫌う竜王によって<line-break>人類はその超人類への道を進むことを<line-break>禁じられてきた…。<end>"
EN: "Humanity could have grown as powerful as the precursor race and become something far greater than it is. Until now, the Dragon King, who abhors such power, has forbidden humanity from following the path to superhumanity...<end>"
KO: "인간은 선인류처럼 강대하고 더 훌륭한 생물이 될 수도 있었을 거다. 지금까지 강대한 힘을 싫어하는 용왕 때문에 인류는 그 초인류로 가는 길을 걷는 것이 금지되어 왔다….<end>"

## 1100118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし今、ネメア様が初めて<line-break>人類の前に、その道を切り開こうと<line-break>しておられる。<line-break>私はネメア様のその行為を<line-break>阻む要因があれば、神であれ<line-break>容赦なく排除するつもりだ。<end>"
EN: "But now, for the first time, Lord Nemea is opening that path before humanity. If anything stands in his way, I intend to remove it without mercy―even if it is a god.<end>"
KO: "하지만 지금 네메아 님께서 처음으로 인류 앞에 그 길을 열려 하고 계신다. 나는 그 일을 방해하는 요소가 있다면 신이라 해도 가차 없이 제거할 생각이다.<end>"

EN: ""

## 1100126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml

EN: ""
KO: "빈 메시지<end>"

## 1100127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこの若いの。<line-break>どう思うかね？<line-break>嘆かわしい世の中ではないか。<line-break>このまま見捨てるか？<line-break>それとも、助けてくれるかな？<end>"
EN: "You there, youngster. What do you make of this? A sorry state of affairs, wouldn't you say? Will you simply leave me to my fate, or might you lend me a hand?<end>"
KO: "거기 젊은이. 어떻게 생각하나? 한탄스러운 세상이 아닌가. 이대로 외면할 텐가? 아니면 도와주겠나?<end>"

## 1100128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やさしいのね。<line-break>でも、そういうやさしさは、<line-break>身を滅ぼすかもしれないわよ。<end>"
EN: "You're kind. But kindness like that may be your undoing.<end>"
KO: "상냥하네. 하지만 그런 상냥함은 네 몸을 망칠지도 몰라.<end>"

## 1100129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むろん竜王を倒しただけで<line-break>人類の革新が達成できるとは<line-break>私も思っていない。<line-break>だが、神の揺り籠を捨て、<line-break>人類が己の足で歩いてゆく時代は<line-break>確実に近づきつつある。<line-break>そのためにも、<line-break>人類を導く強大な存在が必要なのだ。<line-break>　<line-break>その人類革新の指導者に<line-break>ネメア様こそがふさわしいと<line-break>私は確信している。<line-break>あるいはもしかすると<line-break><value:$28>にも<line-break>その資質があるのかもしれぬがな。<end>"
EN: "Of course, I do not believe that defeating the Dragon King alone will bring about Humanity's Evolution. But the age when humanity casts off the gods' cradle and walks on its own feet is surely drawing near. To reach it, humanity needs a being of immense power to lead the way. I am convinced that Lord Nemea is the one best suited to lead Humanity's Evolution. Or perhaps you, too, have the makings of such a leader, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "물론 용왕을 쓰러뜨리는 것만으로 인류의 혁신을 달성할 수 있다고 나도 생각하진 않는다. 하지만 신의 요람을 버리고 인류가 스스로의 발로 걸어가는 시대는 확실히 다가오고 있다. 그러기 위해서도 인류를 이끌 강대한 존재가 필요하다. 그 인류 혁신의 지도자에 네메아 님이 가장 어울린다고 나는 확신한다. 아니면 어쩌면 <value:$28>에게도 그런 자질이 있을지도 모르겠군.<end>"

## 1100130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec110-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>君にこれだけは<line-break>わかっていてほしい。<line-break>人類の革新は、あまたの神々や<line-break>太古の魔道技術を借りて<line-break>行なうものではないということを。<line-break>あくまで人間自身が、己の力で<line-break>より強くありたいと願う<line-break>その意志によって果たされるのだ。<line-break>おそらく革新には気の遠くなるような<line-break>時間が必要になるだろう。<line-break>　<line-break>しかし、それだけの価値はあると<line-break>私は信じている。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... There is one thing I want you to understand. Humanity's Evolution will not be achieved with the aid of countless gods or ancient magical technology. It must come from humanity itself―from the will to grow stronger through its own power. Humanity's Evolution will probably take an almost inconceivable length of time. But I believe it will be worth it.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 이것만은 알아 줬으면 한다. 인류의 혁신은 수많은 신이나 태고의 마도기술을 빌려 이루는 것이 아니다. 어디까지나 인간 자신이 자신의 힘으로 더 강해지고자 하는 의지에 의해 이루어지는 것이다. 아마 혁신에는 아득할 만큼 긴 시간이 필요하겠지. 하지만 그럴 가치가 있다고 나는 믿는다.<end>"

## 1110008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう！　<value:$28>。<line-break>なんかすごいことになってるぜ。　<line-break>　<line-break>ディンガルの四天王のひとり、<line-break>玄武将軍ザギヴが、<line-break>お前に礼を言いたいそうだ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>! Something big's going on. Zagiv, General of the Black Tortoise and one of Dyneskal's Four Heavenly Kings, says she wants to thank you.<end>"
KO: "오! <value:$28>. 뭔가 굉장한 일이 벌어졌어. 딘갈 사천왕 중 하나인 현무장군 자기브가 네게 감사하고 싶대.<end>"

## 1110009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "城まで来てくれってさ。<line-break>いやー、お前も大物になったなぁ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "She wants you to come to the castle. Well, look at you. You're a real big shot now.<end>"
KO: "성까지 와 달래. 이야, 너도 거물이 됐구나.<end>"

## 1110010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無限のソウルの資質を持つ者よ。<line-break>私の執務室まで来てくれ。<end>"
EN: "You who possess the qualities of an Infinite Soul, come to my office.<end>"
KO: "무한의 소울 자질을 가진 자여. 내 집무실로 와 주게.<end>"

## 1110011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "用事がある。<line-break>国計に関わる大事だ。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ネメア様の障害となる者、<line-break>つまり、君を私の執務室におびき寄せ<line-break>兵に囲ませて始末するのだ。<end>"
EN: "I have business with you. It is an important matter of state. I intend to lure the one who stands in Lord Nemea's way―that is, you―to my office, have soldiers surround you, and dispose of you.<end>"
KO: "볼일이 있다. 국정에 관계된 중대사다. 네메아 님의 방해가 되는 자, 즉 너를 내 집무실로 유인해 병사들로 포위하고 처리하는 것이다.<end>"

## 1110012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冗談だ。君ごときなら、<line-break>今、私ひとりで始末できる。<end>"
EN: "I'm joking. I could dispose of the likes of you myself, right now.<end>"
KO: "농담이다. 너 정도라면 지금 나 혼자서도 처리할 수 있어.<end>"

## 1110013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君がネメア様と敵対状態なのは承知だ。<line-break>だが、ネメア様からは<line-break>どうしろとも命令がない。<line-break>ネメア様には君に関して<line-break>何かお考えがあるのだろう。<line-break>私はネメア様に忠実だ。<end>"
EN: "I am aware that you and Lord Nemea are enemies. But he has given me no orders concerning you. I presume Lord Nemea has something in mind. I am loyal to him.<end>"
KO: "네가 네메아 님과 적대 중인 건 안다. 하지만 네메아 님께서는 아무 명령도 내리지 않으셨다. 네메아 님께는 너에 관해 무언가 생각이 있으시겠지. 나는 네메아 님께 충실하다.<end>"

## 1110014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君はだいたいネメア様に<line-break>たてつくようなマネは<line-break>何もしていないではないか。<end>"
EN: "You haven't actually done anything to defy Lord Nemea in the first place.<end>"
KO: "넌 애초에 네메아 님께 맞서는 짓은 아무것도 하지 않았잖아.<end>"

## 1110015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "心配せずについてくるといい。<line-break>今日は、私の気まぐれだ。<line-break>無限のソウルの資質を持つ者よ。<end>"
EN: "You needn't worry. Come with me. This is merely a whim of mine today, you who possess the qualities of an Infinite Soul.<end>"
KO: "걱정하지 말고 따라오면 돼. 오늘은 내 변덕이다. 무한의 소울의 자질을 지닌 자여.<end>"

## 1110016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は君に期待している。<line-break>君のようなソウルを持つ者は、<line-break>人類の革新者たりうる。<line-break>いくら優れていても<line-break>所詮、四天王は凡人だ。<line-break>人類の革新者たりえない。<end>"
EN: "I expect great things of you. Someone with a Soul like yours could bring about Humanity's Evolution. No matter how exceptional the Four Heavenly Kings may be, they are ordinary people in the end. They could never bring about Humanity's Evolution.<end>"
KO: "나는 너를 기대하고 있다. 너 같은 소울을 지닌 자는 인류의 혁신자가 될 수 있다. 아무리 뛰어나도 결국 사천왕은 범인이다. 인류의 혁신자는 될 수 없어.<end>"

## 1110017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは意味がないのだ。<end>"
EN: "Then there would be no point.<end>"
KO: "그러면 의미가 없다.<end>"

## 1110018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また来るといい。<line-break>無限のソウルを持つものよ。<line-break>私は君を歓迎するだろう。<end>"
EN: "Come again, bearer of the Infinite Soul. You will be welcome here.<end>"
KO: "또 오게. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여. 나는 자네를 환영할 것이다.<end>"

JP: "空きメッセージ<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "빈 메시지<end>"

## 1110100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウルカーンでネメア様の　　　　　　<line-break>邪魔をしたそうだな。<line-break>　<line-break>以後、そのようなマネは慎むことだ。<line-break>…復活の時のあとにも<line-break>生き延びることを望むならな。<end>"
EN: "I hear you interfered with Lord Nemea in Ulkarne. See that it does not happen again... if you hope to survive beyond the time of resurrection.<end>"
KO: "울칸에서 네메아 님을 방해했다지. 앞으로는 그런 짓을 삼가라. …부활의 때 이후에도 살아남길 바란다면.<end>"

## 1110101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人を竜王の束縛から解き放つ…。<line-break>その最後の機会こそ復活の時なのだ。<line-break>この機会を逃すわけにはゆかぬ。<end>"
EN: "The moment of resurrection will be the final chance to free humanity from the Dragon King's grasp. We cannot let that chance pass.<end>"
KO: "인간을 용왕의 속박에서 해방한다…. 그 마지막 기회가 바로 부활의 때다. 이 기회를 놓칠 수는 없다.<end>"

## 1110102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無限のソウルを持つ者よ…　　　　　<line-break>なぜ、理解しない？<line-break>　<line-break>ただ、ネメア様のみが<line-break>復活の時を越え、人類を革新へと<line-break>導くことができるのだ。<end>"
EN: "Bearer of the Infinite Soul... why can you not understand? Lord Nemea alone can transcend the hour of resurrection and lead humanity toward Humanity's Evolution.<end>"
KO: "무한의 소울을 지닌 자여… 어째서 이해하지 못하느냐? 오직 네메아 님만이 부활의 때를 넘어 인류를 혁신으로 이끌 수 있다.<end>"

## 1110103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "空きメッセージ<end>"
EN: "Empty message<end>"
KO: "빈 메시지<end>"

## 1110104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "空きメッセージ<end>"
EN: "Empty message<end>"
KO: "빈 메시지<end>"

## 1110105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "宰相閣下はただいま<line-break>会議で出ていらっしゃいます。<line-break>また後日、お越しください。<end>"
EN: "The Chancellor is in a meeting at present. Please come back another day.<end>"
KO: "재상 각하께서는 지금 회의로 자리를 비우셨습니다. 다음에 다시 와 주십시오.<end>"

## 1110106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ベルゼーヴァ様は、<line-break>仕事に厳しすぎます。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ご自分が有能だから<line-break>つい、その尺度で他人をはかられる。<line-break>私じゃあの仕事量についていけません。<end>"
EN: "Lord Beelzeva is far too demanding when it comes to work. He's so capable himself that he tends to judge everyone else by the same standard. I can't keep up with that workload.<end>"
KO: "벨제바 님은 일에 너무 엄격하십니다. 본인이 유능하시니 자꾸 그 기준으로 남을 재시거든요. 저는 그 업무량을 따라갈 수 없습니다.<end>"

## 1110107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、君か。<line-break>そうだな…。<line-break>君は知っておいた方がいいだろう。<end>"
EN: "Ah, it's you. Let me see... Yes, this is something you ought to know.<end>"
KO: "아, 너구나. 그래…. 넌 알아 두는 게 좋겠어.<end>"

## 1110108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%2聖と闇の魔法には<line-break>禁呪があると言われている。　　　　<line-break>今は封印されているが、<line-break>封印を解くことができれば、<line-break>資質を持つもののみ<line-break>使えるようになるという。<end>火水土風の精霊魔法において<line-break>最大威力を誇るものは、　　　　　　<line-break>精霊神が封じていると聞く。<line-break>だが、精霊神の望みを<line-break>かなえる力を持つ者が現れれば、<line-break>封印が解かれるかもしれぬそうだ。　<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%2Forbidden Spells are said to exist in sacred and dark magic. Though they are sealed away now, if the seal can be broken, only those with the necessary aptitude will be able to use them.<end>I hear that the most powerful of all fire, water, earth, and wind spirit magic is sealed away by the Spirit God. But it is said that the seal may be broken if someone appears with the power to fulfill the Spirit God's wish.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%2성속성과 암속성 마법에는 금주가 있다고 한다. 지금은 봉인되어 있지만 봉인을 풀 수 있다면 자질을 가진 자만 사용할 수 있게 된다고 하지.<end>불·물·땅·바람의 정령마법 중 최대 위력을 자랑하는 것은 정령신이 봉인하고 있다고 들었다. 하지만 정령신의 소망을 이룰 힘을 지닌 자가 나타나면 봉인이 풀릴지도 모른다고 한다.<end>"

## 1110109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "力をつけ、　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>挑戦してみてはどうだ？<line-break>損はするまい。<end>"
EN: "Why not build your strength and attempt it? You have nothing to lose.<end>"
KO: "힘을 길러 도전해 보는 게 어떻겠나? 손해 볼 건 없을 테지.<end>"

## 1110110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、君か。<line-break>この私になんの用だ？<end>"
EN: "Ah, it's you. What business do you have with me?<end>"
KO: "아, 너인가. 이 나에게 무슨 용무지?<end>"

## 1110111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無限のソウルについて、<line-break>知りたいのか？<end>"
EN: "You wish to know about the Infinite Soul?<end>"
KO: "무한의 소울에 대해 알고 싶은 건가?<end>"

## 1110112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "時折、現れるのだ。<line-break>成長に限界のない人間がな。<line-break>それが無限のソウルを持つ者だ。<line-break>人間には、普通は限界がある。<line-break>人により差があるが、<line-break>どこかで成長は止まる。<end>"
EN: "Such people appear from time to time―people with no limit to their growth. They are Bearers of the Infinite Soul. Ordinarily, every person has a limit. It varies from one to another, but eventually, growth comes to an end.<end>"
KO: "가끔 나타난다. 성장에 한계가 없는 인간이 말이지. 그게 무한의 소울을 지닌 자다. 인간에게는 보통 한계가 있다. 사람마다 차이는 있지만 어느 지점에서 성장이 멈추지.<end>"

## 1110113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もっとも限界まで、<line-break>成長を続けられるものは<line-break>まれだがな。<line-break>いかに大器であろうと<line-break>器を満たせねば、<line-break>凡人と変わらん。<end>"
EN: "Mind you, few manage to continue growing until they reach their limit. No matter how great one's potential, if it is never fulfilled, one is no different from an ordinary person.<end>"
KO: "한계까지 계속 성장할 수 있는 자는 드물지. 아무리 큰 그릇이라도 채우지 못하면 범인과 다를 바 없다.<end>"

## 1110114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君はどうかな？<line-break>どこまでやれるのか<line-break>…楽しみにしているよ。<end>"
EN: "And what of you? I look forward to seeing how far you can go.<end>"
KO: "넌 어떨까? 어디까지 해낼 수 있을지… 기대하고 있어.<end>"

## 1110115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、悪いが<line-break>予定が詰まっているのでね。<line-break>気が向いたら、また顔を出してくれ。<end>"
EN: "Now, forgive me, but my schedule is rather full. Come and see me again if you feel so inclined.<end>"
KO: "자, 미안하지만 일정이 꽉 차 있어서 말이야. 마음 내키면 또 들러 줘.<end>"

## 1110116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec111-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気が向いたら、<line-break>また顔を出してくれ。<end>"
EN: "Come by again sometime, if you feel like it.<end>"
KO: "마음 내키면 또 얼굴 비춰 줘.<end>"

## 1120000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1120001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0　　　　招かれざる客が来たみたいだね。　　　<line-break>　　　　　彼女を追ってきたのかな？<end>　　　　招かれざる客が来たみたいだね。<line-break>　　　　　彼女を追ってきたのかな？<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Looks like we have an uninvited guest. Did you come after her?<end>Looks like we have an uninvited guest. Did you come after her?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0초대받지 않은 손님이 온 모양이네. 그녀를 쫓아온 걸까?<end>초대받지 않은 손님이 온 모양이네. 그녀를 쫓아온 걸까?<end>"

## 1120002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml

## 1120003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　アハハッ、そんなに　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　いきりたたないでよ。<line-break>　　　　今日は特別な日なんだからさ。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　だから、招かれざる客だけど<line-break>　　　　　特別に、歴史の目撃者に<line-break>　　　　　　　してあげるよ。<end>"
EN: "Ahaha! Don't get so worked up. Today is a very special day. So even though you're an uninvited guest, I'll make an exception and let you witness history.<end>"
KO: "아하하, 그렇게 흥분하지 마. 오늘은 특별한 날이니까. 초대받지 않은 손님이지만 특별히 역사의 목격자로 만들어 줄게.<end>"

## 1120004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　今、いにしえの魔道文明の大いなる遺産、<line-break>　　　　空中都市が、４人の巫女の力で<line-break>　　よみがえり、大空へと飛び立つのさ！！<end>"
EN: "Now the Floating City, the great legacy of an ancient magical civilization, will be restored by the power of the four priestesses and take to the skies!<end>"
KO: "지금 고대 마도문명의 위대한 유산, 공중도시가 네 무녀의 힘으로 되살아나 하늘로 날아오르는 거야!!<end>"

## 1120005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　この空中都市ラドラスってね、　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　こう見えて実は<line-break>　　　ものすごく大掛かりな兵器なんだよ。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　それこそ、これひとつで<line-break>　　　この大陸が沈んじゃうくらいのね。<end>"
EN: "You see, despite appearances, Floating City Ladras is actually an immense weapon―powerful enough to sink this entire continent all by itself.<end>"
KO: "이 공중도시 라드라스 말이야, 이렇게 보여도 실은 엄청나게 거대한 병기야. 이거 하나만으로 이 대륙이 가라앉을 정도로 말이지.<end>"

## 1120006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　…そうだ！！　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　それ、やっちゃおう！<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　この都市の浮上を願いながら、<line-break>　　　それでも死ななければならなかった<line-break>　　　　多くの悲しい魂たちのために！<end>"
EN: "...I know! Let's do that! For all those sorrowful souls who longed to see this city rise, yet had to die all the same!<end>"
KO: "…그래!! 그거 하자! 이 도시가 떠오르길 바라면서도 죽어야 했던 수많은 슬픈 영혼들을 위해서!<end>"

## 1120007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　今生きるすべての命も、　　　　　<line-break>　　　　彼らの所へ連れて行ってやろう。<line-break>　　　さびしくないようにね。アハハハハ！<end>"
EN: "I'll send every living soul to join them, too. Then they won't be lonely. Ahahahaha!<end>"
KO: "지금 살아 있는 모든 생명도 그들이 있는 곳으로 데려가 주마. 외롭지 않도록 말이지. 아하하하하!<end>"

## 1120008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　あ、もし、君に　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　ご意見ご要望があったら<line-break>　　　　この都市の制御の間まで来てよ。<line-break>　<line-break>　…って、言わなくても君はここまで来るか。<line-break>じゃ、今、そこと、この制御の間を直結するよ。<line-break>　　さ、こっちに来なよ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Oh, and if you have any comments or requests, come to the control chamber. Though I suppose you'd come even if I didn't tell you. All right, I'll link your location directly to this control chamber. Come on over, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아, 혹시 네게 의견이나 요구가 있으면 이 도시의 제어실로 와. …라고 안 해도 넌 여기까지 오겠지. 그럼 지금 그곳과 이 제어실을 바로 연결할게. 자, 이쪽으로 와, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1120009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ、<value:$28>。<line-break>こんな時にまで君と一緒なんて<line-break>もうこれは、運命的な何かを感じるね。<line-break>ところで、これ、すごいんだよ。<line-break>一度巫女たちが起動させれば、<line-break>僕でも動かせるようになってるんだ。<end>"
EN: "Hello, <value:$28>. To think we'd end up together even at a time like this... It almost feels like fate, doesn't it? Anyway, this thing is amazing. Once the priestesses activate it, even I can operate it.<end>"
KO: "안녕, <value:$28>. 이런 때까지 너와 함께라니 정말 운명 같은 무언가를 느끼게 되네. 그런데 이거 대단해. 한 번 무녀들이 기동시키고 나면 나도 움직일 수 있게 되어 있어.<end>"

## 1120010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2シャリ！！<line-break>巫女たちはどこにいるの！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>3巫女は…<line-break>イークレムンはどこにおる！！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>4一度巫女たちが…って、<line-break>巫女たちはどこにいるの？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>5そ、そうだ、巫女だよ！！<line-break>彼女たちはどこにいるんだ！！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>13…言え。<line-break>巫女たちをどこに隠した？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>10あ、そうだ、巫女。<line-break>彼女たちはどこにいるんだよ！！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>14そうじゃ、わしらは<line-break>その娘らを取り戻しに来たんじゃ。<line-break>どこにいるか言うのじゃ！！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>15彼女たち、無事なんだろうね？<line-break>どこにいるんだ、教えろ！！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>17こっちはあんたなんかじゃなくて<line-break>巫女の方に会いたいのよ！<line-break>どこにいるか教えなさいよー！！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19お姉ちゃんたちをどこに隠したの！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>27巫女たちをどこに隠した？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>26巫女たちをどこに隠したのじゃ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>193お、教えてください！<line-break>巫女さんたちは<line-break>ど、どこにいるんですか！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>191シャリ！！<line-break>巫女たちをどこにやったの！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>190巫女たちをどこに隠した！！<line-break>答えろ！！<end>その巫女たちの方に<line-break>会いにきたんだ。<line-break>どこにいるか教えなよ！ <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2Shalli! Where are the priestesses?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3The priestess... Where is Iiklmn?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Once the priestesses... Where have you hidden the priestesses?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Oh, right, the priestesses. Where are they?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Aye, we came to take those girls back.
