Tell us where they are!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Where did you hide the ladies?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27Where have you hidden the priestesses?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26Where have you hidden the priestesses?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>193P-please, tell me! W-where are the priestesses?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191Shalli! What have you done with the priestesses?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190Where have you hidden the priestesses?! Answer me!<end>We came to see the priestesses. Tell us where they are!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2샤리!! 무녀들은 어디 있어!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>3무녀는… 이크렘문은 어디 있느냐!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4아까 무녀들이… 아니, 무녀들은 어디 있어?<end><if><value:$01><equal>5그, 그래, 무녀 말이야!! 그녀들은 어디 있어!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…말해. 무녀들을 어디에 숨겼지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10아, 그래, 무녀. 그녀들은 어디 있는 거야!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14그래, 우리는 그 아이들을 되찾으러 왔다. 어디 있는지 말하는 게다!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15그녀들, 무사한 거지? 어디 있어, 말해!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17우린 너 같은 게 아니라 무녀들을 만나러 온 거야! 어디 있는지 말해!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19언니들을 어디에 숨겼어!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>27무녀들을 어디에 숨겼지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26무녀들을 어디에 숨긴 게냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>193가, 가르쳐 주세요! 무녀분들은 어, 어디 계신가요!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>191샤리!! 무녀들을 어디로 데려간 거야!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>190무녀들을 어디에 숨겼지!! 대답해!!<end>그 무녀들을 만나러 왔어. 어디 있는지 말해!<end>"

## 1120011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今、彼女たちには、<line-break>この都市の底の動力の間で<line-break>動力になってもらってるよ。<end>"
EN: "Right now, they're down in the power chamber at the bottom of the city, serving as its power source.<end>"
KO: "지금 그 아이들은 이 도시 밑의 동력실에서 동력이 되어 주고 있어.<end>"

## 1120012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行かせないよ。　　　　　　　　<line-break>言ったでしょ？　<line-break>君は招かれざる客だって。<line-break>僕はこれを使ってやるんだ。<line-break>それをジャマするっていうのだけは<line-break>ほっとけないな。<end>"
EN: "I can't let you go. I told you, didn't I? You're not welcome here. I'm going to use this, and I can't just let you interfere with that.<end>"
KO: "보내 줄 수 없어. 말했잖아? 넌 초대받지 않은 손님이라고. 난 이걸 사용할 거야. 그걸 방해하려는 것만큼은 내버려 둘 수 없어.<end>"

## 1120013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "全部終わったあとで<line-break>ちゃんと彼女たちも送ってあげるから、<line-break>先に逝って待っててよ。<end>"
EN: "Once everything's over, I'll make sure to send them after you. Why don't you go on ahead and wait for them?<end>"
KO: "전부 끝난 뒤에 그 여자들도 제대로 보내 줄 테니 먼저 가서 기다려.<end>"

## 1120014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ…、意外にやるね。<line-break>まさか、これだけ力を出したのに　<line-break>負けるとは思わなかったな。<line-break>でも、たぶん君にとって<line-break>状況はちっともよくなってないよ。<end>"
EN: "Heh... You're better than I expected. I never thought I'd unleash that much power and still lose. But I doubt your situation has improved in the slightest.<end>"
KO: "후후… 의외로 제법이네. 이만큼 힘을 냈는데 질 줄은 몰랐어. 하지만 아마 네 상황은 전혀 나아지지 않았을 거야.<end>"

## 1120015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほ～ら、こんな所で暴れるから、　　<line-break>この部屋のどこかが<line-break>おかしくなっちゃったんだよ。<line-break>あーあ、もう空中都市も終わりかあ。<line-break>巫女や、砂漠の民のみなさんも<line-break>巻き添えにして墜落だね。<end>"
EN: "See what happens when you run wild in a place like this? Something in this chamber has gone wrong. Ah, well. I suppose this is the end of the Floating City. It'll crash with the priestesses and all those desert folk caught up in it.<end>"
KO: "거봐, 이런 데서 날뛰니까 이 방 어딘가가 이상해졌잖아. 아아, 이제 공중도시도 끝인가. 무녀들과 사막의 사람들까지 휘말려 추락하겠네.<end>"

## 1120016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大爆発にまでつきあう気はないから<line-break>僕はここで失礼するよ。<line-break>じゃあね、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I have no intention of staying for the big explosion, so I'll be taking my leave. Goodbye, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "대폭발까지 함께할 생각은 없으니 난 여기서 실례할게. 그럼, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1120017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2<value:$28>！<line-break>巫女たちの所へ行きましょう。<line-break>動力の間にいるって言ってたわ。<line-break>彼女たちなら<line-break>何とかしてくれるかもしれない。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>3こうしてはおれん！<line-break>ゆくぞ、<value:$28>。<line-break>動力の間じゃ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>4ものすごい事態かも！！<line-break>まさか、墜落して大爆発なんていう　<line-break>ピンチだったなんて！<line-break>とりあえず動力の間まで行って、<line-break>そこに巫女のみなさんがいるなら<line-break>頭下げて助けてもらわなきゃ！！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>5だ、大爆発だぁ！？<line-break>大変だぜ、<value:$28>！！<line-break>　<line-break>…っつっても、俺らじゃ<line-break>どうしょうもないか…、そうだ！！<line-break>巫女たちならなんとかできるかも！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>13聞いていたな？<line-break>動力の間に巫女がいる。<line-break>行くぞ。<line-break>この状態を打破できるのは<line-break>おそらく彼女たちだけだ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>10ま、待て、シャリ！！<line-break>巫女たちは<line-break>どこにいるんだよ！！　<line-break>…あ、そういえば<line-break>動力の間にいるって言ってたっけ。<line-break>よし、姉ちゃん、じゃあそこだ！！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>14<value:$28>、急ぐぞい！！<line-break>まずは、動力の間じゃ！　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>そこで巫女たちを助け出したあと、<line-break>彼女らに爆発を防ぐ手立てを聞く。<line-break>これしかあるまい！！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>15<value:$28>、行こう。<line-break>まずは動力の間に行って　　　<line-break>巫女のみんなを助けなきゃ。<line-break>それから、彼女たちと協力して<line-break>爆発を食い止めよう！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>17ああっ、もぅお！<line-break>どうしてこんなに矢継ぎ早に<line-break>絶体絶命が押し寄せるのよー！？<line-break>とにかく、なんとかするのよ！<line-break>まずは、捕まっている巫女たちに<line-break>話を聞くのよ！　急ぐのよー！！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19大変だよぉ、<value:$28>！<line-break>爆発したら、みんな死んじゃうよ！<line-break>なんとかしなきゃ！！<line-break>そう言えばさっき、巫女の<line-break>お姉ちゃんたちが動力の間にいる<line-break>って言ってたよぉ。行かなきゃ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>21<value:$28><line-break>まずは巫女たちを助けなければ。<line-break>動力の間だ！　急ぐぞ！！<end><if><value:$01><equal>27爆発を食い止めなければ！<line-break>まずは巫女たちを助けるぞ！<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>動力の間だ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>26爆発を食い止めなければ！<line-break>急ぐのじゃ、<value:$28><line-break>動力の間じゃ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>193だ、大爆発に墜落！？<line-break>た、大変です！！<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>さん！<line-break>早く巫女さんたちを助けないと！<end><if><value:$01><equal>191大爆発！？<line-break>冗談じゃないわ！<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>、急ぎましょ！<line-break>巫女たちを助けるしか<line-break>打開策はないわ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>190くっ！<line-break>何とか爆発を食い止めないと！<line-break>でもどうすれば…？<line-break>ラドラスの巫女である彼女たちなら<line-break>何か知っているかもしれない。<line-break>動力の間に急ごう！<end>大変だ！　早くなんとかしないと、<line-break>僕たちみんなドッカ～ンだよ！！<line-break>　<line-break>う～ん、う～ん…、そうだ！<line-break>まずは巫女のみんなを助けに行こう！<line-break>彼女たちなら何か対策知ってるかも！ <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2<value:$28>! Let's go to the priestesses. He said they're in the power chamber. They may be able to do something.<end><if><value:$01><equal>3We have no time to waste! Come, <value:$28>. To the power chamber!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4This could be really bad! I never imagined we'd be facing a crash and a huge explosion! First, let's get to the power chamber. If the priestesses are there, we'll have to beg them to help us!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5A-a huge explosion?! This is bad, <value:$28>! Not that there's much we can do about it... Wait, that's it! Maybe the priestesses can!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13You heard him. The priestesses are in the power chamber. Let's go. They're probably the only ones who can get us out of this.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10W-wait, Shalli! Where are the priestesses?! Oh, right... He said they were in the power chamber. All right, sis, that's where we're going!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Hurry, <value:$28>! To the power chamber first! Once we've rescued the priestesses, we'll ask them how to prevent the explosion. It's our only choice!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Let's go, <value:$28>. First we have to reach the power chamber and rescue the priestesses. Then we'll work with them to stop the explosion!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Oh, come on! Why do we keep landing in one life-or-death crisis after another?! We have to do something! First, let's talk to the captive priestesses. Hurry!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19This is awful, <value:$28>! If it explodes, everybody will die! We have to do something! Oh, that's right―he said the priestess ladies were in the power chamber. We have to go!<end><if><value:$01><equal>21<value:$28>, we must rescue the priestesses first. They're in the power chamber! Hurry!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27We have to stop the explosion! First we'll rescue the priestesses. <value:$28>, to the power chamber!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26We must stop the explosion! Hurry, <value:$28>! To the power chamber!<end><if><value:$01><equal>193A crash and a h-huge explosion?! Th-this is terrible! <value:$28>! We have to rescue the priestesses, quickly!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191A huge explosion?! You have got to be joking! Hurry, <value:$28>! Our only hope is to rescue the priestesses!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190Ngh! We have to stop the explosion somehow! But how...? As the priestesses of Ladras, they may know something. Let's hurry to the power chamber!<end>This is bad! If we don't do something fast, we'll all go boom! Hmm... Hmm... I've got it! First, let's go rescue the priestesses. They might know how to stop this!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2<value:$28>! 무녀들에게 가자. 동력실에 있다고 했어. 그들이라면 어떻게든 해 줄지 몰라.<end><if><value:$01><equal>3이러고 있을 때가 아니다! 간다, <value:$28>. 동력실이다!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4엄청난 사태일지도!! 추락해서 대폭발이라니! 일단 동력실로 가서 무녀들이 있다면 부탁해 도움받아야 해!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5대, 대폭발!? 큰일이야, <value:$28>!! 우리 힘으론 안 되나… 그래! 무녀들이라면 어떻게든 할지도!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13들었지? 동력실에 무녀들이 있다. 간다. 이 상황을 타개할 수 있는 건 아마 그들뿐이다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10자, 잠깐 샤리!! 무녀들은 어디 있어!! …아, 동력실에 있다고 했지. 좋아 누나, 거기다!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14<value:$28>, 서두르자!! 우선 동력실이다! 무녀들을 구하고 폭발을 막을 방법을 묻는다. 이 수밖에 없다!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15<value:$28>, 가자. 먼저 동력실의 무녀들을 구하고 그들과 힘을 합쳐 폭발을 막자!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17아아, 정말! 왜 이렇게 연달아 절체절명이 몰려오는 거야!? 아무튼 해결해! 먼저 붙잡힌 무녀들에게 물어보는 거야! 서둘러!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19큰일이야아, <value:$28>! 폭발하면 모두 죽어! 어떻게든 해야 해! 아까 무녀 언니들이 동력실에 있다고 했어. 가야 해!<end><if><value:$01><equal>21<value:$28>, 우선 무녀들을 구해야 한다. 동력실이다! 서두르자!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27폭발을 막아야 해! 우선 무녀들을 구한다! <value:$28>. 동력실이다!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26폭발을 막아야 한다! 서둘러라, <value:$28>. 동력실이다!<end><if><value:$01><equal>193대, 대폭발에 추락!? 큰일이에요!! <value:$28> 씨! 빨리 무녀들을 구해야 해요!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191대폭발!? 장난 아니야! <value:$28>, 서두르자! 무녀들을 구하는 수밖에 없어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190큭! 어떻게든 폭발을 막아야 해! 하지만 어떻게…? 라드라스 무녀라면 뭔가 알지 몰라. 동력실로 서두르자!<end>큰일이야! 빨리 해결하지 않으면 모두 펑이야!! 음… 그래! 먼저 무녀들을 구하러 가자! 뭔가 방법을 알지도 몰라!<end>"

## 1120018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっと、おとなしくしてくれる気に<line-break>なったみたいだね。<line-break>じゃ、ホントに静かになってもらうか。<end>"
EN: "It looks like you're finally ready to behave. Now let's make sure you really do stay quiet.<end>"
KO: "드디어 얌전히 있을 생각이 든 모양이네. 그럼 정말로 조용하게 만들어 줄까.<end>"

## 1120019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？<line-break>この振動、外から！？<line-break>まさか…。<end>"
EN: "Hm? That tremor came from outside?! It can't be...<end>"
KO: "응? 이 진동, 밖에서!? 설마….<end>"

## 1120020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　いく星霜をへて　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　なお消えぬ無念の思いより生まれし<line-break>　　　　　　　虚無の子よ、<line-break>　　　　　ここはお前の世界ではない…。<end>"
EN: "Child of the Void, born of bitter regrets that have endured undiminished through the ages... This is not your world.<end>"
KO: "오랜 세월이 흘러도 사라지지 않는 원념에서 태어난 허무의 아이여, 이곳은 네 세계가 아니다….<end>"

## 1120021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "く…、<line-break>竜王…！！<end>"
EN: "Ngh... The Dragon King...!<end>"
KO: "크… 용왕…!!<end>"

## 1120022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　虚無の子よ…　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　この世界は汝を望まぬ…。<line-break>　　　　　己があるべき場所へと<line-break>　　　　　　　　帰るがよい…。<end>"
EN: "Child of the Void... This world has no wish for you. Return to the place where you belong...<end>"
KO: "허무의 아이여… 이 세계는 그대를 원하지 않는다…. 그대가 있어야 할 곳으로 돌아가거라….<end>"

## 1120023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やんなっちゃうな、もう。<line-break>子供の遊びに出てきてほしくないね。　<line-break>　<line-break>それに、僕はこの世界の人たちの<line-break>満たされない想いから生まれたんだよ？<line-break>だから、ここにいたっていいじゃない。<end>"
EN: "Oh, this is getting tiresome. You really don't need to involve yourself in a child's game. Besides, I was born from the unfulfilled desires of the people of this world, you know? So there's nothing wrong with me being here.<end>"
KO: "정말 싫다니까. 어린애 놀이에 끼어들고 싶지 않네. 게다가 난 이 세계 사람들의 채워지지 않는 마음에서 태어났다고? 그러니까 여기 있어도 되잖아.<end>"

## 1120024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにしても、<line-break>あいかわらずひどいことするなあ、　<line-break>竜王様は。<line-break>この空中都市の脅威を消すために、<line-break>ここの住人も巫女も、無限のソウルを<line-break>持つ者まで巻き添えにする？<end>"
EN: "Still, as cruel as ever, aren't you, Lord Dragon King? To eliminate the threat posed by this Floating City, you'll sacrifice its residents, the priestesses, and even the Bearer of the Infinite Soul along with it?<end>"
KO: "그나저나 여전히 너무하네, 용왕님도. 이 공중도시의 위협을 없애려고 여기 주민도 무녀도, 무한의 소울을 지닌 자까지 휘말리게 한다고?<end>"

## 1120025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　笑止な。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　他人の心配をしてみせるか？<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　　　汝は虚無の子、<line-break>　　　汝は痛みを知ることはできても<line-break>　　　　　　感じることはできぬ。<line-break>　　　ただのうつろな人形に過ぎぬのだ。<end>"
EN: "How absurd. You pretend to care for others? You are the Child of the Void. You may know what pain is, but you cannot feel it. You are nothing more than an empty puppet.<end>"
KO: "우습구나. 남 걱정을 해 보이느냐? 그대는 허무의 아이. 고통을 알 수는 있어도 느낄 수는 없다. 그저 텅 빈 인형에 지나지 않느니라.<end>"

## 1120026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うわ、言ってくれるね。<line-break>確かにその通りだけどさ。<end>"
EN: "Wow. You really don't hold back, do you? Though I can't say you're wrong.<end>"
KO: "우와, 말은 잘하네. 확실히 맞는 말이긴 하지만.<end>"

## 1120027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さてと、そろそろ潮時かな。<line-break>このオモチャが墜落する前に<line-break>僕は失礼するよ。<end>"
EN: "Well, I suppose it's time for me to go. I'll take my leave before this toy crashes.<end>"
KO: "자, 슬슬 떠날 때인가. 이 장난감이 추락하기 전에 난 실례하지.<end>"

## 1120028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、<value:$28>。<line-break>せっかくだから、墜落の瞬間まで<line-break>ゆっくりしてってよ。<line-break>あはははは！<line-break>じゃあね～。<end>"
EN: "Well then, <value:$28>. Since you're here, why not relax and enjoy the moment of impact? Ahahahaha! Bye-bye!<end>"
KO: "그럼 <value:$28>. 모처럼이니 추락하는 순간까지 느긋하게 있다 가. 아하하하! 잘 가~.<end>"

## 1120029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　…<value:$28>、<line-break>　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ…　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　空中都市はこのままゆけば、<line-break>　　　　あとわずかの時で地に落ちよう。<end>"
EN: "...<value:$28>, Bearer of the Infinite Soul... If nothing is done, the Floating City will soon fall to the earth.<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>, 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여… 공중도시는 이대로라면 이제 얼마 지나지 않아 땅으로 추락할 것이다.<end>"

## 1120030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　動力の間へ行き　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　巫女たちを助け出すのだ。<line-break>　　巫女ならば最悪の事態を回避する術を<line-break>　　　　　　　持っていよう…。<line-break>　　　力及ばず虚無の子に敗れたとはいえ、<line-break>　　この空中都市の脅威を退けようとした<line-break>　　　　　　　その魂、見事なり。<line-break>　　　我が再び戦う力を授けてやろう。<end>"
EN: "Go to the power chamber and rescue the priestesses. They may know how to avert the worst... Though your strength was not enough and you fell to the Child of the Void, your soul, which sought to repel the threat posed by this Floating City, is admirable indeed. I shall grant you the power to fight once more.<end>"
KO: "동력의 방으로 가서 무녀들을 구해라. 무녀라면 최악의 사태를 피할 방법을 알고 있을 것이다…. 힘이 모자라 허무의 아이에게 패했다고는 하나, 이 공중도시의 위협을 물리치려 한 그 혼은 훌륭하다. 내가 다시 싸울 힘을 내려 주마.<end>"

## 1120031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　では、さらばだ。　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ。<end>"
EN: "And now, farewell, Bearer of the Infinite Soul.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 잘 가라. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여.<end>"

## 1120033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　ボク…だよ。エステルだよ…。　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　　ゴメンね…。<line-break>　　　　ボクなんかのために、こんなことに<line-break>　　　　　巻き込んじゃって…。怒ってる？<line-break>　　　　　　ボク…、自分でも驚いてるよ。<line-break>　　　　　ボクにこんなモノを動かす力が<line-break>　　　　　　あるなんてさ…。ほとんど<line-break>　　　　　自由にならないんだけど…ね…。<end>"
EN: "It's… me. Esther… I'm sorry. I dragged you into all this… for someone like me. Are you angry? I'm… surprised myself. I never knew I had the power to move something like this… Not that I can control much of it…<end>"
KO: "나…야. 에스텔이야…. 미안해…. 나 같은 것 때문에 이런 일에 휘말리게 해서…. 화났어? 나도… 내가 이런 걸 움직일 힘이 있다는 게 놀라워…. 거의 마음대로 움직이지도 않지만… 말이야….<end>"

## 1120034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　だから、ラドラスが落ちるのを　　　　<line-break>　　　　　ボクにはとめられない…。<line-break>　　　砂漠の一族のみんなを助けられない。<line-break>　　　　　　　くやしいけど…。<line-break>　　　　　　　だけど、ボク、<line-break>　　　　たぶん、キミぐらいなら<line-break>　　　なんとか外へ飛ばせそうなんだ。<line-break>　　　　　…助けられそうなんだ。<end>"
EN: "That's why I can't stop Ladras from falling… I can't save everyone in the desert clan. I hate that… But I think I can at least manage to send you outside. I think… I can save you.<end>"
KO: "그래서 라드라스가 추락하는 걸 나는 막을 수 없어…. 사막 일족 모두를 구할 수도 없어. 분하지만…. 그래도 아마 너 정도라면 어떻게든 밖으로 날려 보낼 수 있을 것 같아. …너는 구할 수 있을 것 같아.<end>"

## 1120035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　…よかった…。　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　最後の最後でキミの役に立てる。<line-break>　　　　　　　キミを救える…。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　　　　サヨナラだね、　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　　<value:$28>…。<line-break>　　　　　ボクのこと…忘れないでね……。<end>"
EN: "…I'm glad… At the very end, I can finally do something for you. I can save you… This is goodbye, <value:$28>… Please… don't forget me…<end>"
KO: "…다행이야…. 마지막 순간에 네게 도움이 될 수 있어. 널 구할 수 있어…. 이제 작별이네, <value:$28>…. 나를… 잊지 말아 줘…….<end>"

## 1120036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　…<value:$28>、<line-break>　　　　　　<value:$28>よ…。<line-break>　　　　　　　　聞こえるか…。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　わらわじゃ、エアじゃ。<line-break>　　　　　　　すべては定め…<line-break>　　　こうなることはわかっておった…<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "…<value:$28>, <value:$28>… Can you hear me? It is I, Er. All is ordained… I knew it would come to this…<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>, <value:$28>여…. 들리는가…. 나다, 에아다. 모든 것은 정해진 운명… 이렇게 될 줄 알고 있었다….<end>"

## 1120037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　ある意味、これはわらわにとって　　　<line-break>　　　　　最高に幸せな結末かもしれぬ。<line-break>　　　　　わらわは子をなざずに死に<line-break>　そなたはわらわを救おうとしてくれておる。<line-break>　　今のわらわは、この都市の力にとらわれ<line-break>　　　魔力のほとんどを吸われてしまった…。<line-break>　　　もはや、わらわにはラドラスの墜落を<line-break>　　　　　　　防ぐことはできぬ…。<end>"
EN: "In a way, this may be the happiest possible ending for me. I shall die without bearing a child, while you are trying to save me. I am now bound by the power of this city, and it has drained away nearly all my magic… I can no longer prevent Ladras from falling…<end>"
KO: "어쩌면 이것은 나에게 가장 행복한 결말일지도 모른다. 나는 자식을 남기지 못한 채 죽고, 너는 나를 구하려 하고 있구나. 지금의 나는 이 도시의 힘에 붙잡혀 마력 대부분을 빼앗겨 버렸다…. 이제 나에게는 라드라스의 추락을 막을 수단이 없다….<end>"

## 1120038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　しかし、<value:$28>よ。<line-break>　　　そなたを再び母なる大地へと飛ばし、　　<line-break>　　　生きながらえさせるくらいならば<line-break>　　　　　今のわらわにもできよう…。<line-break>　　　　　　　さらばじゃ。<line-break>　　　　　　…<value:$28>よ。<line-break>　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ…。<line-break>　　　そなたはわらわの…希望…であった。<end>"
EN: "But, <value:$28>… I still have enough power to send you back to Mother Earth and preserve your life… Farewell, <value:$28>… Bearer of the Infinite Soul… You were… my hope.<end>"
KO: "하지만 <value:$28>여. 너를 다시 어머니인 대지로 날려 보내 목숨을 이어 가게 하는 정도라면 지금의 나도 할 수 있을 것이다…. 잘 가거라. …<value:$28>여. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여…. 너는 나의… 희망…이었다.<end>"

## 1120039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　…<value:$28>様、<line-break>　　　　　　　<value:$28>様…。<line-break>　　　　　　　　フレアです…。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　　　どうしてでしょう。<line-break>　　　　　　　今、私は道具として<line-break>　　　　この造られた命をまっとうできて<line-break>　　　　　何も他に思うこともないはず…。<end>"
EN: "…<value:$28>, <value:$28>… This is Flare… Why, I wonder? Now, as a tool, I am seeing this created life through to its end. I should have nothing else on my mind…<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28> 님, <value:$28> 님…. 플레어입니다…. 어째서일까요. 지금 저는 도구로서 이 만들어진 생명을 다할 수 있어 다른 생각은 아무것도 없어야 할 텐데….<end>"

## 1120040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　それなのに、あなたの名前が浮かび、　　<line-break>　　　　この都市の呪縛にあらがって<line-break>　　私はあなたの名前を叫び続けています。<line-break>　<line-break>　　今、私に残された最後の魔力を使って　　　<line-break>　　　あなたを外の大地へと飛ばします。<line-break>　　　　…これは、道具としてではなく<line-break>　　　　　私という命が持つ願いです…。<end>"
EN: "And yet your name comes to mind, and I keep calling it as I struggle against this city's hold. I will use the last of my magic to send you to the land outside. This is not a tool's wish, but my own wish as a living being…<end>"
KO: "그런데도 당신의 이름이 떠올라 이 도시의 속박에 맞서면서 저는 계속 당신의 이름을 외치고 있습니다. 이제 제게 남은 마지막 마력을 써서 당신을 바깥 대지로 보내겠습니다. …이것은 도구로서가 아니라, 저라는 생명이 품은 소망입니다….<end>"

## 1120041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　私は今まで、このかりそめの生を　　　<line-break>　　　これほど惜しんだことはありません。<line-break>　　あなたにさえ会わなければ、このような<line-break>　　　　切なさは知らずに済んだのに…。<line-break>　　　　　　　さようなら…<line-break>　　　　　私に迷いを与えた人…<line-break>　　　　私の死を苦いものに変えた人…<line-break>　　　　　　　私の大事な人…。<end>"
EN: "Never before have I been so reluctant to relinquish this fleeting life. If only I had never met you, I would never have known such heartache… Farewell… You who made me doubt… You who made my death bitter… You who are so dear to me…<end>"
KO: "저는 지금까지 이 덧없는 생을 이렇게까지 아깝다고 느낀 적이 없습니다. 당신만 만나지 않았더라면 이런 애절함은 몰라도 되었을 텐데…. 안녕히… 제게 망설임을 준 사람… 제 죽음을 쓰라리게 바꾼 사람… 제 소중한 사람….<end>"

## 1120042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　…<value:$28>さん、<line-break>　　　　　　<value:$28>さん…。<line-break>　　　　　　聞こえますか…私の声…。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　　<value:$28>さん…<line-break>　　　　　　イークレムンです。<line-break>　　　　　　私、うれしいです…。<line-break>　　　あなたがここまで来てくれたことが…。<end>"
EN: "…<value:$28>, <value:$28>… Can you hear my voice? <value:$28>… This is Iiklmn. I'm so happy… that you came all this way…<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>님, <value:$28>님…. 들리나요… 제 목소리가…. <value:$28>님… 이크렘입니다. 저, 기뻐요…. 당신이 여기까지 와 주신 것이….<end>"

## 1120043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　この空中都市は墜落します。　　　　<line-break>　　　　　ごめんなさい…。今の私は<line-break>　　　　この都市に力を奪われていて、<line-break>　　　　それを防ぐことができません…。<line-break>　　　今の私に残された力でできることは、<line-break>　　　　　あなたをここから瞬間移動で<line-break>　　　　　外へと逃がすことくらいです。<end>"
EN: "This Floating City is going to fall. I'm sorry… The city is draining my power, so I can't stop it… All I can do with the power I have left is teleport you away from here, to safety outside.<end>"
KO: "이 공중도시는 추락합니다. 미안해요…. 지금의 저는 이 도시에게 힘을 빼앗기고 있어서 그것을 막을 수 없습니다…. 지금 제게 남은 힘으로 할 수 있는 건 당신을 순간이동시켜 이곳 밖으로 피신시키는 것뿐입니다.<end>"

## 1120044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イークレムン！！<end>"
EN: "Iiklmn!!<end>"
KO: "이클레문!!<end>"

## 1120045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　アンギルダン様…。　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　すいません、私は先に<line-break>　　　　お母様のもとへとまいります。<line-break>　　　　どうか、いつまでもお元気で。<line-break>　　　　　　　　さようなら、<line-break>　　　　　　<value:$28>さん、<line-break>　　　　　　　　さようなら、<line-break>　　　　　　アンギ…、お父様…。<end>"
EN: "Lord Angeerdan… I'm sorry. I will go on ahead to Mother. Please stay well for a long time to come. Goodbye, <value:$28>… Goodbye, Angeer… Father…<end>"
KO: "앙길단 님…. 죄송합니다. 저는 먼저 어머님 곁으로 가겠습니다. 부디 오래도록 건강하시길. 안녕히 계세요, <value:$28> 씨. 안녕히 계세요, 앙기…, 아버지….<end>"

## 1120046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　さようなら…。　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　私は、ミズチたちやお母様、<line-break>　　そして、アンギルダンお父様のもとへ<line-break>　　　　　　　　まいります…。<end>"
EN: "Goodbye… I am going to join Mizuchi and the others, Mother, and Father Angeerdan…<end>"
KO: "안녕히…. 저는 미즈치들과 어머님, 그리고 앙길단 아버님 곁으로 가겠습니다….<end>"

## 1120047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　さようなら…。<line-break>私は、ミズチたちや<line-break>　お母様のもとへ<line-break>　　まいります…。<end>"
EN: "Goodbye… I am going to join Mizuchi and the others, and Mother…<end>"
KO: "안녕히…. 저는 미즈치들과 어머님 곁으로 가겠습니다….<end>"

## 1120048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2空中都市が…<line-break>ラドラスが…落ちたのね。<line-break>　<line-break>………………。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>3おおおおぉぉーーーーー！！　　　　<line-break>イークレムン、<line-break>イークレムンよ…。<line-break>この父を…許してくれ…。<line-break>最後の最後まで、何ひとつ<line-break>父らしいことをしてやれなんだ…！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>4ああっ！！　落ちちゃったわ！<line-break>あそこには…、あの中には……。<line-break>　<line-break>…ダメ。<line-break>悲しすぎて、これ以上は<line-break>言葉にならないかも…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>5ちくしょう！<line-break>ちくしょう！！<line-break>ちくしょう！！！　　　<line-break>助けられなかった！<line-break>誰も！　何も！<line-break>ち…くしょ……ぉ……！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>13クッ…！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>10うあああああ！！<line-break>ラドラス、落ちちまったぁぁぁ！！<line-break>姉ちゃん！　姉ちゃん！！<line-break>なあ、あの中の人たちは！？<line-break>巫女のみんなは！？<line-break>なあ、姉ちゃん、姉…ちゃん…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>14ラドラスが…落ちよった…。<line-break>わしらの力が、わしらの力が<line-break>足りんかったばかりに…。<line-break>すまんかったなあ…。<line-break>本当に、すまんかったなあ…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>15<value:$28>…。<line-break>ラドラスが…落ちたよ…。　<line-break>人が、たくさんの人が…。<line-break>くやしいよ…。<line-break>また、僕が無力なせいで…<line-break>僕には、何もできなかった…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>17まぁったく！　人間のクセに<line-break>分不相応なものを作るから<line-break>こんなことになるのよ！！<line-break>…まったく…、<line-break>あんなもの作るから…。<line-break>ホントにもう…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>19イヤだよぉ、<value:$28>！<line-break>ラドラス、落ちちゃったぁ！！<line-break>　<line-break>おねえちゃんたちも<line-break>砂漠の人たちも、死んじゃったぁ！<line-break>ひどいよ、ひどいよ……。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>26イズキヤルだけではのうて<line-break>巫女様方までも<line-break>救えなんだか…！<line-break>シャリ、<line-break>お主のねらいはなんじゃ？<line-break>何をやろうとしておる！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>27彼らはあれを守るために生まれ<line-break>守るべくして死んだのだ。<line-break>悔いはあるまい。<line-break>それが定め。<line-break>もし、彼らでなければ<line-break>後の世の誰かがああなったのだ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>11あれが…定め…。<line-break>誰も、運命には逆らえない…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>20………………。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>193そ、そんなことって…。<line-break>私たちだけが助かって…<line-break>あの中にいた人たちが…！<line-break>こんなの…ひどすぎます！！<end><if><value:$01><equal>191…ごめんなさい。<line-break>あたしたちの力が足りなくて…。<line-break>ごめん…ごめんね…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>190くそ！<line-break>俺の力が及ばなかったばかりに！<line-break>すまない！<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2The Floating City... Ladras has fallen. ...<end><if><value:$01><equal>3Aaaaaaaagh!! Iiklmn... Oh, Iiklmn... Forgive your father... In the end, I never did a single thing a father should have done for you!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4Oh! It fell! There... inside it... No... It's too sad. I don't think I can say any more...<end><if><value:$01><equal>5Damn it! Damn it!! Damn it!!! I couldn't save anyone! I couldn't save anything! Damn... it...<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Ngh...!<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Aaaaaagh!! Ladras fell!! Sis! Sis!! What about the people inside!? What about all the priestesses!? Come on, Sis... Sis...<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Ladras... has fallen... All because we weren't strong enough... I'm sorry. I'm so very sorry...<end><if><value:$01><equal>15<value:$28>... Ladras has fallen... So many people... It's so frustrating... Once again, I was powerless. I couldn't do anything...<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Honestly! That's what happens when mere humans build things beyond their station!! They just had to build that thing... Honestly...<end><if><value:$01><equal>19No, <value:$28>! Ladras fell!! The older girls and the people of the desert... they're all dead! It's awful! It's so awful...<end><if><value:$01><equal>26We failed to save not only Izkiyal, but even the priestesses...! Shalli, what are you after? What are you trying to do!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>27They were born to protect that, and they died as they were meant to. They can have no regrets. Such was their destiny. If it had not been them, someone in a later age would have met the same fate.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11That was... destiny... No one can defy fate...<end><if><value:$01><equal>20...<end><if><value:$01><equal>193H-how could this happen...? We were the only ones saved, while all those people inside...! It's too horrible!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191I'm sorry... We weren't strong enough... I'm sorry... I'm so sorry...<end><if><value:$01><equal>190Damn it! All because I wasn't strong enough! I'm sorry!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2공중도시가… 라드라스가… 떨어졌어. ……………….<end><if><value:$01><equal>3오오오오오――!! 이크렘문, 이크렘문아…. 이 아비를 용서해다오…. 마지막까지 아비다운 일 하나 해 주지 못했다…!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4아아! 떨어졌어! 저 안에는…. …안 돼. 너무 슬퍼서 더는 말이 안 나와….<end><if><value:$01><equal>5젠장! 젠장!! 젠장!!! 구하지 못했어! 누구도! 아무것도! 제…길……!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13큭…!<end><if><value:$01><equal>10으아아아!! 라드라스가 떨어졌어!! 누나! 누나!! 저 안 사람들은!? 무녀들은!? 누나, 누…나….<end><if><value:$01><equal>14라드라스가… 떨어졌구나…. 우리 힘이 부족해서…. 미안하다…. 정말 미안하다….<end><if><value:$01><equal>15<value:$28>…. 라드라스가… 떨어졌어…. 많은 사람들이…. 분해…. 또 내가 무력해서… 아무것도 못 했어….<end><if><value:$01><equal>17정말! 인간 주제에 분수에 안 맞는 걸 만드니까 이런 일이 되는 거야!! …정말… 그런 걸 만드니까….<end><if><value:$01><equal>19싫어어, <value:$28>! 라드라스가 떨어졌어!! 언니들도 사막 사람들도 죽었어! 너무해, 너무해…….<end><if><value:$01><equal>26이즈키얄뿐 아니라 무녀님들까지 구하지 못했나…! 샤리, 네 목적은 뭐냐? 무슨 짓을 하려는 게냐!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>27그들은 그것을 지키기 위해 태어나 지키다 죽었다. 후회는 없겠지. 그것이 운명. 그들이 아니었다면 훗날 다른 누군가가 그렇게 됐을 것이다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11저게… 운명…. 누구도 운명에는 거스를 수 없어….<end><if><value:$01><equal>20……………….<end><if><value:$01><equal>193그, 그럴 수가…. 우리만 살아남고 저 안 사람들은…! 이건… 너무해요!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191…미안해. 우리가 힘이 부족해서…. 미안해… 미안해….<end><if><value:$01><equal>190젠장! 내 힘이 미치지 못해서! 미안하다!<end>"

## 1120049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　動力部、不要生命体混入<line-break>　自動排除反応、動作開始<line-break>排除終了条件、生命活動停止<end>"
EN: "Power unit: unwanted life-form intrusion. Automatic elimination response... initiating. Elimination end condition: cessation of life activity.<end>"
KO: "동력부, 불필요 생명체 혼입. 자동 배제 반응, 작동 개시. 배제 종료 조건, 생명 활동 정지.<end>"

## 1120050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　 動力部、大破…　　　　　　　　<line-break>拘束機能、自動終了…<line-break>動力部全機能…停止…<end>"
EN: "Power unit: critically damaged... Restraint function: automatically terminating... All power unit functions... ceasing...<end>"
KO: "동력부 대파…. 구속 기능 자동 종료…. 동력부 전 기능… 정지…<end>"

## 1120051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様…、<line-break>こんな所にまで…。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>また私は、自分の役目を<line-break>果たしきれませんでした。<line-break>道具に、なりきれませんでした…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... You came all the way here... Once again, I failed to fulfill my role. I couldn't fully become a tool...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님…, 이런 곳까지…. 또 저는 제 역할을 다하지 못했습니다. 도구가 되지 못했습니다….<end>"

## 1120052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ…、実は、私も<line-break>ホッとしているのです。<line-break>なぜでしょう…こんな感覚…。　　<line-break>道具としての自分をまっとうできず、<line-break>こうしてあなたに助けられることを<line-break>こんなにも嬉しく思うなんて…。<end>"
EN: "Yes... The truth is, I feel relieved too. Why do I feel this way...? I failed in my duty as a tool, yet being saved by you fills me with such happiness...<end>"
KO: "네… 사실 저도 안도하고 있습니다. 왜일까요… 이런 느낌은…. 도구로서의 역할을 다하지 못하고 이렇게 당신에게 도움받는 걸 이토록 기쁘게 느끼다니….<end>"

## 1120053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>よ…。<line-break>そなたがここまで来ること、<line-break>このエアにも見通せなんだ。<line-break>無限のソウルを持つ者の未来は<line-break>誰にも見えぬか。<line-break>しかし、これからどうするのじゃ？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Even I, Er, did not foresee that you would come this far. Is the future of the Bearer of the Infinite Soul hidden from all eyes? But what are we to do now?<end>"

## 1120059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1120060

## 1120061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1120062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml

## 1120063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私…ずっと考えてました。<line-break><value:$28>さんが助けてくれたら<line-break>どんなにうれしいだろうって。<line-break>そして今、<value:$28>さんは<line-break>本当に助けに来てくれて…。<line-break>私、本当にうれしいです。<end>"
EN: "All this time, <value:$28>, I kept thinking how happy I would be if you came to save me. And now you really have come to save me, <value:$28>... I am so happy.<end>"
KO: "저… 계속 생각했어요. <value:$28> 씨가 도와주면 얼마나 기쁠까 하고. 그리고 지금 <value:$28> 씨가 정말로 도와주러 와 줘서…. 저, 정말 기뻐요.<end>"

## 1120064

## 1120065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1120067

## 1120068

## 1120070

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかっておる。<line-break>あそこへ飛びたいというのじゃな？<line-break>大丈夫じゃ。それくらいの魔力はある。<line-break>時間がない。<line-break>ゆくぞ。<end>"

## 1120071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このような所まで…。　　　　　　<line-break>まったく、そなたのことだけは<line-break>このわらわにも見通せんわ…。<line-break>しかし、見通せぬがゆえに<line-break>嬉しさも、ひとしおではあるがな…。<end>"
EN: "To think you would come all this way... Truly, you alone remain beyond even my sight. Yet that only makes my joy the greater...<end>"
KO: "이런 곳까지…. 정말 네 일만큼은 이 나도 내다볼 수 없구나…. 하지만 내다볼 수 없기에 기쁨도 더 크다고 할까….<end>"

## 1120072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あー、んー、あのー…<line-break>いい雰囲気なのはわかるんだけど<line-break>そろそろ起きあがってもいいかな？<end>"
EN: "Uh, well, um... I know this is a touching moment and all, but can I get up now?<end>"
KO: "아, 음, 저기…. 분위기 좋은 건 알겠는데 슬슬 일어나도 될까?<end>"

## 1120073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この都市が落ちるまで、<line-break>そんなに時間がありませんね…。<line-break>どうしましょう？<end>"
EN: "There is not much time before this city falls... What should we do?<end>"
KO: "이 도시가 떨어질 때까지 시간이 그리 많지 않네요…. 어떻게 하죠?<end>"

## 1120074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "役割を果たせなかったから…<line-break>不要な存在だから…、<line-break>ここで…死ぬのですね…。<end>"
EN: "I failed to fulfill my role... I am not needed. So I am to die here...<end>"
KO: "역할을 다하지 못했으니까… 필요 없는 존재니까… 여기서… 죽는 거군요…<end>"

## 1120075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカなこと言っちゃいけないよ！！<line-break>ボクたちは生き残るんだ。<line-break>もちろん、ラドラスのみんなも守る！<end>"
EN: "Don't say that! We're going to survive. And of course, we'll save everyone in Ladras too!<end>"

## 1120077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "I understand. You want to teleport to the control chamber, the room they first took you to. There is no time. Let us go.<end>"
KO: "알고 있다. 처음 끌려갔던 제어실, 그곳으로 날아가고 싶은 것이지? 시간이 없다. 가자.<end>"

## 1120078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "い、今さら、何しに来たんだよ。<line-break>ずっと、ずっと、いつだって<line-break>ボクはキミのことを待ってたのに…。<line-break>ホントに…。<line-break>来るのが遅すぎるんだよぉ…。<end>"
EN: "W-what are you doing here now? I waited for you. All that time, every single moment, I waited for you... Honestly... You took far too long...<end>"
KO: "이, 이제 와서 뭐 하러 온 거야. 계속, 계속, 언제나 난 널 기다렸는데… 정말… 너무 늦었잖아…<end>"

## 1120079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…まあ、でも、<line-break>こうして来てくれたから、よし！<end>"

## 1120081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすがに今回は<line-break>ものすっごく恐かったよ～。<line-break>　<line-break>ボク、ここでラドラスに　　<line-break>魔力吸いとられて……、<line-break>もうダメかと…あ！！<end>"
EN: "That was really, really scary! Ladras was draining my magic here, and I thought I was done for... Ah!!<end>"
KO: "이번엔 정말 엄청 무서웠어~. 나 여기서 라드라스한테 마력을 빨려서…… 이제 끝인가 했는데… 아!!<end>"

## 1120082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "みんな！<line-break>大丈夫！？<end>"
EN: "Everyone! Are you all right?!<end>"
KO: "다들! 괜찮아!?<end>"

## 1120083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そなたに心配されるほど<line-break>弱くはないわ、地の巫女よ。<end>"
EN: "I am not so weak as to need your concern, Priestess of Earth.<end>"
KO: "그대에게 걱정받을 만큼 약하지는 않다, 땅의 무녀여.<end>"

## 1120084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだ少し、体に力が入りませんが…<line-break>大丈夫です。<value:$28>さん、<line-break>ありがとうございます。<end>"
EN: "I still feel a little weak, but I am all right. Thank you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아직 몸에 힘이 잘 들어가진 않지만… 괜찮습니다. <value:$28>님, 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1120085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ですが、このラドラスは<line-break>もうすぐ落ちます。<line-break>これも、結局は意味がありません…。<end>"
EN: "But Ladras will fall soon. In the end, this will all have been for nothing...<end>"
KO: "하지만 이 라드라스는 곧 추락합니다. 이것도 결국 아무 의미가 없습니다…<end>"

## 1120086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなことない！！　　　　　　　　<line-break>きっと、何か方法はあるよ。<line-break>希望も、可能性もあるよ！<line-break>ボクたちは生き残るんだ！<line-break>もちろん、ラドラスのみんなも守る！<end>"
EN: "That's not true! There has to be a way. There's still hope―still a chance! We'll survive! And of course, we'll save everyone in Ladras too!<end>"
KO: "그렇지 않아!! 분명 방법이 있을 거야. 희망도 가능성도 있어! 우리는 살아남을 거야! 물론 라드라스 사람들도 지킬 거고!<end>"

## 1120087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エア！！<end>"
EN: "Er!!<end>"
KO: "에아!!<end>"

## 1120088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかっておる。<line-break>最初に連れて行かれた制御の間、<line-break>あそこへ飛びたいというのじゃな？<line-break>時間がない。<line-break>ゆくぞ。<end>"
EN: "I understand. You want to teleport to the control chamber, the room they first took you to. There is no time. Let us go.<end>"
KO: "알고 있다. 처음 끌려갔던 제어실, 그곳으로 날아가고 싶은 것이지? 시간이 없다. 가자.<end>"

## 1120089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ホントに、時間がないみたいだね。<line-break>じゃあ、さっそく始めよう。<line-break>　<line-break>フレアとイークレムンは<line-break>自分たちの精霊の制御球の所に。<line-break>エアは転移の準備をしておいて。<end>"
EN: "Looks like we're really out of time. Let's get started. Flare and Iiklmn, go to your control spheres. Er, get ready to teleport everyone.<end>"
KO: "정말 시간이 없는 모양이네. 그럼 바로 시작하자. 플레어와 이크렘은 각자 자기 정령 제어구로 가. 에아는 전이 준비를 해 둬.<end>"

## 1120090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "転移じゃと？<line-break>エステル、何を考えておる？<end>"
EN: "Teleport? Esther, what are you planning?<end>"
KO: "전이라고? 에스텔, 무슨 생각이냐?<end>"

## 1120091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん、まず、ボクが、せーの！　<line-break>って言ったら、フレアとイークレムンは<line-break>うまく地面に着くように念じて。<line-break>で、地面に着いたらもう一度<line-break>せーの！　って言うから、そしたら<line-break>制御球に、壊れろ！　って念じて。<end>"
EN: "All right. Flare and Iiklmn, when I give the signal, focus on landing safely. Once we're on the ground, I'll give it again. Then focus on breaking the control spheres.<end>"
KO: "응, 먼저 내가 '하나, 둘!' 하면 플레어와 이클렘은 잘 땅에 닿도록 마음속으로 빌어 줘. 그리고 땅에 닿으면 내가 다시 '하나, 둘!' 할 테니까 그때 제어구에 '부서져!'라고 빌어.<end>"

## 1120092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここを、動かなくしよう。<line-break>二度と使えないように。<end>"
EN: "Let's shut this place down, so it can never be used again.<end>"
KO: "여기를 움직이지 못하게 하자. 다시는 쓸 수 없게.<end>"

## 1120093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは！！<line-break>…これを壊すことは…<line-break>動かなくするということは…。<line-break>私たち巫女が今まで<line-break>存在しつづけてきた意味も理由も<line-break>なくなってしまいます。<end>"
EN: "But... if we destroy it―if we render it inoperable―then all meaning and purpose behind our continued existence as priestesses will be gone.<end>"
KO: "그건!! …이걸 부순다는 건… 움직이지 못하게 한다는 건…. 우리 무녀들이 지금까지 존재해 온 의미와 이유도 사라져 버립니다.<end>"

## 1120094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、もし再びこのようなことが<line-break>起きれば、また悲しみが生まれます。<line-break>大切な命が失われるでしょう。<line-break>それよりは、今この空中都市を<line-break>永久に封じた方が、世界にとって<line-break>いいのではないでしょうか。<end>"
EN: "But if something like this happens again, it will bring more sorrow. Precious lives will be lost. Would it not be better for the world if we sealed this Floating City away forever, here and now?<end>"
KO: "하지만 다시 이런 일이 일어나면 또 슬픔이 생기고 소중한 목숨이 사라질 겁니다. 차라리 지금 이 공중도시를 영원히 봉인하는 편이 세계를 위해 더 낫지 않을까요.<end>"

## 1120095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボクもイークレムンと同じ気持ちだよ。<line-break>悲しみや絶望を生むために<line-break>ボクたちはいるんじゃない。<line-break>８００年、ボクたち巫女の存在が<line-break>これのためにあったというのなら、<line-break>今日でそれを終わりにしよう。<end>"
EN: "I feel the same way, Iiklmn. We weren't meant to bring sorrow and despair. If this is what we priestesses have existed for these past eight hundred years, then let's put an end to it today.<end>"
KO: "나도 이크레문과 같은 마음이야. 우리는 슬픔과 절망을 낳기 위해 존재하는 게 아니야. 800년 동안 우리 무녀들의 존재가 이것을 위해 있었던 거라면 오늘로 끝내자.<end>"

## 1120096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、イークレムンとフレアは<line-break>自分たちの制御球の所に行って。<end>"
EN: "Then Iiklmn and Flare, go to your control spheres.<end>"
KO: "그럼 이크레문과 플레어는 자기들 제어구 쪽으로 가 줘.<end>"

## 1120097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エア、転移の方は任せたからね。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>何が起きるかわからないから、<line-break>どこでもいい、安全な場所に<line-break>みんなを飛ばしてね。<end>"
EN: "Er, you'll handle the teleportation. We don't know what might happen, so send everyone somewhere safe―anywhere will do.<end>"
KO: "에아, 전이는 네게 맡길게. 무슨 일이 생길지 모르니 어디든 안전한 곳으로 모두 보내 줘.<end>"

## 1120098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかった。<line-break>任せておけ。<end>"
EN: "Very well. Leave it to me.<end>"
KO: "알았다. 맡겨 둬.<end>"

## 1120099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いい？　じゃ、始めるよ。<end>"
EN: "Ready? Then let's begin.<end>"
KO: "좋아? 그럼 시작할게.<end>"

## 1120100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "せーの！！<end>"
EN: "Now!!<end>"
KO: "하나, 둘!!<end>"

## 1120101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…よし、<line-break>なんとか着地はしたみたいだね。<line-break>じゃ次、壊れろって、いくよ！<line-break>せぇーーーーの！！<end>"
EN: "...All right, looks like we managed to land safely. Next, let's break them! Reaaady... now!!<end>"
KO: "…좋아, 어떻게든 착지는 한 것 같네. 그럼 다음은, 부서져라 하고 간다! 하나ーーーー둘!!<end>"

## 1120102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "昔、これを空に飛ばそうと、<line-break>たくさんの人が犠牲となり<line-break>たくさんの命が空費されたのに…。<end>"
EN: "Long ago, so many people were sacrificed trying to send this into the sky. So many lives were squandered...<end>"
KO: "옛날에 이것을 하늘로 띄우려다 수많은 사람이 희생되고 많은 생명이 헛되이 사라졌는데….<end>"

## 1120103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…今、これは飛べないまま壊される。<end>"
EN: "And now it will be destroyed without ever flying.<end>"
KO: "…지금 이건 날지도 못한 채 부서진다.<end>"

## 1120104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ゴメンね。<line-break>あんなに強い願いだったのに<line-break>かなえてあげられなくて…。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. The wish was so strong, but I couldn't make it come true...<end>"
KO: "…미안해. 그렇게 강한 소원이었는데도 이루어 주지 못해서….<end>"

## 1120105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここならばまず安全じゃろう。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>…しかし、わらわの力では<line-break>このような未来は見えなかった。<line-break>これも<value:$28>の力か…。<end>"
EN: "This place should be safe enough. Yet not even my power foresaw a future like this. Is this your power too, <value:$28>...?<end>"
KO: "여기라면 우선 안전하겠지. …하지만 내 힘으로는 이런 미래를 볼 수 없었다. 이것도 <value:$28>의 힘인가….<end>"

## 1120106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "違うよ、エア。<line-break><value:$28>が特別だから<line-break>こうなったんじゃないよ。　<line-break>みんなで一緒に<line-break>希望を持ってがんばった。<line-break>だから、こうなったんだよ。<end>"
EN: "No, Er. This didn't happen just because <value:$28> is special. We all held on to hope and worked together. That's why it happened.<end>"
KO: "아니야, 에아. <value:$28>이 특별해서 이렇게 된 게 아니야. 모두 함께 희망을 가지고 노력했어. 그래서 이렇게 된 거야.<end>"

## 1120107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ…そうか。<line-break>そなたらしい言葉だな、<line-break>地の巫女よ。<end>"
EN: "Heh... I see. That is just like you, Priestess of Earth.<end>"
KO: "흥… 그런가. 그대다운 말이군, 땅의 무녀여.<end>"

## 1120108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、精霊の巫女たちよ。<line-break>そなたらを、あるべき地へ<line-break>帰してやらねばなるまいな。<end>"
EN: "Now then, priestesses of the spirits. I suppose I must return you to where you belong.<end>"
KO: "자, 정령의 무녀들이여. 너희를 있어야 할 곳으로 돌려보내 줘야겠구나.<end>"

## 1120109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、ボクはラドラスに帰るよ。<line-break>たぶん、もう大丈夫でしょ。<line-break>　<line-break>じゃあね、<value:$28>。<line-break>ラドラスを守ってくれて<line-break>ホントにありがとう！<end>"
EN: "Well, I'm going back to Ladras. Things are probably all right now. See you, <value:$28>. Thank you so much for saving Ladras!<end>"
KO: "그럼 난 라드라스로 돌아갈게. 아마 이제 괜찮겠지. 그럼 안녕, <value:$28>. 라드라스를 지켜 줘서 정말 고마워!<end>"

## 1120110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、私も<line-break>アキュリュースへ帰ります。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>さん、<line-break>アキュリュースに来た時には<line-break>私の所にも来てくださいね。<end>"
EN: "Then I shall return to Aqyurius as well. <value:$28>, when you come to Aqyurius, please visit me.<end>"
KO: "그럼 저도 아큐류스로 돌아가겠습니다. <value:$28>님, 아큐류스에 오시면 제게도 들러 주세요.<end>"

## 1120111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は…<line-break>道具としての役目を<line-break>完全に失ってしまいました…。　　<line-break>これから、どう生きればいいのか、<line-break>それすらもわかりません。<line-break>わかりません…けど…。<end>"
EN: "I have completely lost my purpose as a tool... I do not even know how I am to live from now on. I do not know... and yet...<end>"
KO: "저는… 도구로서의 역할을 완전히 잃어버렸습니다… 앞으로 어떻게 살아야 하는지도 모르겠습니다. 모르겠지만…<end>"

## 1120112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は、生きていきたいと思います。<line-break>あなたと同じこの世界で生きることが<line-break>なぜかとても嬉しいのです…。<line-break>では、私もまいります。<line-break><value:$28>様、<line-break>また、お会いしましょう…。<end>"
EN: "I want to go on living. For some reason, it makes me so happy to live in this same world with you... I too shall be going now. <value:$28>, let us meet again...<end>"
KO: "저는 살아가고 싶습니다. 당신과 같은 이 세계에서 살아간다는 것이 어째서인지 무척 기쁩니다…. 그럼 저도 가겠습니다. <value:$28>님, 또 만나요….<end>"

## 1120113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、そなたも行くがよい。<line-break>無限のソウルを持つ者よ。<line-break>　<line-break>最後に、礼を言うぞ。<line-break>そなたのおかげで、わらわも<line-break>未来に希望を見出せそうじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Now, you too must go, Bearer of the Infinite Soul. But first, you have my thanks. Because of you, it seems that even I may find hope in the future.<end>"
KO: "자, 그대도 가거라. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여. 마지막으로 감사하마. 그대 덕분에 나도 미래에서 희망을 찾을 수 있을 것 같구나.<end>"

## 1120114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、ボクはラドラスに帰るよ。<line-break>たぶん、もう大丈夫でしょ。<line-break>　<line-break>じゃあね、<value:$28>。<line-break>ラドラスを守ってくれて<line-break>ホントにありがとう！<end>"
EN: "Well, I'm going back to Ladras. Things are probably all right now. See you, <value:$28>. Thank you so much for saving Ladras!<end>"
KO: "그럼 난 라드라스로 돌아갈게. 아마 이제 괜찮겠지. 그럼 안녕, <value:$28>. 라드라스를 지켜 줘서 정말 고마워!<end>"

## 1120115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私も、ウルカーンへ戻ります。<line-break>そこにはもう、私の役目は<line-break>何もないかもしれませんが…。<line-break><value:$28>様、<line-break>それでは…。<end>"
EN: "I too will return to Ulkarne. There may no longer be any role for me there, but... Farewell, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "저도 울칸으로 돌아가겠습니다. 그곳에는 이제 제 역할이 아무것도 남지 않았을지도 모르지만…. <value:$28>님, 그럼….<end>"

## 1120116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん…、<line-break>本当にありがとうございました。<line-break>　<line-break>私…私もできることなら<line-break><value:$28>さんの旅に…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... thank you. Truly. If I could, I... I would join <value:$28> on the journey...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님… 정말 감사합니다. 저… 저도 가능하다면 <value:$28>님의 여행에….<end>"

## 1120117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いえ、なんでもありません。<line-break>私は、アキュリュースで<line-break>私のなすべきことをなしましょう。<line-break>では、<value:$28>さん、<line-break>また、アキュリュースへ…<line-break>私の所へいらしてください。<end>"
EN: "No, it is nothing. I shall do what I must in Aqyurius. Well then, <value:$28>, when you next come to Aqyurius... please come and see me.<end>"
KO: "…아니요, 아무것도 아닙니다. 저는 아큐류스에서 제가 해야 할 일을 하겠습니다. 그럼 <value:$28> 씨, 또 아큐류스로… 제게 와 주세요.<end>"

## 1120118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml

## 1120121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私も、ウルカーンへ戻ります。<line-break>そこにはもう、私の役目は<line-break>何もないかもしれませんが…。<line-break><value:$28>様、<line-break>それでは…。<end>"
EN: "I too will return to Ulkarne. There may no longer be any role for me there, but... Farewell, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "저도 울칸으로 돌아가겠습니다. 그곳에는 이제 제 역할이 아무것도 남지 않았을지도 모르지만…. <value:$28>님, 그럼….<end>"

## 1120122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>わらわは、今<line-break>希望を感じておる。　　　　　<line-break>おそらく、わらわにとって<line-break>生まれて始めての希望じゃ。<line-break>未来を強く願うほどのな…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... I feel hope now. Perhaps it is the first hope I have ever known―the kind that makes me yearn for the future.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 나는 지금 희망을 느끼고 있느니라. 아마 내게는 태어나 처음 느끼는 희망일 것이다. 미래를 간절히 바랄 만큼의….<end>"

## 1120123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今までのわらわは<line-break>見える未来に絶望するだけで<line-break>あらがう強さを持たなかった。　<line-break>そなたが、教えてくれたのじゃ。<line-break>未来を切り開く強さを。<line-break>希望を持ち、進む生き方を…。<end>"
EN: "Until now, I could only despair at the future I foresaw. I had not the strength to defy it. You taught me that strength: the strength to carve out a future, to live with hope and press onward.<end>"
KO: "지금까지의 나는 보이는 미래에 절망할 뿐, 맞설 강함을 지니지 못했다. 네가 가르쳐 주었지. 미래를 개척하는 힘을. 희망을 품고 앞으로 나아가는 삶을….<end>"

## 1120124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>行くがよい。<line-break>このままでは、わらわはそなたを<line-break>引きとめてしまいそうじゃ。<end>"
EN: "... Go now. If you remain, I fear I may try to keep you here.<end>"
KO: "………………. 가거라. 이대로라면 내가 그대를 붙잡아 둘 것 같구나.<end>"

## 1120125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、私は<line-break>アキュリュースへ帰ります。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>さん、<line-break>アキュリュースに来た時には<line-break>私の所にも来てくださいね。<end>"

## 1120126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1120127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へへっ、えへへへ…。　　　　　<line-break>ホント、ありがとう。<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>やっぱり、キミはすごいよ。<line-break>ボクにとっては、勇者ネメアより<line-break>ずっとずっと素敵な勇者様だ。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh... Thank you. Really, thank you, <value:$28>. You really are amazing. To me, you're a far more wonderful hero than Hero Nemea.<end>"
KO: "헤헤, 에헤헤헤…. 정말 고마워, <value:$28>. 역시 넌 대단해. 나한테는 용사 네메아보다 훨씬 더 멋진 용사님이야.<end>"

## 1120128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>ボク…、ホントに……。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... I... I truly...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 나… 정말로…….<end>"

## 1120129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よいか？　エステルよ。<end>"
EN: "Ready, Esther?<end>"
KO: "잘 듣거라, 에스텔.<end>"

## 1120130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え！！<line-break>あ、あ、あ、ああ、うん。<line-break>よろしくお願い…します。<end>"
EN: "What?! Ah, uh... y-yes. Please.<end>"
KO: "에!! 아, 아, 아, 아아, 응. 잘 부탁…드립니다.<end>"

## 1120131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、ボク<line-break>ラドラスのみんなの所に帰るよ。<line-break>やっぱり、ちょっと心配だし。<line-break>じゃあね！　<value:$28>。<line-break>ラドラスを……ボクたちを助けてくれて<line-break>ホントにありがとう！！<end>"
EN: "Well, I'm going back to everyone in Ladras. I'm still a little worried about them. See you, <value:$28>. Thank you so much for saving Ladras... for saving all of us!!<end>"
KO: "그럼 난 라드라스 사람들한테 돌아갈게. 역시 조금 걱정되니까. 그럼 안녕, <value:$28>. 라드라스를…… 우리를 도와줘서 정말 고마워!!<end>"

## 1120132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、そなたも行くがよい。<line-break>無限のソウルを持つ者よ。　　<line-break>　<line-break>最後に、礼を言うぞ。<line-break>そなたのおかげで、わらわも<line-break>未来に希望を見出せそうじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Now, you too must go, Bearer of the Infinite Soul. But first, you have my thanks. Because of you, it seems that even I may find hope in the future.<end>"
KO: "자, 그대도 가거라. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여. 마지막으로 감사하마. 그대 덕분에 나도 미래에서 희망을 찾을 수 있을 것 같구나.<end>"

## 1120133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行くがよい。　　　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>よ。<line-break>　<line-break>そなたの力ならば<line-break>この大陸を包む暗き運命をも<line-break>打ち砕くことができようぞ。<end>"
EN: "Go forth, <value:$28>. With your power, you may yet shatter even the dark fate that hangs over this continent.<end>"
KO: "가거라, <value:$28>여. 그대의 힘이라면 이 대륙을 뒤덮은 어두운 운명마저 깨뜨릴 수 있을 것이다.<end>"

## 1120134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　待っていたぞ。　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　地の巫女よ。そして<line-break>　　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ…。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　　　　かつて汝らに<line-break>　　　　巨人フルーヴを倒してもらったが<line-break>　　　　　　我の力はいまだ戻らぬ。<end>"
EN: "I have been waiting for you, Priestess of Earth―and you, Bearer of the Infinite Soul... You once defeated Giant Fleuve at my behest, yet my power has still not returned.<end>"
KO: "기다리고 있었다. 대지의 무녀여. 그리고 무한의 소울을 가진 자여…. 예전에 그대들에게 거인 프루브를 쓰러뜨려 달라고 했지만, 내 힘은 아직 돌아오지 않았다.<end>"

## 1120135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　来たか。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ。<line-break>　　　　　　我は汝を待っておった…。　<line-break>　　　　　　　　かつて汝に<line-break>　　　巨人フルーヴを倒してもらったが<line-break>　　　　　我の力はいまだ戻らぬ。<end>"
EN: "So you have come, Bearer of the Infinite Soul. I have been waiting for you... You once defeated Giant Fleuve at my behest, yet my power has still not returned.<end>"
KO: "왔는가. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여. 나는 너를 기다리고 있었다…. 예전에 네가 거인 프루브를 쓰러뜨려 줬지만, 나의 힘은 아직 돌아오지 않았다.<end>"

## 1120136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　巨人フルーヴの兄　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　巨人パンタ・レイが<line-break>　　　　　我が力を封じておるのだ。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　ゆえに今再び、我は汝に求める。<line-break>　　　　　　　彼の巨人を退け<line-break>　　　　　我の力を解き放ってほしい。<end>"
EN: "Giant Fleuve's elder brother, Giant Panta Rhei, has sealed away my power. Thus I ask this of you once more: drive back that giant and set my power free.<end>"
KO: "거인 프루브의 형인 거인 판타 레이가 내 힘을 봉인하고 있다. 그러니 다시 한 번 그대에게 부탁하겠다. 그 거인을 물리치고 내 힘을 해방해 다오.<end>"

## 1120137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかった<end>"
EN: "All right.<end>"
KO: "알았어<end>"

## 1120138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "関係ないな<end>"
EN: "It's got nothing to do with me.<end>"
KO: "상관없어<end>"

## 1120139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　　よし。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　では我は、汝を<line-break>　　　　　　精霊の座へと導かん。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　汝、気高き地の光を越え<line-break>　　　　　　彼の巨人に挑むがよい。<line-break>　　　　　挑み、打ち破るがよい…。<end>"
EN: "Very well. Then I shall guide you to the Seat of the Spirits. Pass beyond the light of the exalted land and challenge that giant. Challenge the giant, and vanquish it…<end>"
KO: "좋다. 그럼 내가 그대를 정령의 자리로 인도하리라. 그대, 고귀한 땅의 빛을 넘어 저 거인에게 도전하라. 도전하여 쓰러뜨려라….<end>"

## 1120140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　そうか。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　ならば無理強いはすまい。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　うつろいやすき人の子よ。<line-break>　　　　　　気が変わったならば<line-break>　　　　　また我を訪ねるがよい。<end>"
EN: "I see. Then I shall not force you. O fickle child of man, if you change your mind, come and visit me again.<end>"
KO: "그런가. 그렇다면 억지로 강요하진 않겠다. 변덕스러운 인간의 아이여. 마음이 바뀌면 다시 나를 찾아오거라.<end>"

## 1120141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　どうした？　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　巨人を前に臆したか？<end>"
EN: "What's wrong? Lost your nerve before the giant?<end>"
KO: "왜 그러느냐? 거인을 앞에 두고 겁먹었느냐?<end>"

## 1120142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなことはない<end>"
EN: "Not at all.<end>"
KO: "그렇지 않아<end>"

## 1120143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめておこうかな<end>"
EN: "Maybe I should call it off.<end>"
KO: "그만둘까<end>"

## 1120144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　ならば行くがよい。　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "If so, then go.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 가라.<end>"

## 1120145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　そうか。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　ならば無理強いはすまい。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　うつろいやすき人の子よ。<line-break>　　　　　　気が変わったならば<line-break>　　　　　また我を訪ねるがよい。<end>"
EN: "I see. Then I shall not force you. Fickle child of man, if you change your mind, you may seek me out again.<end>"

## 1120146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フン…貴様が<line-break>無限のソウルを持つ者か…。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>この世界のため、<line-break>そして、我が弟フルーヴの無念のため、<line-break>貴様を無へと帰してやろう！！<end>"
EN: "Hmph... So you are the Bearer of the Infinite Soul. For the sake of this world, and for my younger brother Fleuve's unfulfilled hopes, I shall return you to nothingness!<end>"
KO: "흥… 네가 무한의 소울을 지닌 자인가…. 이 세계를 위해, 그리고 내 동생 프루브의 한을 위해 너를 무로 돌려보내겠다!!<end>"

## 1120147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "く、無念…。<line-break>我が力もってしても<line-break>貴様らの愚行をとめられぬか…。<line-break>大きく、しかし愚かなる魂よ。<line-break>やがて来る闇に絶望し<line-break>己が行いをくいるがいい…。<end>"
EN: "Damn... So even my power cannot put an end to your folly. O great but foolish soul, despair at the darkness soon to come, and rue what you have done.<end>"
KO: "크, 분하다… 내 힘으로도 네놈들의 어리석은 짓을 막지 못하는가… 크고도 어리석은 영혼이여. 다가올 어둠에 절망하며 자신의 행동을 후회하거라…<end>"

## 1120148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　さすがだな…。　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　　よくやった、<line-break>　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ…。<end>"
EN: "Impressive... You have done well, Bearer of the Infinite Soul...<end>"
KO: "역시 대단하군…. 잘했다, 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여….<end>"

## 1120149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　よくぞ、巨人パンタ・レイを倒した。　　<line-break>　　　これで、我の力を妨げる者はない。<line-break>　　礼を言うぞ。無限のソウルを持つ者よ。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　　我は感謝の証として、<line-break>　　　　汝に我の力の一部を与えよう。<line-break>　　　人の子にはあまるほどの巨大な力を。<end>"
EN: "You have done well to defeat the giant Panta Rhei. Now no one remains who can hinder my power. You have my thanks, Bearer of the Infinite Soul. As a token of my gratitude, I shall grant you a portion of my power―an immense power beyond what any child of man could contain.<end>"
KO: "잘도 거인 판타 레이를 쓰러뜨렸구나. 이제 짐의 힘을 방해할 자는 없다. 감사하마, 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여. 감사의 증표로 그대에게 짐의 힘 일부를 주겠다. 인간의 아이에게는 넘칠 만큼 거대한 힘을.<end>"

## 1120150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "地の最高位の魔法が解放された。<end>"
EN: "The highest-level earth magic has been unlocked.<end>"
KO: "대지의 최고위 마법이 해방되었다.<end>"

## 1120151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　エステルよ…、　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　　地の巫女よ…。<end>"
EN: "Esther... Priestess of Earth...<end>"
KO: "에스텔이여… 땅의 무녀여….<end>"

## 1120152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何？　精霊神様。<end>"
EN: "What is it, Spirit God?<end>"

## 1120153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　我の力を分け与えよう。　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　地の巫女である汝ならば<line-break>　　　　受け入れることができるはずだ。<end>"
EN: "I shall share my power with you. As the Priestess of Earth, you should be able to receive it.<end>"
KO: "나의 힘을 나누어 주마. 땅의 무녀인 그대라면 받아들일 수 있을 것이다.<end>"

## 1120154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エステルの巫女としての力が<line-break>大きく引き出された。<end>"
EN: "A great deal of Esther's power as a priestess has been brought forth.<end>"
KO: "에스텔의 무녀로서의 힘이 크게 끌어내졌다.<end>"

## 1120155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すごい…。<line-break>これが、ボクの力…？<end>"
EN: "Incredible... Is this my power...?<end>"
KO: "대단해…. 이게 내 힘…?<end>"

## 1120156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　さよう。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　その力、我ら精霊の使いとして<line-break>　　　　　　存分にふるうがよい。<end>"
EN: "Just so. Wield that power to the fullest in the service of us spirits.<end>"
KO: "그렇다. 그 힘을 우리 정령의 사자로서 마음껏 휘두르거라.<end>"

## 1120157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　では、さらばだ。　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ。<end>"
EN: "Then farewell, Bearer of the Infinite Soul.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 잘 가라. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여.<end>"

## 1120158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　来たか、無限のソウルを持つ者よ…。　　<line-break>　　　　ならば、我は今一度汝に乞う。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　我が大いなる力を封じる<line-break>　　　　　巨人、パンタ・レイを<line-break>　　　　　倒してはくれまいか？<end>"

KO: "왔는가, 무한의 소울을 가진 자여…. 그렇다면 나는 다시 한 번 그대에게 청하겠다. 나의 위대한 힘을 봉인하는 거인 판타 레이를 쓰러뜨려 주지 않겠는가?<end>"

## 1120159

## 1120160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml

## 1120161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　 　　来たか、地の巫女よ。　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　　　ん、汝は…。<line-break>　　　なるほど、無限のソウルを持つ者か…。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　 　 　　大いなる可能性を持つ<line-break>　　　  　　 　　人の子らよ。<line-break>　　　  　　 　汝に頼みがある。<end>"
EN: "You have come, Priestess of Earth. Hmm, and you are... I see. The Bearer of the Infinite Soul... Children of man, you hold great potential. I have a favor to ask of you.<end>"
KO: "왔는가, 땅의 무녀여. 음, 그대는…. 그렇군, 무한의 소울을 지닌 자인가…. 커다란 가능성을 지닌 인간의 아이들이여. 그대에게 부탁이 있다.<end>"

## 1120162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　 　　ここより先に、<line-break>　　 フルーヴという巨人がおる。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　汝の力で葬ってくれ。<line-break>　 　彼奴は我の力を妨げる力。<line-break>　　 　不要な存在なのだ…。<end>"
EN: "Beyond this place dwells a giant called Fleuve. Bury it with your power. That creature is a force that impedes my own. Its existence serves no purpose...<end>"
KO: "이 앞에 프루브라는 거인이 있다. 네 힘으로 묻어 버려라. 그자는 내 힘을 방해하는 힘. 불필요한 존재다….<end>"

## 1120163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　　よし。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　では我は、汝を<line-break>　　　　 　　精霊の座へと導かん。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　汝、気高き地の光を越え<line-break>　　　　　　彼の巨人に挑むがよい。<line-break>　　　　 　挑み、打ち破るがよい…。<end>"
EN: "Good. I shall guide you to the Seat of the Spirits. Pass beyond the noble light of earth and face that giant. Challenge it, and strike it down...<end>"

## 1120164

## 1120165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1120166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フン…地の巫女と<line-break>無限のソウルを持つ者か。<line-break>　<line-break>グラジェオンめ…<line-break>闇の力の高まりを知り、<line-break>我を滅ぼさんと欲したか。<end>"
EN: "Hmph... The Priestess of Earth and the Bearer of the Infinite Soul. Curse you, Grajeon... You sensed the growing power of darkness and sought to destroy me?<end>"
KO: "흥… 땅의 무녀와 무한의 소울을 지닌 자인가. 그라제온 놈… 어둠의 힘이 커지는 걸 알고 나를 멸하려 했나.<end>"

## 1120167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、汝らごときに倒される<line-break>このフルーヴではない。<line-break>来るがよい、精霊神のしもべよ！！<end>"
EN: "But Fleuve will not fall to the likes of you. Come, servants of the Spirit God!<end>"
KO: "하지만 너희 따위에게 쓰러질 이 프루브가 아니다. 덤벼라, 정령신의 종들이여!!<end>"

## 1120168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "愚かなり、人の子よ。<line-break>精霊神に躍らされ、<line-break>我を撃ち、闇を呼ぶか…。<line-break>愚かなり…愚かなり…。<end>"
EN: "Foolish child of man. Made to dance to the Spirit God's tune, you strike me down and call forth darkness...? Foolish... How foolish.<end>"
KO: "어리석구나, 인간의 아이여. 정령신에게 놀아나, 나를 쏘고 어둠을 부르는가…. 어리석구나… 어리석어….<end>"

## 1120169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Well done... my Priestess of Earth... Bearer of the Infinite Soul...<end>"
KO: "잘했다…. 나의 대지의 무녀여…. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여….<end>"

## 1120170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　地の巫女よ、無限のソウルを持つ者よ。　　<line-break>　　　よくぞ、巨人フルーヴを倒した。<line-break>　　　心から、礼を言わせてもらうぞ。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　　さあ、再び行くがよい。<line-break>　　　　　汝らのなすべき旅の道へ。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　さらばだ。<end>"
EN: "Priestess of Earth! Bearer of the Infinite Soul! You have done well to defeat Giant Fleuve. I offer you my heartfelt thanks. Now, all of you, go forth once more upon the path you must travel. Farewell.<end>"
KO: "대지의 무녀여, 무한의 소울을 가진 자여. 거인 프루브를 잘 쓰러뜨렸다. 진심으로 감사를 표하마. 자, 다시 가거라. 그대들이 가야 할 여행의 길로. 잘 가라.<end>"

## 1120171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　来たか、地の巫女よ。そして、　　　　<line-break>　　　　 　無限のソウルを持つ者よ…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　ならば、我は今一度汝に乞う。<line-break>　　　　　　我の力を封じつづける<line-break>　　　 　彼の巨人を倒してはくれまいか？<end>"
EN: "You have come, Priestess of Earth, and you, Bearer of the Infinite Soul... Then I beseech you once more: will you defeat the giant that continues to seal away my power?<end>"
KO: "왔는가, 대지의 무녀여. 그리고 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여…. 그렇다면 나는 다시 한 번 그대에게 청하겠다. 계속해서 나의 힘을 봉하고 있는 그 거인을 쓰러뜨려 주지 않겠는가?<end>"

## 1120172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ！？<line-break>せ、制御の間ですか？<end>"
EN: "What!? Th-the control chamber?<end>"
KO: "엣!? 제, 제어실 말인가요?<end>"

## 1120173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ！？<line-break>ど、動力の間ですか？<end>"
EN: "What!? Th-the Chamber of Power?<end>"
KO: "네!? 도, 동력실인가요?<end>"

## 1120174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec112-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あちらです！<end>"
EN: "O-over there!<end>"
KO: "저, 저쪽입니다!<end>"

## 1130001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec113-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "訃報だ。<line-break>アンギルダンは死んだよ。　<line-break>反逆者としてね。<line-break>敗戦について挑発してやれば<line-break>すぐにかみついてきた。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I have news of a death. Angeerdan is dead. He died a traitor. I baited him over the defeat, and he bit at once.<end>"
KO: "부고다. 앙길단은 죽었어. 반역자로서 말이지. 패전에 대해 도발했더니 바로 덤벼들더군.<end>"

## 1130002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec113-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすがに老いたりとはいえ、<line-break>骨のある男だった。<line-break>少しは戦いも楽しめたかな。<end>"
EN: "Old as he was, the man had backbone. At least the fight proved mildly entertaining.<end>"
KO: "아무리 늙었다 해도 제법 뼈대 있는 사내였어. 전투도 조금은 즐길 수 있었군.<end>"

## 1130003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec113-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君は長生きするのだな、<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>…無限のソウルを持つ者よ。　　　　　<end>"
EN: "You will live a long life, <value:$28>... Bearer of the Infinite Soul.<end>"
KO: "오래 살아야 한다, <value:$28>. …무한의 소울을 지닌 자여.<end>"

## 1130004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec113-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの戦いに生き残ったのならば、<line-break>ぼんくらどもとは違うのだろうから。<line-break>資質だけでなく、現在の能力的にもな。<end>"
EN: "If you survived that battle, you must be different from those fools: not merely in promise, but in your abilities as they stand now.<end>"
KO: "그 싸움에서 살아남았다면 얼간이들과는 다르겠지. 자질뿐 아니라 지금 능력도 말이야.<end>"

## 1130005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec113-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、アンギルダンには　　　　<line-break>感謝している。<line-break>　<line-break>本来、人間の革新の日の目を<line-break>見ることのないぼんくらどもを、<line-break>大量に処分してくれたのだからな。<end>"
EN: "Still, I am grateful to Angeerdan. He disposed of great numbers of fools who would never have lived to see the dawn of Humanity's Evolution.<end>"
KO: "하지만 앙길단에게는 감사하고 있다. 본래 인류 혁신의 빛을 볼 일도 없을 멍청이들을 대량으로 처리해 줬으니 말이지.<end>"

EN: ""
KO: "예비 메시지 1 자기브, 볼륨 4<end>"

## 1140000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec114-part99.toml

JP: "ザギヴメッセージｖ５用開始<end>"
EN: ""

## 1140001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec114-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴに気に入られているようだな。<line-break>だが、気をつけることだ。<line-break>見た目では判断できぬからな。<end>"
EN: "It seems Zagiv has taken a liking to you. But be careful. Appearances can be deceiving.<end>"
KO: "자기브에게 꽤 마음에 든 모양이군. 하지만 조심해. 겉모습만으로는 판단할 수 없으니까.<end>"

## 1140002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec114-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく来てくれたわね、　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>　<line-break>今日は、どうしても<line-break>あなたにお礼が言いたかったの。<end>"
EN: "I'm glad you came, <value:$28>. I really wanted to thank you today.<end>"
KO: "잘 와 줬어, <value:$28>. 오늘은 꼭 네게 감사하다는 말을 하고 싶었어.<end>"

## 1140003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec114-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたがギルドで受けていた　　　　　<line-break>モンスター退治の依頼の中には、<line-break>闇の怪物も含まれていたのよ。<line-break>私もずっと追っていたの。<line-break>一匹でも多くこの世から消すためにね。<end>"
EN: "Some of the monsters you were hired to hunt through the Guild were creatures of darkness. I'd been hunting them too, trying to rid the world of as many as I could.<end>"
KO: "당신이 길드에서 받은 몬스터 퇴치 의뢰 중에는 어둠의 괴물도 포함되어 있었어. 나도 계속 쫓고 있었지. 한 마리라도 더 이 세상에서 없애려고.<end>"

## 1140004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec114-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたのおかげで<line-break>ずいぶんと気分が楽になったわ。<line-break>ありがとう、殺してくれて。<end>"
EN: "You've taken a great weight off my mind. Thank you for killing them.<end>"
KO: "당신 덕분에 마음이 한결 편해졌어. 고마워, 죽여 줘서.<end>"

## 1140005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec114-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は、闇を憎んでいるのよ。　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>なぜなら、私の体の中にも<line-break>闇が巣くっているから。<line-break>あなたも墓地で見たでしょう？<end>"
EN: "I hate darkness because it lurks inside my body, too. You saw it in the graveyard, didn't you?<end>"
KO: "난 어둠을 증오해. 내 몸속에도 어둠이 깃들어 있으니까. 당신도 묘지에서 봤잖아?<end>"

## 1140006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec114-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ、聞かせてあげるわ。<line-break>私がなぜ闇にとりつかれたのか。<end>"
EN: "Heh... I'll tell you how the darkness came to possess me.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 들려주지. 내가 왜 어둠에 사로잡혔는지.<end>"

## 1140007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec114-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれは、まだディンガルが、<line-break>魔王バロルに支配されていたころ、<line-break>妖術宰相ゾフォルはこう予言した…。<line-break>ザギヴがディンガルを支配する<line-break>…とね。<end>"
EN: "Back when Dyneskal was still under the rule of Balor, the Demon King, Zofor, the Sorcerous Chancellor, made a prophecy: that Zagiv would rule Dyneskal.<end>"
KO: "그건 아직 딩갈이 마왕 발로르에게 지배받던 시절, 요술재상 조포르는 이렇게 예언했어…. 자기브가 딩갈을 지배할 것이라고….<end>"

## 1140008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec114-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それで、私の家族は魔王バロルに<line-break>捕らえられて…破壊神の円卓騎士<line-break>マゴスの餌食に…………。<line-break>マゴスには魔法も…打撃も…<line-break>効かなかった。<end>"
EN: "Because of that, my family was captured by Balor, the Demon King... and fell prey to Magos, a Knight of the Round Table serving the God of Destruction. Neither magic nor physical blows had any effect on Magos.<end>"
KO: "그래서 내 가족은 마왕 발로르에게 붙잡혀… 파괴신의 원탁기사 마고스의 먹이가 됐어… 마고스에겐 마법도… 타격도… 통하지 않았어.<end>"

## 1140009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec114-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の目の前で…家族が、私の家族が…<line-break>なぶり殺されて…いったわ。<line-break>私が…ザギヴ…だったばっかりに…。<line-break>あのとき…、<line-break>ネメア様が助けに来てくださって、<line-break>私だけ…助かった…。<end>"
EN: "Before my very eyes... my family, my own family... were tortured to death. All because I... was Zagiv. Then... Nemea came to the rescue, and I alone... survived.<end>"
KO: "내 눈앞에서… 가족이, 내 가족이… 잔혹하게 죽어 갔어. 내가… 자기브였기 때문에…. 그때… 네메아 님이 구하러 와 주셔서 나만… 살아남았어….<end>"

## 1140010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec114-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私…だけ…。<end>"
EN: "Only me...<end>"
KO: "나…만….<end>"

## 1140011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec114-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マゴスはその時、私の中に潜んだの。<line-break>それ以来、私の中にはマゴスが<line-break>棲み続けているわ。<end>"
EN: "That was when Magos hid inside me. Magos has dwelled within me ever since.<end>"
KO: "마고스는 그때 내 안에 숨어들었어. 그 뒤로 줄곧 내 안에는 마고스가 살고 있어.<end>"

## 1140012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec114-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マゴスは死んでいない。<line-break>私の中に逃げ込んで、生きている。<line-break>でも、ネメア様は私を殺さなかった…。<end>"
EN: "Magos isn't dead. Magos fled into me and is still alive there. But Nemea didn't kill me...<end>"
KO: "마고스는 죽지 않았어. 내 안으로 도망쳐 살아 있어. 하지만 네메아 님은 나를 죽이지 않았어….<end>"

## 1140013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec114-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇に巣くわれていようと、<line-break>心までは闇に堕ちていないなら<line-break>戦え。生き続けろって…。<end>"
EN: "He told me that even if darkness dwelled within me, as long as my heart had not fallen to it, I must fight. I must go on living.<end>"
KO: "어둠이 깃들어 있어도 마음까지 어둠에 떨어지지 않았다면 싸워. 계속 살아가라고….<end>"

## 1140014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec114-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ…。ごめんなさい。<line-break>こんな話を聞きに来たんじゃないわね。<line-break>闇の怪物退治、ありがとう。<line-break>これは私からの気持ちよ。<line-break>受け取っておいて。<end>"
EN: "Heh... I'm sorry. You didn't come here to listen to all this. Thank you for hunting those creatures of darkness. This is a token of my gratitude. Please, take it.<end>"
KO: "후후후…. 미안해. 이런 이야기를 들으러 온 건 아니지. 어둠의 괴물을 퇴치해 줘서 고마워. 이건 내 마음이야. 받아 둬.<end>"

## 1140015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec114-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くだらない話を<line-break>聞いてくれて、ありがとう。<line-break>　<line-break>少し楽になったわ。<end>"
EN: "Thank you for listening to my foolish story. I feel a little better now.<end>"
KO: "시답잖은 얘기 들어줘서 고마워. 조금은 마음이 편해졌어.<end>"

## 1140016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec114-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい。<line-break>今日はもう相手をできないわ。<line-break>政務がたくさん残ってるの。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry, but I can't spend any more time with you today. I still have so much state business to attend to.<end>"
KO: "미안해요. 오늘은 더 상대해 줄 수 없어요. 정무가 많이 남았거든요.<end>"

## 1140017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec114-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの話を聞いて<line-break>まだここに来るなんて…。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ふふふ、変な人ね。<line-break>うれしいけど、複雑な気分だわ。<end>"
EN: "Even after hearing all that, you still came... Heh. You are a strange one. I'm glad, though I don't quite know how to feel.<end>"
KO: "그 이야기를 듣고도 여기 오다니…. 후후, 이상한 사람이네. 기쁘긴 하지만 복잡한 기분이야.<end>"

## 1140018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec114-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私、いつまで<line-break>この闇を抑えておけるのかしら？　<line-break>　<line-break>もし、闇に飲まれてしまったら…。<line-break>その時は、きっとネメア様が<line-break>楽にしてくれるわよね。<end>"
EN: "How much longer can I keep this darkness at bay? If it consumes me... then Lord Nemea will surely put me out of my misery, won't he?<end>"
KO: "나는 언제까지 이 어둠을 억누를 수 있을까? 만약 어둠에 삼켜진다면… 그때는 분명 네메아 님이 편하게 해 주겠지.<end>"

## 1140019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec114-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エリュマルク帝のように…。　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>それとも、あなたがしてくれる？<line-break>ふふふ。それも悪くないわね。<end>"
EN: "Just as he did for Emperor Elmark... Or would you do it for me? Heh. That wouldn't be so bad either.<end>"
KO: "에류마르크 황제처럼…. 아니면 당신이 해 줄래? 후후후. 그것도 나쁘지 않겠네.<end>"

## 1140020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec114-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォルの予言は<line-break>成就しないですみそうね。<line-break>　<line-break>ネメア様がいれば<line-break>何もかも平気だわ。<line-break>…あなたもいるしね。<end>"
EN: "It seems Zofor's prophecy may never come to pass. With Lord Nemea here, everything will be all right. And you're here too.<end>"
KO: "조포르의 예언은 이루어지지 않을 것 같네. 네메아 님이 있으면 아무 걱정 없어. …당신도 있고.<end>"

## 1140021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec114-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんなところにいつまでもいないの。<line-break>他にやることがあるでしょ。<line-break>　<line-break>ふふふ、ほんとに変な人。<end>"
EN: "Don't stay in a place like this forever. You have other things to do, don't you? Hee hee... You really are a strange one.<end>"
KO: "이런 데 계속 있지 마. 다른 할 일이 있잖아. 후후후, 정말 이상한 사람이네.<end>"
