EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0A pleasure to meet you, young man.<end>A pleasure to meet you, kitten.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0처음 뵙겠군, 꼬마야.<end>처음 뵙겠어, 아가씨.<end>"

## 1170010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、この人は、カフィンです。<line-break>えと、で、こちらが…<end>"
EN: "Oh, this is Coffin. Um, and this is...<end>"
KO: "아, 이 사람은 카핀입니다. 어, 그리고 이쪽이….<end>"

## 1170011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はレイヴン。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>…よろしく。<end>"
EN: "I'm Raven... Good to meet you.<end>"
KO: "나는 레이븐. …잘 부탁해.<end>"

## 1170012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、えと、その…<end>"
EN: "Oh, um, well...<end>"
KO: "아, 저기, 그게…<end>"

## 1170013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう、それから、<line-break>こっちにいるのがナーシェスです。<end>"
EN: "Right, and over here is Narscess.<end>"
KO: "그래, 그리고 여기 있는 사람이 나셰스입니다.<end>"

## 1170014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ。<line-break>…よろしく。<end>"
EN: "Heh. A pleasure.<end>"
KO: "후후. …잘 부탁해.<end>"

## 1170015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、ノエル。もう用は済んだ。<line-break>そろそろ、行くとしよう。<end>"
EN: "Come, Noel. Our business here is done. It's time we were going.<end>"
KO: "자, 노엘. 이제 볼일은 끝났다. 슬슬 가자.<end>"

## 1170016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、失礼する。<line-break>…無限のソウルを持つ者、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Then we shall take our leave... <value:$28>, Bearer of the Infinite Soul.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 실례하겠다. …무한의 소울을 지닌 자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1170017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まったく、無愛想な男よね。<end>"
EN: "Honestly, what an unsociable man.<end>"
KO: "정말 무뚝뚝한 남자네.<end>"

## 1170018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えと、カフィンも、<line-break>レイヴンも、ナーシェスも、<line-break>とても腕が立つんですよ。<line-break>だから、わたし、<line-break>みんなが一緒に旅をしてくれて、<line-break>すごく心強いんです。<end>"
EN: "Um, Coffin, Raven, and Narscess are all very skilled. Having them travel with me is such a comfort.<end>"
KO: "저, 카핀도 레이븐도 나셰스도 아주 실력이 좋아요. 그래서 모두가 함께 여행해 줘서 정말 든든해요.<end>"

## 1170019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル、そろそろ行こう。<end>"
EN: "Noel, it's time to go.<end>"
KO: "노엘, 이제 가자.<end>"

## 1170020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、そうですね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, right.<end>"
KO: "아, 그렇네요.<end>"

## 1170021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃあ、<line-break><value:$28>さん。<line-break>わたしたち、もう行きます。<line-break>あの、<value:$28>さんも<line-break>がんばってくださいね。<end>"
EN: "Well, <value:$28>, we should be going now. And please keep doing your best, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그럼 <value:$28>님. 저희는 이제 가겠습니다. 저기, <value:$28>님도 힘내세요.<end>"

## 1170022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えと、わたしもがんばりますから、<line-break>それじゃあ、失礼します！<end>"
EN: "Um, I'll do my best too. Goodbye!<end>"
KO: "저, 저도 열심히 할 테니까요. 그럼 실례하겠습니다!<end>"

## 1170023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、ノエル。<line-break>…フフ、しょうがない子ね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, Noel. Heh, what are we going to do with you?<end>"
KO: "아, 노엘. …후후, 어쩔 수 없는 아이네.<end>"

## 1170024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0それじゃあ、ボウヤ。<line-break>またどこかで会いましょ。<end>それじゃあ、子猫ちゃん。<line-break>またどこかで会いましょ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Well then, young man. I hope we meet again somewhere.<end>Well then, kitten. I hope we meet again somewhere.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0그럼, 꼬마야. 또 어디선가 만나자.<end>그럼, 아기 고양이야. 또 어디선가 만나자.<end>"

## 1170025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失礼するよ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Farewell, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "실례하지, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1170026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…お、お前たち…。<end>"
EN: "Y-you...<end>"
KO: "…너, 너희들….<end>"

## 1170027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何かしら？<line-break>あたしたちにご用？<end>"
EN: "What is it? Did you want something from us?<end>"
KO: "무슨 일이야? 우리한테 볼일 있어?<end>"

## 1170028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいさつもなしに刃物を向ける<line-break>なんて感心しないわ。<line-break>もてないわよ、そういうの。<end>"
EN: "Pointing a blade at someone without so much as a hello? That's hardly charming. You'll never be popular that way.<end>"
KO: "인사도 없이 칼부터 겨누는 건 감탄할 일이 아니네. 그러면 인기 없어.<end>"

## 1170029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聞く耳もたぬって感じね。<line-break>…強引な男って嫌いじゃないけど<line-break>こういうのはいい気がしないわね。<line-break>ちょっと、痛い目に<line-break>あってもらうしかないかしら？<end>"
EN: "I don't think they're in the mood to listen. I don't mind a forceful man, but this is a bit much. Perhaps they need to be taught a painful little lesson?<end>"
KO: "들을 생각이 전혀 없는 것 같네. …강압적인 남자가 싫은 건 아니지만 이런 건 기분이 좋지 않아. 조금 아픈 꼴을 보게 해줄 수밖에 없으려나?<end>"

## 1170030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>씨!<end>"

## 1170031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あ、<value:$28>さん！<end>"
EN: "Oh! <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "아, 아, <value:$28> 씨!<end>"

## 1170032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、えと、また助けていただきました。<line-break>あの…本当にありがとうございます！<end>"
EN: "Oh, um... You've saved me again. Thank you so much!<end>"
KO: "아, 저기, 또 도움을 받았네요. 그… 정말 감사합니다!<end>"

## 1170033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無事か？<end>"
EN: "Are you all right?<end>"
KO: "무사한가?<end>"

## 1170034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？<line-break>ほう…これは…。<end>"
EN: "Hm? Well now... This is...<end>"
KO: "응? 호오… 이건….<end>"

## 1170035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと、ナーシェス！<line-break>あんた、どこに行ってたのよ！？<end>"
EN: "Narscess! Where have you been!?<end>"
KO: "잠깐, 나셰스! 너 어디 갔다 온 거야!?<end>"

## 1170036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまなかったな。<line-break>…それで、襲ってきた連中の<line-break>目星はついたのか？<end>"
EN: "Sorry about that. So, do you have any idea who these attackers are?<end>"
KO: "미안했어. …그래서 습격해 온 녀석들은 짐작이 가나?<end>"

## 1170037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これを見て。<line-break>みんなそろって刺青をしてるわ。<line-break>イヤねぇ…。<end>"
EN: "Look at this. Every one of them has a tattoo. I don't like this...<end>"
KO: "이걸 봐. 다들 문신을 하고 있어. 싫다….<end>"

## 1170038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふむ、どうやら<line-break>この一件は施文院が<line-break>絡んでいるようだな。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. It seems the Inscription Order is involved in this affair.<end>"
KO: "흠, 아무래도 이번 일에는 시문원이 얽혀 있는 것 같군.<end>"

## 1170039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "施文院ですって？<line-break>…あたしたち、<line-break>厄介なのに狙われてるわね。<end>"
EN: "The Inscription Order? We've caught the attention of a troublesome lot.<end>"
KO: "시문원이라고? …우리, 귀찮은 녀석들에게 찍혔네.<end>"

## 1170041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えと、…せもんいん？<end>"
EN: "Um... the Inscription Order?<end>"
KO: "저기, …세몬인?<end>"

## 1170042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "施文院とは、天空神ノトゥーンに　<line-break>仕える宗派のひとつだ。<line-break>　<line-break>もともとは、<line-break>各地を巡って邪悪なものを<line-break>封印していたのだ。<end>"
EN: "The Inscription Order is one of the sects devoted to Notun, the Sky God. Originally, its members traveled the lands sealing away evil.<end>"
KO: "시문원은 천공신 노툰을 섬기는 종파 중 하나다. 원래는 각지를 돌아다니며 사악한 것을 봉인하던 자들이지.<end>"

## 1170043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "中世には神聖王サラミスによって<line-break>異端とされ、<line-break>ほとんどの宗徒が処刑された…。<line-break>だが、その生き残りは地下へ潜って<line-break>暗殺集団・告死天使を組織し、<line-break>歴史の裏舞台で暗躍を続けた。<end>"
EN: "In the Middle Ages, the Holy King Salamis branded them heretics, and nearly all their followers were executed. But the survivors went underground and formed an order of assassins known as the Angels of Death. They have continued to work from the shadows of history ever since.<end>"
KO: "중세에는 성왕 살라미스에게 이단으로 규정되어 대부분의 신자가 처형됐다…. 하지만 살아남은 자들은 지하로 숨어 암살집단 고사천사를 조직하고 역사의 뒤편에서 계속 암약했다.<end>"

## 1170044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、現在もな…。<end>"
EN: "And they remain active to this day...<end>"
KO: "그리고 지금도 말이지….<end>"

## 1170045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、この人たちは…。<end>"
EN: "Then these people are...<end>"
KO: "그럼 이 사람들은….<end>"

## 1170046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "間違いない。<line-break>施文院の暗殺者、告死天使だ。<end>"
EN: "There is no doubt. They are the Inscription Order's assassins: the Angels of Death.<end>"
KO: "틀림없어. 시문원의 암살자, 고사천사다.<end>"

## 1170047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0こんな、物騒な連中に<line-break>ねらわれるようなこと…<line-break>したかしら？<line-break>でも、ボウヤと再会できたのは<line-break>幸運だったわ…。<line-break>フフフッ。<end>こんな、物騒な連中に<line-break>ねらわれるようなこと…<line-break>したかしら？<line-break>でも、子猫ちゃんと再会できたのは<line-break>幸運だったわ…。<line-break>フフフッ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0What could we have done to make such a dangerous lot come after us? Still, I was lucky to see you again, young man... Hee hee.<end>What could we have done to make such a dangerous lot come after us? Still, I was lucky to see you again, kitten... Hee hee.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0이런 험악한 녀석들에게 노려질 만한 짓을… 내가 했던가? 그래도 꼬마와 다시 만난 건 행운이었어…. 후후후.<end>이런 험악한 녀석들에게 노려질 만한 짓을… 내가 했던가? 그래도 아기고양이와 다시 만난 건 행운이었어…. 후후후.<end>"

## 1170048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…とにかく、この場を離れよう。<line-break>奴らの連れがまだ<line-break>近くにいるかもしれない。<end>"
EN: "Whatever the case, we should leave. More of them may still be nearby.<end>"
KO: "…아무튼 이곳을 벗어나자. 놈들의 동료가 아직 근처에 있을지도 몰라.<end>"

## 1170049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、その通りだな。<line-break>では、退散するとしようか。<end>"
EN: "Heh, quite right. Let us make our retreat.<end>"
KO: "후후, 맞는 말이군. 그럼 물러나도록 하지.<end>"

## 1170050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日は助けてくれて、ありがと。<line-break>戦っているときの真剣な顔…<line-break>フフフ…、かわいかったわよ。<end>"
EN: "Thanks for saving us today. That serious look on your face while you were fighting... Hee hee. It was adorable.<end>"
KO: "오늘 도와줘서 고마워. 싸울 때의 진지한 얼굴… 후후후… 귀여웠어.<end>"

## 1170051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの、それじゃ、<line-break>わたしはこれで失礼します！<end>"
EN: "U-um, I'll be going, then!<end>"
KO: "저, 저기, 그럼 저는 이만 실례하겠습니다!<end>"

## 1170052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "物騒な世の中になったものね。　<line-break>こんなところで、<line-break>白昼堂々襲ってくるなんて。<line-break>いったい、誰がお目当てか<line-break>知らないけど、<line-break>いいかげんにしてもらいたいわね。<end>"
EN: "What a dangerous world it's become. Attacking us here in broad daylight... I don't know who they're after, but I've had quite enough of this.<end>"
KO: "험한 세상이 됐네. 이런 곳에서 대낮에 대놓고 습격하다니. 대체 누가 목표인지는 모르겠지만 이제 좀 그만했으면 좋겠어.<end>"

## 1170054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと、レイヴン。<line-break>あんた、何びびってんのよ？<end>"
EN: "Hey, Raven. What are you so scared of?<end>"
KO: "잠깐, 레이븐. 너 뭐가 그렇게 겁나?<end>"

## 1170055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "い、いや、<line-break>そんなことは…ない。<end>"
EN: "I-I'm not scared.<end>"
KO: "아, 아니, 그런 건… 아니야.<end>"

## 1170056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ！<line-break><value:$28>さん！<end>"
EN: "Oh! <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "아! <value:$28> 씨!<end>"

## 1170057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>さん、<line-break>ありがとうございます！<line-break>また助けてもらっちゃいました。<line-break>わたしも、一日も早く<line-break><value:$28>さんみたいな<line-break>一人前の冒険者になりたいな…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>, thank you! You've rescued me again. I hope I can become a full-fledged adventurer like <value:$28> as soon as possible.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28> 씨, 감사합니다! 또 도움을 받았네요. 저도 하루빨리 <value:$28> 씨 같은 어엿한 모험자가 되고 싶어요….<end>"

## 1170058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふむ…また施文院の連中か？<end>"
EN: "Hmm... The Inscription Order again?<end>"
KO: "흠… 또 시문원 놈들인가?<end>"

## 1170059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ、間違いないわ。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>…で、ナーシェス。あんた<line-break>どうして、こういうときに限って、<line-break>いつもいないのよ？<end>"
EN: "Yes, no question about it. And Narscess, why are you never around when something like this happens?<end>"
KO: "그래, 틀림없어. …그런데 나셰스. 넌 왜 꼭 이럴 때마다 항상 없는 거야?<end>"

## 1170060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言いがかりはよしてもらおう。<line-break>お前たちが、いつまでたっても<line-break>来ないから、見に来たまでだ。<end>"
EN: "Enough of these accusations. You were taking so long that I merely came to see what was keeping you.<end>"
KO: "억지는 그만둬. 너희가 언제까지고 오지 않으니까 보러 왔을 뿐이야.<end>"

## 1170061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの、でも、どうして、<line-break>わたしたちを、ねらうのかな…。<end>"
EN: "Um, but... why would anyone be after us?<end>"
KO: "아, 하지만 왜 우리를 노리는 걸까….<end>"

## 1170062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたしは、告死天使の親玉を<line-break>たぶらかした覚えはないわよ。<end>"
EN: "I don't remember seducing the leader of the Angels of Death.<end>"
KO: "나는 고사천사의 우두머리를 꼬신 기억은 없어.<end>"

## 1170063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、お前の色気に<line-break>ひっかかる程度の相手なら、<line-break>ありがたいのだがな。<end>"
EN: "If this foe were susceptible to your charms, we would be fortunate indeed.<end>"
KO: "그래, 네 색기에 넘어갈 정도의 상대라면 고맙겠지만.<end>"

## 1170064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…どういう意味？<end>"
EN: "What's that supposed to mean?<end>"
KO: "…무슨 뜻이야?<end>"

## 1170066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、ノエルが望むなら、<line-break>今度はあたしの美貌で、　　　　<line-break>問いただしてあげるわ。<line-break>さぁ、こんな物騒なところからは<line-break>早く離れましょ。<end>"
EN: "Well, if Noel wants to know, next time I'll use my beauty to make them talk. For now, let's get out of this dangerous place.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 노엘이 원한다면 다음엔 내 미모로 캐물어 줄게. 자, 이런 험한 곳에서는 빨리 벗어나자.<end>"

## 1170067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そうだな…。<end>"
EN: "Y-Yes, let's.<end>"
KO: "그, 그렇군….<end>"

## 1170068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0ボウヤ、今回もありがと。<line-break>お礼はそのうち、させてもらうから<line-break>期待しててね？<end>子猫ちゃん、今回もありがと。<line-break>お礼はそのうち、させてもらうから<line-break>期待しててね？<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Thanks again, young man. I'll find a way to reward you one of these days, so you have that to look forward to.<end>Thanks again, kitten. I'll find a way to reward you one of these days, so you have that to look forward to.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0꼬마야, 이번에도 고마워. 답례는 나중에 해 줄 테니 기대해도 좋아.<end>아기 고양이, 이번에도 고마워. 답례는 나중에 해 줄 테니 기대해도 좋아.<end>"

## 1170069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えと…、<value:$28>さん、　　　<line-break>ありがとうございました！<line-break>　<line-break>わたしも<value:$28>さんに<line-break>負けないくらい、強くなりますから！<end>"
EN: "Um... thank you, <value:$28>! I'll become every bit as strong as <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "저기… <value:$28>님, 감사합니다! 저도 <value:$28>님에게 지지 않을 만큼 강해질게요!<end>"

## 1170070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec117-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "予備メッセージだ。<line-break>ノエル２／Ｖ２の１番目。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "예비 메시지다. 노엘 2/V2의 첫 번째.<end>"

## 1170071

## 1180002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "時間がないわ。<line-break>今すぐ猫屋敷へ行って。<end>"
EN: "There's no time. Go to Cat Manor, now.<end>"
KO: "시간이 없어. 지금 당장 고양이 저택으로 가.<end>"

## 1180003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "父さんが危ないの。<line-break>早く猫屋敷へ！<end>"
EN: "Father's in danger. Hurry to Cat Manor!<end>"
KO: "아버지가 위험해. 빨리 고양이 저택으로!<end>"

## 1180004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "兄さんが…、ネメアが<line-break>新たな神器のありかを嗅ぎつけたわ。<line-break>　<line-break>闇の神器、魂吸いの指輪…。<line-break>それは猫屋敷にあるの。<line-break>ネメアは猫屋敷へ向かっているわ。<end>"
EN: "My brother... Nemea has caught wind of another divine artifact. The Dark Artifact known as the Soul-Draining Ring... It's in Cat Manor, and Nemea is on his way there.<end>"
KO: "오빠가… 네메아가 새로운 신기의 위치를 알아냈어. 어둠의 신기, 영혼흡수의 반지…. 그건 고양이 저택에 있어. 네메아는 그곳으로 향하고 있어.<end>"

## 1180005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たとえ父さんでも、　　　　　　　<line-break>ひとりでは兄さんに勝てない。<line-break>　<line-break>あなたの力が必要なの。<line-break>無限のソウルを持つあなたには、<line-break>兄さんを倒せる可能性があるのよ。<end>"
EN: "Even Father can't defeat my brother alone. We need your strength. With the Infinite Soul, you may stand a chance against him.<end>"
KO: "아버지라도 혼자서는 오빠를 이길 수 없어. 네 힘이 필요해. 무한의 소울을 가진 너라면 오빠를 쓰러뜨릴 가능성이 있어.<end>"

## 1180006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今すぐ猫屋敷へ急いで。　　　<line-break>時間がないわ。<line-break>　<line-break>ネメアが新たな神器のありかを<line-break>嗅ぎつけたのよ。<end>"
EN: "Hurry to Cat Manor, now. There's no time. Nemea has caught wind of another divine artifact.<end>"
KO: "지금 당장 고양이 저택으로 서둘러. 시간이 없어. 네메아가 새로운 신기의 위치를 알아냈어.<end>"

## 1180007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "猫屋敷にある魂吸いの指輪よ。<line-break>ネメアは猫屋敷へ向かっているわ。<end>"
EN: "The Soul-Draining Ring in Cat Manor. Nemea is on his way there.<end>"
KO: "고양이 저택에 있는 영혼흡수 반지야. 네메아는 고양이 저택으로 향하고 있어.<end>"

## 1180008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "父さんの命が危険なの。<line-break>早く猫屋敷へ！<end>"
EN: "Father's life is in danger. Hurry to Cat Manor!<end>"
KO: "아버지 목숨이 위험해. 빨리 고양이 저택으로!<end>"

## 1180009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウスは…、<line-break>あたしの育ての父なのよ。<end>"
EN: "Orphaus... is my foster father.<end>"
KO: "오르파우스는… 나를 키워 준 아버지야.<end>"

## 1180010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は、エルフと人間の間に生まれ、<line-break>すぐに森に捨てられたらしいわ。<line-break>　<line-break>そして、オルファウスに拾われ、<line-break>育てられた。<end>"
EN: "I was born to an Elf and a human, and apparently abandoned in the forest soon afterward. Orphaus found me there and raised me.<end>"
KO: "나는 엘프와 인간 사이에서 태어나 곧 숲에 버려졌다고 해. 그리고 오르파우스에게 발견되어 자랐어.<end>"

## 1180011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメアも<line-break>オルファウスに育てられたの。<line-break>私と一緒に。<end>"
EN: "Orphaus raised Nemea too. We grew up together.<end>"
KO: "네메아도 오르파우스에게 길러졌어. 나와 함께.<end>"

## 1180012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かつて、魔王バロルの娘エスリンは、<line-break>破壊神の円卓騎士バルザーの子を<line-break>身ごもった。<line-break>けれど、魔王バロルは、<line-break>エスリンの首をかき切って<line-break>森に捨てたわ。<end>"
EN: "Long ago, Ethlinn, daughter of Balor, Demon King, conceived a child by Baltzer, a Knight of the Round Table serving the God of Destruction. But Balor slit Ethlinn's throat and left the body in the forest.<end>"
KO: "옛날 마왕 발로르의 딸 에스린은 파괴신의 원탁기사 발자의 아이를 임신했어. 하지만 마왕 발로르는 에스린의 목을 베어 숲에 버렸지.<end>"

## 1180013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バロルの娘の子がバロルを滅ぼす<line-break>という妖術宰相ゾフォルの予言を<line-break>真に受けてね。<line-break>父さんは森でエスリンの屍を見つけ<line-break>そのお腹の子を救ったの。<end>"
EN: "He took the Sorcerous Chancellor Zofor's prophecy to heart: a child born to Balor's daughter would destroy Balor. Father found Ethlinn's body in the forest and saved the child in the womb.<end>"
KO: "발로르의 딸이 낳은 아이가 발로르를 멸망시킨다는 요술재상 조포르의 예언을 진짜로 믿은 거야. 아버지는 숲에서 에슬린의 시신을 발견하고 그 뱃속의 아이를 구해냈어.<end>"

## 1180014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…その赤ん坊がネメアよ。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>そして、私とネメアは兄妹として<line-break>育てられた…。でも、今の兄さんは、<line-break>もう昔の兄さんではないわ。<end>"
EN: "That child was Nemea. Nemea and I were raised as brother and sister... But my brother isn't the man he once was.<end>"
KO: "…그 아기가 네메아야. 그리고 나와 네메아는 남매로 자랐어…. 하지만 지금 오빠는 더 이상 예전의 오빠가 아니야.<end>"

## 1180015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お願い！<line-break>兄さんをとめて！<end>"
EN: "Please! Stop my brother!<end>"
KO: "부탁해! 오빠를 막아 줘!<end>"

## 1180016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は、ケリュネイア。<line-break>あなたに闇の神器探索を依頼した者よ。<end>"
EN: "I'm Ceryneia. I'm the one who asked you to search for the Dark Artifacts.<end>"
KO: "나는 케류네이아. 네게 어둠의 신기 수색을 의뢰한 사람이야.<end>"

## 1180017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメアも闇の神器を狙っていることは、<line-break>もう知っているかしら？<line-break>ネメアは、次の神器を見つけたのよ。<line-break>場所は、猫屋敷。<line-break>ねらいは、オルファウスが所有する<line-break>魂吸いの指輪。<end>"
EN: "Do you already know that Nemea is after the Dark Artifacts too? He's found the next one. It's at Cat Manor: the Soul-Draining Ring in Orphaus's possession.<end>"
KO: "네메아도 어둠의 신기를 노리고 있다는 건 알고 있겠지? 네메아는 다음 신기를 찾아냈어. 장소는 고양이 저택. 노리는 건 오르파우스가 가진 영혼흡수의 반지야.<end>"

## 1180018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おそらく、ネメアは、　　　　　　<line-break>オルファウスを殺してでも、<line-break>手に入れようとするわ。<line-break>オルファウスには、<line-break>あなたも世話になっているわよね？<line-break>これから助けに行ってほしいのよ。<end>"
EN: "Nemea will probably try to take it even if he has to kill Orphaus. Orphaus has helped you too, hasn't he? I want you to go help him now.<end>"
KO: "아마 네메아는 오르파우스를 죽여서라도 손에 넣으려 할 거야. 오르파우스에게는 너도 신세를 졌지? 이제 구하러 가 줬으면 해.<end>"

## 1180019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんとしても行ってもらうわ！<line-break>父さんの危機を、黙って見ている<line-break>わけにはいかないから。<line-break>兄さんを倒せる可能性のある<line-break>無限のソウルの持ち主を<line-break>なんとしてでも連れて行く！<end>"
EN: "You're going, whatever it takes! I can't stand by while Father is in danger. You possess the Infinite Soul, and you may be able to defeat my brother. I'll get you there, no matter what!<end>"
KO: "무슨 수를 써서라도 데려갈 거야! 아버지의 위기를 가만히 보고 있을 순 없으니까. 오빠를 쓰러뜨릴 가능성이 있는 무한의 소울을 지닌 자를 어떻게든 데려가겠어!<end>"

## 1180020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここまでムキになる理由？<line-break>それを話せば、<line-break>来てくれるのかしら？<end>"
EN: "You want to know why I'm so worked up about this? If I tell you, will you come?<end>"
KO: "내가 이렇게까지 열을 올리는 이유? 그걸 말하면 와 줄 거야?<end>"

## 1180021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウスは…、<line-break>あたしの育ての父なのよ。<end>"
EN: "Orphaus... is my foster father.<end>"
KO: "오르파우스는… 나를 키워 준 아버지야.<end>"

## 1180028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃあ、テレポートで、　　<line-break>猫屋敷まで送るわ。<line-break>　<line-break>この人数で飛ぶとなると、<line-break>魔力を使い果たしちゃうけど…<line-break>仕方ないわね。<end>"
EN: "Then I'll teleport all of you to Cat Manor. Taking this many people will use up all my magic, but... there's no other choice.<end>"
KO: "그럼 텔레포트로 고양이 저택까지 보내줄게. 이 인원으로 날아가면 마력을 다 써버리겠지만… 어쩔 수 없네.<end>"

## 1180029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、行くわよ！<end>"
EN: "All right, here we go!<end>"
KO: "그럼, 간다!<end>"

## 1180030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はぁ、はぁ…。<line-break>さすがに…きついわね…。<end>"
EN: "Hah... hah... This is... rough...<end>"
KO: "하아, 하아…. 역시… 힘드네….<end>"

## 1180031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（この気配は…！）<end>"
EN: "(That presence...!)<end>"
KO: "(이 기척은…!)<end>"

## 1180032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この気は…！<line-break>ネメア！！<end>"
EN: "That presence...! Nemea!!<end>"
KO: "이 기운은…! 네메아!!<end>"

## 1180033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>3む、この闘気は…。<line-break>ネメア様か…！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>4な、なんか…、<line-break>すごい圧力を感じるかな。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>5お、おい！<line-break>なんかすげぇ圧力を感じないか！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>6な、なんだろう…。<line-break>気のせいじゃないよね。<line-break>ものすごい圧力を感じるよ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>13この威圧感…！<line-break>いったい誰だ！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>10ね、姉ちゃん！<line-break>すごい圧力を感じるよ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>14むむ。<line-break>なんじゃ、この圧倒的な気配は…！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>15ねえ、<value:$28>。<line-break>なんかすごい威圧感がしない？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>17な、な、なんだっていうの！<line-break>この不愉快な圧力は！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19ねえ…、<line-break>なんかすごい威圧感だよぉ、<line-break><value:$28>。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>11こ…この闘気は…<line-break>ネメア様…？！<end><if><value:$01><equal>21この威圧感…！<line-break>いったい…！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>23こ…この闘気は…<line-break>ネメア様か…？！<end><if><value:$01><equal>27この威圧感…！<line-break>いったい誰だ！？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>26なんじゃ、この圧倒的な気配は…！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>24すごい威圧感…。<line-break>いったい…誰？<end><if><value:$01><equal>25なんかすごい威圧感…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>192………………！<line-break>あ、圧倒的な…力…！<end><if><value:$01><equal>193<value:$28>さん…！<line-break>す、すごい威圧感です…！<line-break>わ、私、怖い！　怖いです！<end><if><value:$01><equal>194こ、この圧倒的な気配は…！<line-break>間違いない！<line-break>…奴だ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>191す、すごい威圧感ね。<line-break>いったい誰なの！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>190凄まじい闘気だ！<line-break>ネメアなのか！？<end>ぼ、ぼくの気のせいかな…？<line-break>すごい威圧感があるんだけど…。 <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>3Hm, this fighting spirit... Could it be Nemea...?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4I-I think I feel some kind of... incredible pressure.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5H-hey! You feel that incredible pressure too?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6W-what is that...? I'm not imagining it, am I? That pressure is incredible!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13That oppressive presence...! Who is it?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>10S-sis! I feel this incredible pressure!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Hmm. What is this overwhelming presence...?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Hey, <value:$28>. Don't you feel that oppressive presence?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17W-what is this?! This awful pressure!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Hey... can you feel that? It's really intimidating, <value:$28>...<end><if><value:$01><equal>11Th-this fighting spirit... Nemea...?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>21That oppressive presence...! What in the...?<end><if><value:$01><equal>23Th-this fighting spirit... Could it be Nemea...?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27That oppressive presence...! Who is it?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26What is this overwhelming presence...?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>24What an oppressive presence... Who... is it?<end><if><value:$01><equal>25There's something really intimidating here...<end><if><value:$01><equal>192...! S-such overwhelming... power...!<end><if><value:$01><equal>193<value:$28>...! Th-this presence... it's overwhelming! I'm scared! I'm scared!<end><if><value:$01><equal>194Th-this overwhelming presence...! No mistake! It's... him.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191S-such an intimidating presence. Who is it?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190What tremendous fighting spirit! Is that Nemea?!<end>M-maybe it's just me...? But I can feel this oppressive presence...<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>3음, 이 투기는…. 네메아 님인가…!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4뭐, 뭔가… 엄청난 압박감이 느껴질지도.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5어, 어이! 뭔가 엄청난 압박감이 느껴지지 않아!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>6뭐, 뭐지…. 기분 탓이 아니야. 엄청난 압박감이 느껴져!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13이 위압감…! 대체 누구지!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누, 누나! 엄청난 압박감이 느껴져!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14으음. 뭐냐, 이 압도적인 기척은…!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15저기, <value:$28>. 뭔가 엄청난 위압감이 느껴지지 않아?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17뭐, 뭐, 뭐야! 이 불쾌한 압박감은!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19저기… 뭔가 엄청난 위압감이야아, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11이… 이 투기는… 네메아 님…?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>21이 위압감…! 대체…!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>23이… 이 투기는… 네메아 님인가…?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27이 위압감…! 대체 누구지!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26뭐냐, 이 압도적인 기척은…!<end><if><value:$01><equal>24엄청난 위압감…. 대체… 누구지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>25뭔가 엄청난 위압감….<end><if><value:$01><equal>192………………! 아, 압도적인… 힘…!<end><if><value:$01><equal>193<value:$28> 씨…! 어, 엄청난 위압감이에요…! 저, 저 무서워요! 무서워요!<end><if><value:$01><equal>194이, 이 압도적인 기척은…! 틀림없어! …녀석이다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191어, 엄청난 위압감이네. 대체 누구야!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>190엄청난 투기다! 네메아인가!?<end>내, 내 기분 탓인가…? 엄청난 위압감이 느껴지는데….<end>"

## 1180034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "に、兄さんだわ…！<line-break>早く行って、<line-break>父さんを助けて！<line-break>あたしも…<line-break>すぐに行くから…！<end>"
EN: "I-it's my brother...! Hurry, go help Father! I'll... be there soon...!<end>"
KO: "오, 오빠야…! 빨리 가서 아버지를 도와줘! 나도… 곧 갈 테니까…!<end>"

## 1180035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あたしもすぐに行くから…<line-break>早く…！<line-break>先に行って！<end>"
EN: "I-I'll be right behind you... Hurry! Go on ahead!<end>"
KO: "나, 나도 곧 갈 테니까… 빨리…! 먼저 가!<end>"

## 1180036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "父よ、久しいな。<line-break>闇の神器をもらいうけに来た。<end>"
EN: "Father. It has been too long. I have come to claim the Dark Artifact.<end>"
KO: "아버지여, 오랜만이군. 어둠의 신기를 받으러 왔다.<end>"

## 1180037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おやおや。　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>久しぶりに帰ってきたと思ったら、<line-break>さっそくおねだりですか？<line-break>おみやげを持ってくるくらいが<line-break>普通なんですが…<line-break>育て方を間違えましたかねえ？<end>"
EN: "Well, well. You finally come home after all this time, and the first thing you do is ask me for something? Most sons would at least bring a gift. Perhaps I raised you wrong.<end>"
KO: "이런 이런. 오랜만에 돌아왔나 했더니 벌써부터 조르는 겁니까? 보통은 선물이라도 가져오는 법인데…. 제가 잘못 키운 걸까요?<end>"

## 1180038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ところで、<line-break>お仲間はどうしたのですか？<end>"
EN: "By the way, what became of your companions?<end>"
KO: "그런데 동료분들은 어떻게 됐나요?<end>"

## 1180039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたとの戦いに　　　　　　<line-break>他の者の力を借りる気などない。<line-break>　<line-break>己の力のみで、決着をつける。<line-break>それがあなたへの礼儀だ。<line-break>我が父よ。<end>"
EN: "I have no intention of borrowing another's strength to fight you. I will settle this by my power alone. That is the respect I owe you, my father.<end>"
KO: "당신과의 싸움에 다른 이의 힘을 빌릴 생각은 없다. 오직 내 힘으로 결착을 짓겠다. 그것이 당신에 대한 예의다. 아버지여.<end>"

## 1180040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ、<line-break>やはり戦うつもりなのですか？<line-break>まったく困った息子だ…。<end>"
EN: "Dear me. So you truly mean to fight me? What a troublesome son you are.<end>"
KO: "이런, 역시 싸울 생각입니까? 정말 곤란한 아들이군….<end>"

## 1180041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや？　<value:$28>。<line-break>ダメじゃないですか、<line-break>こんな危ない場面に出てきたら。<end>"
EN: "Oh? <value:$28>. You really shouldn't be here when things are this dangerous.<end>"
KO: "어라? <value:$28>. 안 되잖아요, 이렇게 위험한 곳에 나오면.<end>"

## 1180042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まったくふたりとも…、<line-break>なんでこう私を困らせるのか。<end>"
EN: "Honestly, you two... Why must you both cause me so much trouble?<end>"
KO: "정말 둘 다…. 왜 이렇게 나를 곤란하게 하는 거야.<end>"

## 1180043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしても、<line-break>魂吸いの指輪が必要ですか？<end>"
EN: "Do you need the Soul-Draining Ring that badly?<end>"
KO: "꼭 영혼 흡수의 반지가 필요한가요?<end>"

## 1180044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか…。<line-break>覚悟に揺るぎはないようですね。<end>"
EN: "I see. It seems your resolve will not be shaken.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까…. 각오는 흔들리지 않는 모양이군요.<end>"

## 1180045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめて！<end>"
EN: "Stop!<end>"
KO: "그만해!<end>"

## 1180046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "兄さん！<line-break>もうバカな真似はやめて！<end>"
EN: "Brother! Stop doing such foolish things!<end>"
KO: "오빠! 이제 바보 같은 짓은 그만해!<end>"

## 1180047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ケリュネイア！<line-break>あなたまで来たのですか！？<end>"
EN: "Ceryneia! You came too?<end>"
KO: "케류네이아! 당신까지 온 겁니까!?<end>"

## 1180048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "父さんと戦ってまで、力がほしいの？<line-break>そんな力になんの意味があるの？<line-break>こんなの兄さんじゃないわ！！<end>"
EN: "You want power badly enough to fight Father for it? What good is power like that? You're not the brother I know!<end>"
KO: "아버지와 싸워서까지 힘이 갖고 싶은 거야? 그런 힘에 무슨 의미가 있어? 이런 건 오빠가 아니야!!<end>"

## 1180049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ケリュネイアよ。<line-break>お前が追っているのは、<line-break>過去の幻影だ。<end>"
EN: "Ceryneia. What you are chasing is only a phantom of the past.<end>"
KO: "케류네이아여. 네가 쫓고 있는 것은 과거의 환영이다.<end>"

## 1180050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "兄さん…。<line-break>…ならば、<line-break>私が目を覚まさせてあげるわ！<end>"
EN: "Brother... Then I'll make you come to your senses!<end>"
KO: "오빠…. …그렇다면 내가 정신을 차리게 해 줄게!<end>"

## 1180051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめなさい！<line-break>ケリュネイア！！<end>"
EN: "Stop, Ceryneia!<end>"
KO: "그만둬! 케류네이아!!<end>"

## 1180052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "と、父さん…。<end>"
EN: "F-Father...<end>"
KO: "아, 아버지….<end>"

## 1180053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "兄妹ゲンカなんてみっともない。<end>"
EN: "A quarrel between siblings is a shameful sight.<end>"
KO: "남매 싸움이라니 보기 흉해.<end>"

## 1180054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア、あなたがあくまで<line-break>運命と戦うというのならば…<end>"
EN: "Nemea, if you insist on fighting your fate...<end>"
KO: "네메아, 네가 끝까지 운명과 싸우겠다면….<end>"

## 1180055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私も黙って見ているわけには<line-break>いきません。<end>"
EN: "Then I cannot simply stand by and watch.<end>"
KO: "저도 가만히 보고만 있을 수는 없습니다.<end>"

## 1180056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "と、父さん…！<end>"
EN: "F-Father...!<end>"
KO: "아, 아버지…!<end>"

## 1180057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…とうさ…ん…。<end>"
EN: "Fa...ther...<end>"
KO: "…아버…지….<end>"

## 1180058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "父よ、あなたの意志、<line-break>確かに受け取った。<end>"
EN: "Father, I have received your will.<end>"
KO: "아버지, 당신의 뜻을 확실히 이어받았습니다.<end>"

## 1180059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待って…。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>待ってよ、兄さん…。<end>"
EN: "Wait... Please wait, Brother...<end>"
KO: "기다려…. 기다려 줘, 오빠….<end>"

## 1180060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待ちなさい、ネメア！<end>"
EN: "Wait, Nemea!<end>"
KO: "기다려, 네메아!<end>"

## 1180061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたはバロル以下のケダモノよ！<line-break>おぞましい悪の化身よ！<end>"
EN: "You're a beast lower than Balor! A vile incarnation of evil!<end>"
KO: "당신은 발로르보다도 못한 짐승이야! 소름 끼치는 악의 화신이야!<end>"

## 1180062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたは私の心を踏みにじった。<line-break>どうして…、どうしてなの！！<end>"
EN: "You trampled all over my heart. Why? Why did you do it?!<end>"
KO: "당신은 내 마음을 짓밟았어. 왜… 왜 그런 거야!!<end>"

## 1180063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、ああ…。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>…とうさん…。<line-break>とうさん…。<end>"
EN: "A-ah... Father... Father...<end>"
KO: "아, 아아…. …아버지…. 아버지….<end>"

## 1180064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これが私のやり方だ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "This is my way, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "이게 내 방식이다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1180065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前が望むのならば、<line-break>私はいつでも相手になろう。<end>"
EN: "If you wish it, I will face you whenever you please.<end>"
KO: "네가 원한다면 나는 언제든 상대해 주겠다.<end>"

## 1180066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、今はまだその時ではない。<line-break>強くなるのだな、<value:$28>。<line-break>私を、しのぐほどに。<end>"
EN: "But the time has not yet come. Grow stronger, <value:$28>. Strong enough to surpass me.<end>"
KO: "하지만 아직 때가 아니다. 강해져라, <value:$28>. 나를 뛰어넘을 만큼.<end>"

## 1180067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "泣かないで、<line-break>ケリュネイア。<end>"
EN: "Don't cry, Ceryneia.<end>"
KO: "울지 마, 케류네이아.<end>"

## 1180068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私です。オルファウスです。<end>"
EN: "It's me. Orphaus.<end>"
KO: "접니다. 오르파우스입니다.<end>"

## 1180069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この子は、今朝、屋敷の近くで<line-break>死んでいた子なんですけど、<line-break>しばらく身体を借りることにしました。<end>"
EN: "This little one was lying dead near the estate this morning, so I decided to borrow the body for a while.<end>"
KO: "이 아이는 오늘 아침 저택 근처에서 죽어 있던 아이인데, 잠시 몸을 빌리기로 했습니다.<end>"

## 1180070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "父さん…！？<line-break>猫に意識を移したの？<end>"
EN: "Father...?! You transferred your consciousness into a cat?<end>"
KO: "아버지…!? 고양이에게 의식을 옮긴 거야?<end>"

## 1180071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私たちの世話、<line-break>お願いできますね？<line-break>ケリュネイア。<end>"
EN: "I can trust you to look after us, can't I, Ceryneia?<end>"
KO: "저희를 돌봐 주실 수 있죠? 케류네이아.<end>"

## 1180072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よかった、無事で…。<end>"
EN: "Thank goodness you're safe...<end>"
KO: "다행이야, 무사해서….<end>"

## 1180073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく休めましたか？<line-break>昨日は、すみませんね、<line-break>親子ゲンカに巻き込んでしまって。<end>"
EN: "Did you sleep well? I'm sorry for dragging you into our family quarrel yesterday.<end>"
KO: "잘 쉬었나요? 어제는 미안했어요, 부자 싸움에 휘말리게 해서.<end>"

## 1180074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ。<line-break>最後は、あなたもネメアに<line-break>にらまれていたみたいですからね。<line-break>これから大変ですよ。<line-break>今まで以上に、いろいろと。<line-break>世界はより大きく動くでしょうね。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. It looked as though Nemea had marked you out by the end, too. Things will be difficult from here on, more so than ever and in all sorts of ways. The world is about to undergo even greater upheaval.<end>"
KO: "후후후. 마지막에는 당신도 네메아에게 찍힌 것 같더군요. 앞으로 힘들 겁니다. 지금까지보다 더 여러 가지로요. 세계는 더욱 크게 움직이겠죠.<end>"

## 1180075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメアが何を考えているかは、<line-break>今ははっきりとはわかりません。<line-break>　<line-break>ただ、あの子が、自分の運命と<line-break>戦っていることは確かです。<end>"
EN: "I cannot yet say for certain what Nemea is thinking. But one thing is clear: my son is fighting against his own fate.<end>"
KO: "네메아가 무슨 생각을 하는지는 지금은 분명히 모르겠습니다. 다만 그 아이가 자신의 운명과 싸우고 있다는 건 확실합니다.<end>"

## 1180076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "時が来れば、<line-break>その運命がどのような物なのかも、<line-break>わかるでしょう。<line-break>その時まで、<line-break>あなたはあなたの運命と<line-break>戦っていてください。<end>"
EN: "When the time comes, you will learn what that fate entails. Until then, keep fighting your own.<end>"
KO: "때가 오면 그 운명이 어떤 것인지도 알게 되겠지요. 그때까지 당신은 당신의 운명과 싸워 주세요.<end>"

## 1180077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつか、あなたとネメアの運命が、<line-break>重なり合うときが来るまで。<end>"
EN: "Until the day your fate and Nemea's converge.<end>"
KO: "언젠가 당신과 네메아의 운명이 겹치는 날이 올 때까지.<end>"

## 1180078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec118-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わからないことがあったら、<line-break>来てくださいね。<line-break>猫でも、お話くらいはできますから。<end>"
EN: "If there's anything you don't understand, please come and see me. Even a cat can at least talk with you.<end>"
KO: "모르는 게 있으면 찾아오세요. 고양이라도 이야기 정도는 할 수 있으니까요.<end>"

KO: "케류네이아 V5 예비 메시지 1입니다.<end>"

## 1190001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、<value:$28>。<line-break>ちょっと来てくれないか。　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. Could you come over here a moment?<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>. 잠깐 와 줄래?<end>"

## 1190002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "実はな、お前に名指しで　　　　　　<line-break>依頼が来ているそうだ。<line-break>　<line-break>依頼主はハーフエルフらしい。　　　<line-break>エンシャントの冒険者ギルドで<line-break>詳しい内容が聞けるぜ。<end>"
EN: "Apparently, a request has come in asking for you by name. The client seems to be a Half-Elf. You can hear the details at the Ancient Adventurers' Guild.<end>"
KO: "사실 말이야, 너를 지목한 의뢰가 들어왔대. 의뢰인은 하프엘프라는군. 엔샨트 모험자 길드에서 자세한 내용을 들을 수 있어.<end>"

## 1190003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "指名されるなんて大したもんだぜ。　<line-break>この依頼、逃すなよ。<line-break>名を売る絶好の機会だからな。<end>"
EN: "Not bad, getting asked for by name. Don't let this one get away. It's the perfect chance to make a name for yourself.<end>"
KO: "지명 의뢰라니 대단한데. 이 의뢰 놓치지 마. 이름을 알릴 절호의 기회니까.<end>"

## 1190004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、<value:$28>。<line-break>ちょっと来てくれないか。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. Could you come over here a moment?<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>. 잠깐 와 줄래?<end>"

## 1190005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "実はな、お前に名指しで依頼が来てる。<line-break>依頼主は、ケリュネイアというハーフエルフで、<line-break>闇の神器を手に入れてほしいって内容だ。<line-break>こいつは全部で１２あるらしいんだが、<line-break>在処がわかってるのは、３点。<end>"
EN: "You've been asked for by name. The client is a Half-Elf called Ceryneia, and she wants you to obtain the Dark Artifacts. There are apparently twelve in all, but the whereabouts of only three are known.<end>"
KO: "실은 너를 지명한 의뢰가 들어왔어. 의뢰인은 케류네이아라는 하프엘프고, 어둠의 신기를 손에 넣어 달라는 내용이야. 전부 열두 개라는데 위치가 알려진 건 세 개뿐이야.<end>"

## 1190006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひとつはロセン北の罪深き者の迷宮に　　　　　<line-break>あるらしいんだが、いつでも見つかる<line-break>わけじゃないそうだ。<line-break>もうひとつはウルカーンにあって、<line-break>最後のひとつは考古学者の<line-break>エスト・リューガが持っているらしい。<end>"
EN: "One is said to be in the Labyrinth of Sinners north of Rocen, though apparently it isn't always possible to find it. Another is in Ulkarne, and the last is said to be in the possession of the archaeologist Est Ryuga.<end>"
KO: "하나는 로센 북쪽 죄인의 미궁에 있다는데 언제나 발견되는 건 아니라고 해. 또 하나는 울칸에 있고, 마지막 하나는 고고학자 에스트 류가가 가지고 있다는군.<end>"

## 1190007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺もこれ以上詳しい内容は知らないんだ。　　　<line-break>もっと詳しく知りたかったら、<line-break>ケリュネイアに直接会ってくれ。<line-break>この町の港の向こうに<line-break>ライラネート神殿があるだろ？<line-break>そこで待ってるって言ってたよ。<end>"
EN: "That's all I know. If you want the details, you'll have to speak to Ceryneia yourself. You know the Temple of Lailanate, across the harbor? She said she'd be waiting there.<end>"
KO: "나도 이 이상 자세한 내용은 몰라. 더 자세히 알고 싶으면 케류네이아를 직접 만나 봐. 이 마을 항구 건너편에 라이라네이트 신전이 있지? 거기서 기다린다고 했어.<end>"

## 1190008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、そんな顔しなさんな。<line-break>とんでもない依頼内容だってことは　<line-break>わかるがさ。<end>"
EN: "Come on, don't look like that. I know it's an outrageous request.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그런 표정 짓지 마. 터무니없는 의뢰라는 건 나도 알지만.<end>"

## 1190009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ともかく、闇の神器を見つけたら、　<line-break>持ってきてくれ。<line-break>　<line-break>期限は無期限。<line-break>今言った３つ以外でもいいそうだから<line-break>がんばってくれよ。<end>"
EN: "Anyway, if you find a Dark Artifact, bring it in. There's no deadline, and it doesn't have to be one of the three I mentioned. Good luck.<end>"
KO: "어쨌든 어둠의 신기를 찾으면 가져와 줘. 기한은 무기한. 방금 말한 세 개가 아니어도 된다니까 힘내 줘.<end>"

## 1190010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、なんだか怪しい感じだからな。　<line-break>無理はしないこった。<line-break>気をつけろよ。<end>"
EN: "Well, the whole thing sounds a bit suspicious. Don't overdo it. Watch yourself.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 어쩐지 수상한 느낌이니까. 무리하지 마. 조심해.<end>"

## 1190011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたが<value:$28>ね。<end>"
EN: "So you're <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "당신이 <value:$28>군요.<end>"

## 1190012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はじめまして。<line-break>私が、闇の神器探索の依頼主、<line-break>ケリュネイアよ。<line-break>どう、神器は手に入れた？<end>"
EN: "A pleasure to meet you. I'm Ceryneia, the one who hired you to search for the Dark Artifacts. Well? Did you find one?<end>"
KO: "처음 뵙겠어요. 제가 어둠의 신기 탐색 의뢰인 케류네이아예요. 어때요, 신기는 손에 넣었나요?<end>"

## 1190013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "手に入れた<end>"
EN: "I have it.<end>"
KO: "손에 넣었다<end>"

## 1190014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだこれから<end>"
EN: "Not yet.<end>"
KO: "아직 이제부터야<end>"

## 1190015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう。ならば、もう気付いているわね。<line-break>ネメアも闇の神器を<line-break>探しているということに。<end>"
EN: "Good. Then you've already realized that Nemea is searching for the Dark Artifacts as well.<end>"
KO: "그래. 그렇다면 이제 눈치챘겠지. 네메아도 어둠의 신기를 찾고 있다는 걸.<end>"

## 1190016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だったら、急いだ方がいいわ。<line-break>闇の神器は<line-break>ネメアも狙っているのよ。<end>"
EN: "Then you'd better hurry. Nemea is after the Artifacts of Darkness too.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 서두르는 게 좋아. 어둠의 신기는 네메아도 노리고 있어.<end>"

## 1190017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の神器が１２個すべて集まれば、<line-break>破壊神ウルグを<line-break>呼び出せると言われているわ。<line-break>王位を簒奪して、<line-break>世界征服をたくらむ男に、<line-break>渡していいような代物じゃないのよ。<end>"
EN: "It is said that if all twelve Dark Artifacts are gathered, Ulugh, the God of Destruction, can be summoned. They cannot be allowed to fall into the hands of a man plotting to usurp the throne and conquer the world.<end>"
KO: "어둠의 신기를 12개 모두 모으면 파괴신 울그를 불러낼 수 있다고 해. 왕위를 찬탈하고 세계 정복을 꾀하는 남자에게 넘겨도 될 물건이 아니야.<end>"

## 1190018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪用するとは限らない<end>"
EN: "That doesn't mean he'll misuse them.<end>"
KO: "악용한다고 단정할 순 없어<end>"

## 1190020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "甘いわ、<value:$28>。<line-break>英雄はいつまでも<line-break>英雄でいるとは限らないのよ。<line-break>ネメアに倒された魔王バロルも<line-break>もとはディンガルを統一した<line-break>英雄だったのよ。<end>"
EN: "You're naive, <value:$28>. A hero does not always remain a hero. Balor, the Demon King defeated by Nemea, was once the hero who united Dyneskal.<end>"
KO: "무르네, <value:$28>. 영웅이 언제까지나 영웅인 건 아니야. 네메아에게 쓰러진 마왕 발로르도 원래는 딩갈을 통일한 영웅이었어.<end>"

## 1190021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメアは確かに英雄だったわ。　　　<line-break>ディンガルに現れた<line-break>魔王バロルを倒したのですもの。<line-break>でもね、ネメアに倒された魔王バロルも<line-break>もとはディンガルを統一した<line-break>英雄だったのよ。<end>"
EN: "Nemea was certainly a hero. He defeated Balor, the Demon King who appeared in Dyneskal. But Balor himself was once the hero who united Dyneskal.<end>"
KO: "네메아는 분명 영웅이었어. 딘갈에 나타난 마왕 발로르를 쓰러뜨렸으니까. 하지만 네메아에게 쓰러진 마왕 발로르도 원래는 딘갈을 통일한 영웅이었지.<end>"

## 1190022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにね…、<line-break>ネメアは人の子じゃないのよ。<end>"
EN: "And there is something else... Nemea is no child of man.<end>"
KO: "게다가 말이야… 네메아는 인간의 아이가 아니야.<end>"

## 1190023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼の父親は魔人、<line-break>破壊神の円卓騎士バルザーよ。<end>"
EN: "His father is a demon: Baltzer, a Knight of the Round Table serving the God of Destruction.<end>"
KO: "그의 아버지는 마인, 파괴신의 원탁기사 발자르야.<end>"

## 1190024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメアをこのまま<line-break>放っておくことは危険だわ。<end>"
EN: "It is too dangerous to leave Nemea unchecked.<end>"
KO: "네메아를 이대로 내버려 두는 건 위험해.<end>"

## 1190025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の神器を渡すわけにはいかないの。<line-break>覚悟を決めて、<line-break>ネメアと対決して。<end>"
EN: "You can't hand over the Dark Artifacts. Steel yourself and confront Nemea.<end>"
KO: "어둠의 신기를 넘겨줄 수는 없어. 각오를 다지고 네메아와 맞서.<end>"

## 1190026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぜあなたでなければ<line-break>ならないのか？<line-break>…いい質問ね。<end>"
EN: "Why does it have to be you? ...That's a good question.<end>"
KO: "왜 꼭 당신이어야 하는가? …좋은 질문이네.<end>"

## 1190027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の神器は強力な魔道器なの。　<line-break>その力は、持っているだけで、<line-break>その者を闇に染めてしまうわ。<line-break>闇の神器に取り込まれないだけの<line-break>強力なソウルの持ち主なんて、<line-break>この世に限られているのよ。<end>"
EN: "The Dark Artifacts are powerful arcane devices. Simply possessing one can taint its bearer with darkness. There are very few in this world whose Soul is strong enough to resist being consumed by one.<end>"
KO: "어둠의 신기는 강력한 마도기야. 그 힘은 지니고 있기만 해도 사람을 어둠에 물들여. 어둠의 신기에 먹히지 않을 만큼 강한 소울을 가진 사람은 이 세상에서도 한정돼 있어.<end>"

## 1190028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、あなたは<line-break>そのひとりなの。<end>"
EN: "And you are one of them.<end>"
KO: "그리고 당신도 그중 한 사람이야.<end>"

## 1190029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "好む好まざるに関わらず、<line-break>あなたの周りで運命は<line-break>動きはじめているのよ。<line-break>それだけは覚えておきなさい。<end>"
EN: "Whether you want it or not, fate has begun to move around you. Remember that.<end>"
KO: "좋든 싫든, 당신 주위에서 운명은 움직이기 시작했어. 그것만은 기억해 둬.<end>"

## 1190030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "束縛の腕輪を持ってきたのか。<line-break>よしよし、ケリュネイアから聞いている<line-break>形状と同じだな。<line-break>合格だ。こいつが報酬だ。<line-break>受け取りな。<end>"
EN: "So, you brought the Bracelet of Binding. Good, good. It matches the description Ceryneia gave me. That'll do. Here's your reward. Take it.<end>"
KO: "속박의 팔찌를 가져왔군. 좋아, 케류네이아에게 들은 모양과 같아. 합격이다. 이게 보수야. 받아.<end>"

## 1190031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec119-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1200001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "父さん、魔王バロルを倒したのは<line-break>なんのためだったの？<line-break>　<line-break>バロルの代わりにネメア兄さんを<line-break>魔王にするためだったの？<line-break>違うでしょう？<end>"
EN: "Father, what was the point of defeating Balor, the Demon King? Was it so Nemea could take his place as Demon King? That wasn't the reason, was it?<end>"
KO: "아버지, 마왕 발로르를 쓰러뜨린 건 무엇을 위해서였어? 발로르 대신 네메아 형을 마왕으로 만들기 위해서였어? 아니잖아?<end>"

## 1200002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですねえ。でも、あんまり<line-break>目的意識もなかったですかねえ。<end>"
EN: "Quite right. Though I can't say we had much of a purpose in mind at the time.<end>"
KO: "그렇네요. 하지만 딱히 목적의식도 없었던 것 같네요.<end>"

## 1200003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "父さん！<end>"
EN: "Father!<end>"
KO: "아버지!<end>"

## 1200004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにネメアも、別に魔王になろうと<line-break>しているわけでもないでしょう？<end>"
EN: "Besides, Nemea isn't actually trying to become the Demon King, is he?<end>"
KO: "게다가 네메아도 딱히 마왕이 되려는 건 아니잖아?<end>"

## 1200005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界に戦争を挑み、<line-break>闇の神器を集めているわ！　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>早く、くい止めないと<line-break>兄さんは魔王になる。そしたら、<line-break>もう誰も兄さんを止められないわ！<end>"
EN: "He's waging war on the world and gathering the Dark Artifacts! If we don't stop him soon, he'll become the Demon King. And then no one will be able to stop him!<end>"
KO: "세상에 전쟁을 걸고 어둠의 신기를 모으고 있어! 빨리 막지 않으면 오빠는 마왕이 돼. 그러면 아무도 오빠를 막을 수 없어!<end>"

## 1200006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すでに今でも、<line-break>誰もネメアをとめられませんよ。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>剣聖レーグですら、引き分けたんです。<line-break>ま、好きにさせるしかないでしょう。<end>"
EN: "No one can stop Nemea even now. Even Reig the Sword Saint could only fight him to a draw. Well, I suppose we'll just have to let him do as he pleases.<end>"
KO: "이미 지금도 아무도 네메아를 막을 수 없습니다. 검성 레그조차 무승부였으니까요. 뭐, 마음대로 하게 둘 수밖에 없겠죠.<end>"

## 1200007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それより、感心しませんねえ。<line-break>無限のソウルを持つ者を使って、<line-break>私に内緒で何かたくらんでいるなんて。<end>"
EN: "More to the point, I don't approve of you using the Bearer of the Infinite Soul to plot something behind my back.<end>"
KO: "그보다 감탄할 일은 아니군요. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자를 이용해서 제게 몰래 뭔가 꾸미고 있다니.<end>"

## 1200008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あれは…！<end>"
EN: "Th-that was...!<end>"
KO: "저, 저건…!<end>"

## 1200009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言いわけは別の機会に聞きます。<line-break>行きなさい、ケリュネイア。<end>"
EN: "You can make your excuses another time. Go, Ceryneia.<end>"
KO: "변명은 다음에 듣겠습니다. 가세요, 케류네이아.<end>"

## 1200010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすがは閃光の異名をとるだけある。<line-break>素早いですねえ。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>もう、入ってきてもいいですよ。<end>"
EN: "She certainly lives up to the name “Flash.” Very quick indeed. <value:$28>, you may come in now.<end>"
KO: "역시 섬광이라는 별명답게 빠르군요. <value:$28>, 이제 들어와도 됩니다.<end>"

## 1200011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ、おそらく、あなたには<line-break>質問したいことがあるのでしょう。<line-break>　<line-break>あの女性は何者なのか？<line-break>私とネメアとあの女性との関係は？<line-break>ま、順に話していきましょうか。<end>"
EN: "Heh. I imagine you have questions. Who was that woman? What is the connection between her, Nemea, and me? Well, let us take things in order.<end>"
KO: "후후, 아마 당신은 묻고 싶은 게 있겠지요. 그 여성은 누구인가? 나와 네메아, 그리고 그 여성의 관계는? 자, 차례대로 이야기해 보죠.<end>"

## 1200012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さっきの女性の名はケリュネイア。<line-break>あなたにネメアの野望を阻止するように<line-break>言っているみたいですね。<line-break>彼女はハーフエルフです。<line-break>ハーフエルフは人からもエルフからも<line-break>うとまれています。<end>"
EN: "The woman you just saw is Ceryneia. It seems she's been telling you to thwart Nemea's ambitions. She's a Half-Elf, and Half-Elves are shunned by humans and Elves alike.<end>"
KO: "아까 그 여성의 이름은 케류네이아. 당신에게 네메아의 야망을 막으라고 말하는 것 같군요. 그녀는 하프엘프입니다. 하프엘프는 인간에게도 엘프에게도 꺼림받고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1200013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それで、両親は持て余して<line-break>赤ん坊の彼女を捨てたのでしょう。<line-break>それを私が拾って育てたのです。<line-break>だから、彼女は私のことを<line-break>父と呼んでいたのです。<end>"
EN: "Her parents presumably found her too much to deal with and abandoned her as an infant. I found her and raised her. That is why she called me Father.<end>"
KO: "그래서 부모는 감당하지 못해 갓난아이였던 그녀를 버렸겠죠. 그걸 제가 주워 키웠습니다. 그래서 그녀는 저를 아버지라고 불렀던 겁니다.<end>"

## 1200014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私が育てた子はもうひとりいます。<line-break>それが今は血塗られた覇道を行く男<line-break>獅子帝ネメアなのです。<line-break>３０年程前、円卓騎士バルザーと<line-break>魔王バロルの娘エスリンの間に<line-break>恋が芽生え、生命が宿りました。<end>"
EN: "There is one other child I raised: Nemea, the Lion Emperor, now treading a bloodstained path to supremacy. Some thirty years ago, love blossomed between Baltzer, a Knight of the Round Table, and Ethlinn, daughter of Balor, the Demon King. A new life was conceived.<end>"
KO: "내가 키운 아이는 한 명 더 있습니다. 지금 피로 물든 패도를 걷는 남자, 사자제 네메아입니다. 약 30년 전 원탁기사 발자와 마왕 발로르의 딸 에스린 사이에 사랑이 싹트고 생명이 잉태되었습니다.<end>"

## 1200015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、バロルの娘の子がバロルを<line-break>滅ぼすという妖術宰相ゾフォルの<line-break>予言が、エスリンの命を奪いました。<line-break>予言をおそれた魔王バロルが、<line-break>自分の娘の首をかき切り<line-break>この森に捨てたのです。<end>"
EN: "But the Sorcerous Chancellor Zofor prophesied that the child of Balor's daughter would destroy him, and that prophecy cost Ethlinn her life. Fearing it, Balor, the Demon King, slit his own daughter's throat and cast her body into this forest.<end>"
KO: "그러나 발로르의 딸의 자식이 발로르를 멸망시킨다는 요술 재상 조포르의 예언이 에스린의 목숨을 앗아갔습니다. 예언을 두려워한 마왕 발로르가 자기 딸의 목을 베어 이 숲에 버린 것입니다.<end>"

## 1200016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私はエスリンの命を救うことは　　　　<line-break>できませんでしたが、彼女に宿っていた<line-break>新しい命は救うことができました。<line-break>それがネメアなのです。<line-break>やがて、成長したネメアは<line-break>魔王バロルを倒すべく旅に出ました。<end>"
EN: "I could not save Ethlinn, but I was able to save the new life within her. That child was Nemea. When he came of age, Nemea set out on a journey to defeat Balor, the Demon King.<end>"
KO: "나는 에슬린의 목숨은 구하지 못했지만, 그녀 안에 깃든 새로운 생명은 구할 수 있었습니다. 그 아이가 네메아입니다. 그리고 성장한 네메아는 마왕 발로르를 쓰러뜨리기 위해 여행을 떠났습니다.<end>"

## 1200017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その旅に付き従ったのが<line-break>先ほどのケリュネイアに剣聖レーグ、<line-break>そして私なのです。<end>"
EN: "Those who accompanied him were Ceryneia, whom you just met, Reig the Sword Saint, and myself.<end>"
KO: "그 여행을 함께한 것이 아까 말한 케류네이아와 검성 레그, 그리고 저입니다.<end>"

## 1200018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハーフエルフと半魔。この似た境遇も<line-break>手伝って、ケリュネイアは<line-break>ネメアを慕うようになりました。<line-break>ともに旅をし、ともに苦難を抜け、<line-break>ケリュネイアは妹と言うよりはむしろ、<line-break>恋人のようにネメアに接していました。<end>"
EN: "Ceryneia was a Half-Elf and Nemea a half-Demon. Their similar circumstances helped foster her affection for him. They traveled and endured hardship together, and Ceryneia behaved toward Nemea more like a lover than a younger sister.<end>"
KO: "하프엘프와 반마. 이 비슷한 처지도 한몫해 케류네이아는 네메아를 사모하게 됐습니다. 함께 여행하고 함께 고난을 넘으며, 케류네이아는 여동생이라기보다 오히려 연인처럼 네메아를 대했지요.<end>"

## 1200019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それだけに、彼女には<line-break>今現在のネメアが許せないのです。<line-break>　<line-break>自分の理想の男としてではなく、<line-break>血塗られた覇道を進むネメアの意図が<line-break>ケリュネイアには理解できないのです。<end>"
EN: "That is precisely why she cannot forgive the man Nemea is now. She cannot understand why the man she idealized would choose to walk such a bloodstained path to supremacy.<end>"
KO: "그렇기에 그녀는 지금의 네메아를 용서할 수 없는 겁니다. 자기 이상형으로서가 아니라 피로 물든 패도를 걷는 네메아의 의도를 케류네이아는 이해하지 못하는 거죠.<end>"

## 1200020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自分の願い、自分の思い描く姿から、<line-break>ネメアが逸脱していくのが<line-break>許せないのです。<line-break>人はおのれのうちに抱く<line-break>幻想を打ち破るものを<line-break>たやすく憎むものです。<end>"
EN: "She cannot forgive Nemea for straying from her wishes, from the image she formed of him. People readily come to hate anyone who shatters the illusions they hold within themselves.<end>"
KO: "자신의 소원과 마음속 모습에서 네메아가 벗어나는 걸 용납할 수 없는 겁니다. 사람은 자기 안의 환상을 깨뜨리는 존재를 쉽게 미워하니까요.<end>"

## 1200021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ケリュネイアはネメアの真の意図を<line-break>確かめようともしません。<line-break>ネメアと直接対決するのが恐いのです。<line-break>そうすることによって自分の幻想が<line-break>壊れてしまうのが恐いから…。<line-break>つまり、信じ切れていないんですね。<end>"
EN: "Ceryneia will not even try to discover Nemea's true intentions. She's afraid to confront him directly, because doing so might destroy her illusions. In other words, her faith in him is not absolute.<end>"
KO: "케류네이아는 네메아의 진짜 뜻을 확인하려 하지도 않습니다. 네메아와 직접 마주하는 게 두려운 거예요. 그렇게 해서 자기 환상이 무너질까 두려우니까…. 즉, 완전히 믿고 있는 건 아니죠.<end>"

## 1200022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ですから、ネメアをただ憎み、<line-break>あなたを使って、自分の幻想の中の<line-break>ネメアを取り戻そうとしているのです。<end>"
EN: "And so she simply hates Nemea and uses you in an attempt to reclaim the Nemea of her imagination.<end>"
KO: "그래서 네메아를 그저 미워하며 당신을 이용해 자기 환상 속의 네메아를 되찾으려는 겁니다.<end>"

## 1200023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さてさて、長い話になりました。<line-break>おさらいの質問を受け付けますよ。<line-break>何か聞きたいことはありますか？<end>"
EN: "Well, that turned into quite a long story. Now, is there anything you'd like me to go over again? Any questions?<end>"
KO: "자, 이야기가 길어졌군요. 복습 질문을 받겠습니다. 궁금한 게 있습니까?<end>"

## 1200024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ケリュネイアについて<end>"
EN: "About Ceryneia<end>"
KO: "케류네이아에 대해서<end>"

## 1200025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメアについて<end>"
EN: "About Nemea<end>"
KO: "네메아에 대하여<end>"

## 1200026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメアの意図について<end>"
EN: "Nemea's intentions<end>"
KO: "네메아의 의도에 대해서<end>"

## 1200027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう聞くことはない<end>"
EN: "I have no more questions<end>"
KO: "더 들을 건 없다<end>"

## 1200028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたにネメアの野望を阻むように　　<line-break>言っている女性です。<line-break>　<line-break>彼女はハーフエルフです。<line-break>ハーフエルフは人からもエルフからも<line-break>うとまれます。<end>"
EN: "She's the woman telling you to thwart Nemea's ambitions. She's a Half-Elf, and Half-Elves are shunned by humans and Elves alike.<end>"
KO: "당신에게 네메아의 야망을 막으라고 말한 여자입니다. 그녀는 하프엘프예요. 하프엘프는 인간에게도 엘프에게도 배척받습니다.<end>"

## 1200029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それで、彼女の両親は<line-break>赤ん坊の彼女を捨てたのでしょう。<line-break>それを私が拾って育てたのです。<line-break>ハーフエルフであるケリュネイアは<line-break>似た境遇のネメアに親近感を持ち、<line-break>彼になかば恋愛感情を抱いています。<end>"
EN: "Her parents presumably abandoned her as an infant. I found her and raised her. As a Half-Elf, Ceryneia felt a kinship with Nemea because of their similar circumstances, and her feelings for him are partly romantic.<end>"
KO: "그래서 그녀의 부모는 아기였던 그녀를 버렸겠지요. 그 아이를 제가 주워 길렀습니다. 하프엘프인 케류네이아는 비슷한 처지의 네메아에게 친근감을 느꼈고, 그에게 어느 정도 연애 감정까지 품고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1200030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それだけに自分の理想像から離れていく<line-break>ネメアが許せないのかもしれません。<line-break>　<line-break>素早い動きで魔法を連発する<line-break>優れた魔道士で、閃光の異名をとり、<line-break>バロル討伐ではネメアを助けました。<end>"
EN: "That may be why she cannot forgive Nemea for straying from her ideal of him. She is an exceptional mage known as the Flash, moving swiftly as she casts spell after spell, and she helped Nemea defeat Balor.<end>"
KO: "그렇기에 자기 이상상에서 멀어져 가는 네메아를 용서할 수 없는지도 모릅니다. 빠른 움직임으로 마법을 연속 사용하는 뛰어난 마도사로 '섬광'이라는 이명을 얻었고, 발로르 토벌 때 네메아를 도왔습니다.<end>"

## 1200031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "他に、聞きたいことはありますか？　　<end>"
EN: "Is there anything else you would like to ask?<end>"
KO: "그 밖에 묻고 싶은 게 있습니까?<end>"

## 1200032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バロルの娘エスリンと　　　　　　　　<line-break>破壊神ウルグの円卓騎士<line-break>魔人バルザーの間の子です。<line-break>ゾフォルの予言を恐れた魔王バロルに、<line-break>彼の母、エスリンは命を奪われましたが<line-break>私が死んだ母胎から彼を助けました。<end>"
EN: "He is the son of Ethlinn, Balor's daughter, and Baltzer the Demon, a Knight of the Round Table who served Ulugh, God of Destruction. Fearing Zofor's prophecy, Balor, the Demon King, took the life of Nemea's mother, Ethlinn, but I rescued him from her dead womb.<end>"
KO: "발로르의 딸 에슬린과 파괴신 울그의 원탁기사, 마인 발자르 사이에서 태어난 아이입니다. 조포르의 예언을 두려워한 마왕 발로르에게 어머니 에슬린은 목숨을 잃었지만, 저는 죽은 어머니의 몸에서 그를 구해 냈습니다.<end>"

## 1200033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その後、私は彼をケリュネイアと一緒に<line-break>育てました。いわば、養子ですね。<line-break>やがて立派な戦士に成長しました。<line-break>彼はバロルを倒し、世界を救いました。<line-break>そのときの仲間が閃光のケリュネイア<line-break>剣聖レーグ、そして私です。<end>"
EN: "Afterward, I raised him alongside Ceryneia. You might say he was my adopted son. In time, he grew into a fine warrior. He defeated Balor and saved the world. His companions then were Ceryneia the Flash, Reig the Sword Saint, and myself.<end>"
KO: "그 뒤 나는 그를 케류네이아와 함께 키웠습니다. 말하자면 양자였죠. 이윽고 훌륭한 전사로 성장했습니다. 그는 발로르를 쓰러뜨리고 세계를 구했습니다. 당시 동료가 섬광의 케류네이아, 검성 레그, 그리고 나였습니다.<end>"

## 1200034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "他に、聞きたいことはありますか？　　<end>"
EN: "Is there anything else you would like to ask?<end>"
KO: "그 밖에 묻고 싶은 게 있습니까?<end>"

## 1200035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメアに、直接<line-break>挑み、対決することです。<line-break>そこから確かめてください。<line-break>私は教えられません。<line-break>ただ、ケリュネイアのように<line-break>臆病にならないでください。<end>"
EN: "Challenge Nemea and confront him directly. Find your answer there. I cannot give it to you. Just do not be a coward like Ceryneia.<end>"
KO: "네메아에게 직접 도전해 맞서는 겁니다. 거기서 확인하세요. 저는 알려 드릴 수 없습니다. 다만 케류네이아처럼 겁먹지는 마세요.<end>"

## 1200036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "他に、聞きたいことはありますか？　<end>"
EN: "Is there anything else you would like to ask?<end>"
KO: "그 밖에 묻고 싶은 게 있습니까?<end>"

## 1200037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。では最後にもうひとつ。<line-break>ネメアのやり方は、間違っていない<line-break>わけではありません。<line-break>ですが、あなたが彼の行動に<line-break>疑問を感じているのなら、<line-break>彼に挑んでください。いいですね？<end>"
EN: "Very well. Then one last thing. I cannot say Nemea's methods are without fault. But if his actions trouble you, challenge him. Do you understand?<end>"
KO: "그렇군요. 그럼 마지막으로 하나만 더. 네메아의 방식이 완전히 틀렸다고 할 수는 없습니다. 하지만 당신이 그의 행동에 의문을 느낀다면, 그에게 도전하세요. 알겠죠?<end>"

## 1200038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今はまだ、わからないかもしれません。<line-break>しかし、挑み続けているうちに、<line-break>いずれ、ぼんやりとわかってきますよ。<end>"
EN: "You may not understand yet. But if you continue to challenge him, in time you will begin to see, however dimly.<end>"
KO: "지금은 아직 모를지도 모릅니다. 하지만 계속 도전하다 보면 언젠가는 어렴풋이 알게 될 겁니다.<end>"

## 1200039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日もいい天気ですね。<line-break>たまには庭で昼寝でもどうです？<line-break>…ムリは、よくありませんよ。<end>"
EN: "Another beautiful day, isn't it? Why not take a nap in the garden for a change? You really shouldn't push yourself too hard.<end>"
KO: "오늘도 날씨가 좋네요. 가끔은 정원에서 낮잠이라도 어떠세요? …무리는 좋지 않아요.<end>"

## 1200040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>また背が伸びたようですね。<end>"
EN: "It looks as though you've grown taller again, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 또 키가 큰 것 같네요.<end>"

## 1200041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私が縮んだから？<line-break>…なるほど、そうかもしれません。<end>"
EN: "Because I've shrunk? Ah, perhaps you're right.<end>"
KO: "제가 작아져서요? …그렇군요, 그럴지도 모르겠네요.<end>"

## 1200042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ケリュネイアは<line-break>よくやってくれてますよ。<line-break>わがままな猫２匹の世話をね。<line-break>ああ見えても<line-break>やさしい子なんです。<end>"
EN: "Ceryneia has been doing a fine job, looking after those two willful cats. She may not look it, but she's a kind girl.<end>"
KO: "케류네이아는 잘해 주고 있어요. 제멋대로인 고양이 두 마리를 돌보느라 말이죠. 저래 보여도 상냥한 아이예요.<end>"

## 1200043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、昼寝でもしましょうかね。<end>"
EN: "Well then, perhaps it's time for a nap.<end>"
KO: "자, 낮잠이나 자 볼까.<end>"

## 1200044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様。<line-break>ご無事でなによりです。<line-break>ゆっくりしていってくださいね。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, what a relief to see you safe. Please stay and rest awhile.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님. 무사하셔서 다행입니다. 편히 쉬다 가세요.<end>"

## 1200045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここにいると　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>心が安まるんです。<line-break> 　<line-break>つらかったことや<line-break>悲しかったことを<line-break>みんな忘れられるような気がして…。<end>"
EN: "I feel at peace here. It's as though I can forget all the painful, sorrowful things that have happened...<end>"
KO: "여기 있으면 마음이 편안해져요. 힘들었던 일도 슬펐던 일도 전부 잊을 수 있을 것 같아서….<end>"

## 1200046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネモさんってとても<line-break>やさしい方なんですよ。<end>"
EN: "Nemo is such a kind man.<end>"
KO: "네모 씨는 정말 상냥한 분이에요.<end>"

## 1200047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当です。<line-break>親切にいろいろと<line-break>教えてくださるんです。<end>"
EN: "It's true. He's been kind enough to teach me all sorts of things.<end>"
KO: "정말이에요. 친절하게 여러 가지를 가르쳐 주세요.<end>"

## 1200048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec120-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あまりムリを<line-break>なさらないでくださいね。<end>"
EN: "Please don't push yourself too hard.<end>"
KO: "너무 무리하지 마세요.<end>"

JP: "ケリュネイアＶ３<line-break>予備メッセージ１です<end>"
