## 1210000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（たしかアキュリュースの傭兵として<line-break>　登録したと思うけど…）<line-break><line-break>（アキュリュースの神殿には<line-break>　行ったかな？）<line-break><end>"
EN: "(I think I registered as a mercenary for Aqyurius...) (Have I visited the temple in Aqyurius yet?)<end>"
KO: "(분명 아큐류스의 용병으로 등록했던 것 같은데….) (아큐류스 신전에는 갔었나?)<end>"

## 1210001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自分が申し込んだくせに忘れちゃう<line-break>なんて、しっかりしなさいよ！<line-break>アキュリュースよ、思い出した？<end>"
EN: "You signed up yourself, and now you've forgotten? Get it together! It's Aqyurius. Remember now?<end>"
KO: "자기가 신청해 놓고 잊어버리다니 정신 차려! 아큐류스야, 기억났어?<end>"

## 1210002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "傭兵といえども金をもらう以上は<line-break>その国のために精一杯働くものよ。<line-break>お主はアキュリュースの傭兵じゃぞ。<end>"
EN: "Mercenary or not, once you accept payment, you must do your utmost for that country. You are a mercenary for Aqyurius.<end>"
KO: "용병이라 해도 돈을 받는 이상 그 나라를 위해 최선을 다하는 법이다. 너는 아큐류스의 용병이야.<end>"

## 1210003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっと…ディンガルです。<line-break>いや…ロストールだったかも。<line-break>そうだ！　アキュリュースです。<end>"
EN: "Um... Dyneskal. No... maybe it was Rostorl. That's it! Aqyurius.<end>"
KO: "음… 딩갈입니다. 아니… 로스톨이었나. 맞다! 아큐류스입니다.<end>"

## 1210004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、アキュリュース傭兵だぜ。<line-break>相手がディンガルなもんで、<line-break>びびっちまったか？<end>"
EN: "Yeah, you're an Aqyurius mercenary. Did you lose your nerve because you're up against Dyneskal?<end>"
KO: "그래, 아큐류스 용병이다. 상대가 딩갈이라 겁먹었냐?<end>"

## 1210005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん。アキュリュースの傭兵に<line-break>申し込んだと思うけど。<line-break>相手はディンガル帝国だよ。<end>"
EN: "Yes. I think you signed up as a mercenary for Aqyurius. Your enemy is the Dyneskal Empire.<end>"
KO: "응. 아큐류스의 용병에 지원한 것 같은데. 상대는 딩갈 제국이야.<end>"

## 1210006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたはアキュリュースの傭兵として<line-break>登録したはずよ。<line-break>忘れるなんて、情けないわね！<end>"
EN: "I'm sure you registered as a mercenary for Aqyurius. How pathetic, forgetting something like that!<end>"
KO: "당신은 아큐류스의 용병으로 등록했을 텐데. 그걸 잊다니 한심하네!<end>"

## 1210007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたが傭兵登録した国は<line-break>アキュリュースですよ。<line-break>思い出しましたか？<end>"
EN: "The country where you registered as a mercenary is Aqyurius. Do you remember now?<end>"
KO: "당신이 용병 등록한 나라는 아큐류스예요. 기억났나요?<end>"

## 1210008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっとちょっと、ぼけるには<line-break>早いんじゃな～い。<line-break>アキュリュースよ、アキュリュース。<end>"
EN: "Hey, hey, aren't you a little young to be going senile? Aqyurius. It's Aqyurius.<end>"
KO: "잠깐 잠깐, 벌써 깜빡하기엔 너무 이른 거 아냐~? 아큐류스야, 아큐류스.<end>"

## 1210009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだよ、姉ちゃん。<line-break>相手はディンガル帝国だから<line-break>負けないように強くならないとな。<end>"
EN: "That's right, sis. We're up against the Dyneskal Empire, so we'll have to get stronger if we don't want to lose.<end>"
KO: "그래, 누나. 상대는 딩갈 제국이니까 지지 않게 강해져야지.<end>"

## 1210010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたが傭兵登録したのは<line-break>アキュリュースだったと思いますよ。<end>"
EN: "I believe you registered as a mercenary for Aqyurius.<end>"
KO: "당신이 용병 등록을 한 곳은 아큐류스였던 것 같습니다.<end>"

## 1210011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その通り。<line-break>お前さんはアキュリュース傭兵として<line-break>ディンガルと戦うわけだ。<end>"
EN: "That's right. You're going to fight Dyneskal as an Aqyurius mercenary.<end>"
KO: "맞아. 자네는 아큐류스 용병으로서 딘갈과 싸우는 거다.<end>"

## 1210012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだ、アキュリュースだ。<line-break>戦いの準備はできているのか？<end>"
EN: "Right, Aqyurius. Are you ready for battle?<end>"
KO: "그래, 아큐류스다. 전투 준비는 되어 있나?<end>"

## 1210013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうじゃ、アキュリュースじゃ。<line-break>相手はディンガル帝国じゃから強いぞ。<line-break>気合いをいれてのぞむのじゃぞ。<end>"
EN: "That's right, Aqyurius. The Dyneskal Empire is a powerful enemy, so steel yourself for the fight.<end>"
KO: "그래, 아큐류스다. 상대는 딘갈 제국이라 강하다. 단단히 각오하고 임해라.<end>"

## 1210014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん、アキュリュースだと思うよ。<line-break>相手はディンガル帝国だから、<line-break>強い兵隊ばかりなんだろうね。<end>"
EN: "Yes, I think it's Aqyurius. You're up against the Dyneskal Empire, so I expect all its soldiers are strong.<end>"
KO: "응, 아큐류스라고 생각해. 상대는 딘갈 제국이니까 강한 병사들뿐이겠지.<end>"

## 1210015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふふ、君は私をからかっているのか？<line-break>君の雇い主はアキュリュースだろ。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. Are you teasing me? Your employer is Aqyurius, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "후후후, 자네는 나를 놀리는 건가? 자네 고용주는 아큐류스잖아.<end>"

## 1210016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "高貴で気品あふれるエルフのアタクシが<line-break>どうしてあんたの雇い主を覚えなくちゃ<line-break>いけないのよ。失礼するわねぇ。<end>"
EN: "Why should a noble, refined Elf like me have to remember who employs you? How insulting.<end>"
KO: "고귀하고 기품 넘치는 엘프인 내가 왜 네 고용주까지 기억해야 하는데. 실례할게.<end>"

## 1210017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よー、平和ですな傭兵さん…<line-break>拙者にはダジャレの才能がないので<line-break>あろうか…否！　くじけませんぞ！<end>"
EN: "Yo! Peaceful times, eh, mercenary...? Could it be that I have no talent for puns...? No! I shall not be discouraged!<end>"
KO: "안녕하십니까, 평화롭군요 용병님… 소인에게는 말장난 재능이 없는 걸까요… 아니! 꺾이지 않겠습니다!<end>"

## 1210018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだよ、アキュリュースだよぉ。<line-break>ちゃんとメモして、忘れないように<line-break>しなくちゃダメだよぉ。<end>"
EN: "That's right, it's Aqyurius. You need to write it down so you don't forget again.<end>"
KO: "맞아, 아큐류스야. 제대로 메모해서 잊지 않게 해야지.<end>"

## 1210019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break><line-break><line-break>そうだ、アキュリュースだ。<end>"
EN: "............... Right, Aqyurius.<end>"
KO: "………………. 그래, 아큐류스다.<end>"

## 1210020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自分の仕える国を忘れるとは…<line-break>しょせん騎士と傭兵は別ということか。<line-break>お前の所属はアキュリュースだぞ。<end>"
EN: "To forget the country you serve... I suppose knights and mercenaries are different after all. You belong to Aqyurius.<end>"
KO: "자기가 섬기는 나라를 잊다니…. 결국 기사와 용병은 다르다는 건가. 네 소속은 아큐류스다.<end>"

## 1210021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだよ、アキュリュースの傭兵だよ。<line-break>ディンガルの兵隊がいつ攻めてくるか<line-break>わからないから注意しないとね。<line-break>時々、アキュリュースに行って<line-break>戦況を聞いておいたほうが<line-break>いいと思うよ。<end>"
EN: "That's right, you're a mercenary for Aqyurius. Dyneskal's soldiers could attack at any time, so be careful. You should visit Aqyurius now and then to ask how the war is going.<end>"
KO: "그래, 아큐류스의 용병이야. 딘갈 병사들이 언제 공격해 올지 모르니 조심해야 해. 가끔 아큐류스에 가서 전황을 들어 두는 게 좋을 것 같아.<end>"

## 1210022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "天然の要害アキュリュースも、<line-break>傭兵がこの調子なら、確実に落ちる。<line-break>我らディンガルの勝利だな！<end>"
EN: "Even Aqyurius, protected by natural defenses, is certain to fall if this is the caliber of its mercenaries. Victory belongs to Dyneskal!<end>"
KO: "천연 요새 아큐류스도 용병들이 이 모양이면 확실히 함락되겠군. 우리 딘갈의 승리다!<end>"

## 1210023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アキュリュースです。<line-break>相手はディンガル帝国、<line-break>一時たりとも油断なりません。<end>"
EN: "Aqyurius. Your opponent is the Dyneskal Empire, so you cannot let your guard down for even a moment.<end>"
KO: "아큐류스입니다. 상대는 딩갈 제국, 한순간도 방심할 수 없습니다.<end>"

## 1210024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん、アキュリュースだよ！<line-break>もっと自信を持って！<end>"
EN: "Yes, Aqyurius! Have a little more confidence!<end>"
KO: "응, 아큐류스야! 좀 더 자신감을 가져!<end>"

## 1210025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アキュリュースじゃ。<line-break>相手はディンガル軍、生半可な心構えでは<line-break>生きて帰ってこれぬかもしれんぞ。<line-break>…なんて脅かしてみるがの、<line-break>おぬしならば大丈夫だと思っておるよ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Aqyurius. You face the Dyneskal Army, and if you go in halfheartedly, you may not make it home alive. ...Or so I say to scare you. But I think you'll be all right, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아큐류스다. 상대는 딘갈군. 어중간한 각오로는 살아 돌아오지 못할지도 몰라. …라고 겁을 줘 보지만, 너라면 괜찮다고 생각한다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1210026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう、アキュリュースだぞ。<line-break>ディンガルが攻めて来る前に、<line-break>神殿へは行っておこう。<end>"
EN: "Yes, Aqyurius. Visit the temple before Dyneskal attacks.<end>"
KO: "그래, 아큐류스야. 딩갈이 공격해 오기 전에 신전에는 가 두자.<end>"

## 1210027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "傭兵登録したのは<line-break>アキュリュースだ。 <line-break>　 <line-break>まあ、あそこはミズチの護りが固いから <line-break>滅多に戦闘にはならないだろうがな。<end>"
EN: "You registered as a mercenary for Aqyurius. Still, Mizuchi's protection is strong, so there probably won't be much fighting there.<end>"
KO: "용병 등록을 한 곳은 아큐류스다. 뭐, 거긴 미즈치의 수비가 단단해서 좀처럼 전투는 없겠지만.<end>"

## 1210028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アキュリュースに傭兵登録したでしょ。<line-break>引き受けたからには、<line-break>しっかり働くわよ。<end>"
EN: "You registered as a mercenary for Aqyurius, remember? Since we accepted the job, we'd better work hard.<end>"
KO: "아큐류스에 용병 등록했잖아. 맡은 이상 제대로 일할 거야.<end>"

## 1210030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アキュリュースですよ。<line-break>私、物覚えはいいほうなんです。<line-break>忘れたらいつでも聞いてくださいね。<end>"
EN: "It's Aqyurius. I have a good memory, so ask me anytime you forget.<end>"
KO: "아큐류스예요. 저, 기억력은 좋은 편이거든요. 잊으면 언제든 물어보세요.<end>"

## 1210032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（アトレイア王女に光を与えるために<line-break>　必要な魔法の宝石。東方の博士を<line-break>　名乗るシャリの依頼で探すことに）<line-break>（シャリの話ではロストール北西にある<line-break>　邪眼の迷宮にあるらしい）<end>"
EN: "(A magical jewel needed to restore Princess Atleia's sight. I agreed to search for it at the request of Shalli, who claims to be a scholar from the East.) (According to Shalli, it is in the Evil Eye Labyrinth northwest of Rostorl.)<end>"
KO: "(아트레이아 왕녀에게 빛을 되찾아 주기 위해 필요한 마법의 보석. 동방의 박사라고 자칭하는 샤리의 의뢰로 찾기로 했다.) (샤리의 말에 따르면 로스톨 북서쪽 사안의 미궁에 있는 모양이다.)<end>"

## 1210033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec121-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイア王女の目を見えるように<line-break>するために必要な魔法の宝石ね。<line-break>ロストール北西の邪眼の迷宮にある…。<line-break>依頼してきたシャリは怪しげだけど<line-break>王女様のためにもがんばりましょう。<end>"
EN: "It's the magical jewel needed to restore Princess Atleia's sight. It's in the Evil Eye Labyrinth northwest of Rostorl... Shalli seems suspicious, but let's do our best for the princess.<end>"
KO: "아트레이아 왕녀의 눈을 보이게 하는 데 필요한 마법의 보석이네. 로스톨 북서쪽 사안의 미궁에 있어…. 의뢰한 샤리는 수상하지만 왕녀님을 위해서라도 힘내자.<end>"
